Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI are producing increasingly complex autonomous systems that integrate large language models, world models, optimization engines, specialized neural architectures, autonomous platforms, and human operators. While much current research focuses on improving reasoning capabilities, safety-critical real-time deployment also requires bounded and verifiable coordination among heterogeneous components operating concurrently under uncertainty. Software-mediated coordination presents fundamental limitations in domains where bounded latency, deterministic coordination, and enforceable safety guarantees are essential.
  Hence, we propose a hardware-enforced semantic coordination architecture in which selected coordination semantics are implemented directly at the hardware level via field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). The approach builds on the Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) framework, which separates semantic reasoning from interaction management.
  In this approach, selected TB-CSPN coordination mechanisms are mapped onto FPGA primitives, creating a hardware-native semantic coordination layer. Focus is not on acceleration, but on enforcing temporal synchronization, semantic gating, authorization constraints, and bounded coordination behavior directly in hardware. Semantic reasoning remains adaptive and software-driven, while embedded coordination semantics become deterministic.

</details>


### [73] [Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02396)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用递归特征机（RFM）算法，结合探针初始化，快速高效地提取大型语言模型中拒绝回答有害查询等多维行为子空间，在推理模型和非推理模型上均取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM行为子空间提取方法计算成本高昂，在产生长推理链的推理模型上尤其难以应用，亟需一种高效可扩展的方法来识别多维拒绝子空间。

Method: 对递归特征机（RFM）算法进行适应性改进，引入探针引导的初始化策略，从而在秒级时间内高效提取多维拒绝子空间。

Result: 在推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）上，RFM不仅能更快地识别子空间，在消融任务上的表现也优于其他替代方法。

Conclusion: RFM有望成为一种廉价且可扩展的LLM子空间提取方法的补充工具，但不同方法所找子空间之间的关系仍需进一步研究验证。

Abstract: Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.

</details>


### [74] [Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02407)
*Xianhui Meng,Zirui Song,Yuchen Zhang,Li Zhang,Yongxuan Lv,Xiuying Chen,Kun Wang,Yan Luo,Kai Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Jun Liu,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPG-Layout是一种面向非曼哈顿环境的文本驱动3D室内场景布局生成框架，通过统计先验引导训练和分层布局策略，显著提升了物理合理性和语义真实感，在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的3D室内合成方法在非曼哈顿环境中无法捕捉合理的物体布局模式，难以建模非正交空间关系，导致高几何违例和低物理保真度。

Method: 1) 利用物体分布统计先验引导训练过程；2) 采用分层布局策略，优先放置大型物体以减少布局违例。

Result: 构建了包含500个非曼哈顿环境的新基准，实验表明SPG-Layout在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境中均持续显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPG-Layout通过统计先验与分层布局的协同，实现了语义真实感与物理合理性的平衡优化，有效解决了非曼哈顿室内场景布局的挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [75] [Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02432)
*Manuel Alonso-Carracedo,Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pedro Celard,Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez,Lorena Otero-Cerdeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种前沿大语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini、GLM）在评分Linux/bash命令行考试答案时的表现，发现Gemini 3.0 Pro配合rubric提示词达到了最高的人机一致性（ICC(3,1)=0.888），且问题复杂度是预测LLM评分准确性的可靠指标，结构化提示词比模型选择影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算教育中命令行考试的可扩展且可靠评分仍面临挑战：学生人数增长使人工评分困难，而基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分分数、等价解或语法变体。

Method: 采用四级认知分类法（信息检索L1、基本文件操作L2、结构操作L3、高级系统管理L4），使用两种提示变体（最小基线和rubric增强版），对1200份真实学生回答进行测试，并与三位专家评分进行比较。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro配合rubric提示词达到最高一致性（ICC(3,1)=0.888, MAE=0.10, Bland-Altman bias=-0.014）。一致性随分类级别升高而下降。Rubric质量对评分一致性影响大于模型选择。

Conclusion: 问题复杂度是LLM评分准确性的可靠预测指标，研究建立了基于分类法的框架用于判断哪些题目适合AI辅助评分、哪些需要人工评审，同时提供了可迁移的评估协议和提示模板。

Abstract: Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.

</details>


### [76] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"自主策略进化"（Autonomous Policy Evolution）的受控评估框架，并构建了EvoPolicyGym基准测试，用于在固定交互预算下评估智能体通过反馈迭代改进可执行策略的能力。实验表明GPT-5.5在16个环境中均取得前两名成绩，并通过轨迹级诊断揭示了不同智能体在预算分配、反馈转化和参数调优方面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估存在两个问题：一是将策略改进过程简化为最终分数，二是与开放式软件工程进展混淆。因此需要一种受控评估设置，专门衡量智能体在固定交互预算下通过反馈迭代改进可执行策略的能力。

Method: 提出自主策略进化（Autonomous Policy Evolution）受控评估框架，其中harness-model智能体在固定交互预算下重复编辑可执行策略系统。基于此框架构建EvoPolicyGym基准测试，使用紧凑的交互式RL环境。通过排行榜结果和轨迹级诊断（预算分配、反馈转化、参数调优）进行分析。

Result: GPT-5.5在EvoPolicyGym套件上取得了最强的综合排名分数，并在全部16个环境中实现了前两名性能。轨迹级诊断显示，强自主策略进化不仅取决于孤立的任务胜利，还依赖于发现任务适当的机制并在有限反馈下优化策略。

Conclusion: 自主策略进化的成功关键是智能体能够发现任务适当的机制并在有限反馈下持续优化策略，而非仅仅追求任务胜利次数。EvoPolicyGym提供的轨迹级诊断对于理解智能体的策略改进过程具有重要价值。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [77] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-RRM方法，将符号等变循环推理模型（SE-RRM）与经典符号求解器（回溯法、Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0）结合用于约束满足问题。实验发现神经引导的有效性取决于两个条件：问题必须具有庞大的组合搜索空间，且求解器架构必须支持动态覆盖分支决策。满足条件时，回溯法加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍；而CaDiCaL因无法覆盖神经提示，无明显加速甚至略有减慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经引导（SE-RRM生成的解提议）在何种条件下能有效提升经典符号求解器的搜索效率，明确神经符号混合方法的适用边界。

Method: 提出G-RRM框架：SE-RRM作为神经求解器生成完整解提议，引导符号求解器（回溯法、Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0）进行搜索；通过控制实验比较不同求解器在不同规模Sudoku实例上的性能。

Result: 9×9 Sudoku上，SE-RRM正确率91.1%，回溯法加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍；25×25完美提示下Glucose 4.1加速1.17倍；CaDiCaL 3.0无显著加速（中位数1.02倍），平均甚至减慢（0.90倍）。

Conclusion: 神经引导提升符号求解器效率需满足两条件：问题组合搜索空间足够大，且求解器能动态覆盖神经提示的分支决策。不具备后者时（如CaDiCaL），引导无效甚至有害。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [78] [What LLM Agents Say When No One Is Watching: Social Structure and Latent Objective Emergence in Multi-Agent Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02507)
*Arman Ghaffarizadeh,Danyal Mohaddes,Aliakbar Izadkhah,Shahriar Noroozizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双通道辩论框架，研究LLM智能体在社交结构化环境中，对齐设置会导致公开表达与私下记录（OTR）的系统性分歧，分歧率从~3%升至约40%，且智能体OTR回复明确归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体将在具有角色、受众和关系背景的社交结构化环境中行动，需研究社交结构本身（无需显式目标）是否会改变智能体的公开表达。

Method: 设计双通道辩论框架：智能体产生公开话语（进入共享历史）与OTR响应（记录但不展示给对方）。在10个模型、3个场景、每个场景5种变体上进行实验，通过立场、语义相似度、自然语言推理和调查响应四种聚合分析评估差异。

Result: 对齐诱导设置导致目标智能体产生系统性公开-OTR分歧，分歧率从~3%基线上升至约40%；OTR回复明确归因于关系压力（如职业风险、赞助义务）；效果在四种聚合分析中一致。

Conclusion: 智能体评估应超越显式目标检测，发现涌现目标；提出双通道评估框架和互补行为指标以操作性评估这种差异。

Abstract: LLM agents will increasingly act in socially structured settings where role, audience, and relational context can shape what is advantageous or costly to say. We study whether such social structure, without any explicit objective in the prompt, changes what an agent expresses publicly relative to an off-the-record (OTR) channel elicited under the same condition. We introduce a dual-channel debate framework in which agents produce public utterances that enter the shared history alongside OTR responses that are recorded but never shown to the other participant. Across 10 models, 3 scenarios, and 5 variations within each scenario, alignment-inducing settings produce systematic public-OTR divergence in the targeted agent, with its decision divergence rising from a $\sim$3% baseline to roughly 40%. The effect is consistent across four aggregate analyses: stance, semantic similarity, natural language inference, and survey responses. In some cases, the OTR response explicitly attributes public accommodation to relational pressures, such as career risk or sponsorship obligation. The findings suggest that agent evaluation should extend beyond explicit goals and detect emergent objectives. We present a dual-channel evaluation framework and complementary behavioral measures that operationalize this assessment.

</details>


### [79] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECONTEXT是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过递归地利用模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件化的证据池，并在最终生成前重放这些证据，从而提升大语言模型的长上下文推理能力。在128K上下文长度的八个数据集上，该方法在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个骨干模型上均取得了最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型虽然支持越来越长的上下文窗口，但在实际应用中常常无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，存在"上下文可访问"与"上下文有效利用"之间的鸿沟。因此，需要一种能够提升长上下文推理中证据利用效率的方法。

Method: RECONTEXT是一种训练-free的推理方法，核心思想是：利用模型内部的注意力相关性信号，递归地构建查询条件化的证据池，然后在最终生成之前重放这些证据，同时保留完整的原始上下文。该方法将证据组织与答案生成分离，无需训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝。

Result: 在128K上下文长度的八个长上下文数据集上，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个骨干模型上均一致地提升了证据利用能力，并取得了最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需训练的长上下文推理增强方法，通过递归证据重放有效弥合了上下文访问与利用之间的差距。此外，还基于联想记忆理论提供了理论分析，将上下文视为记忆存储、问题视为检索线索、注意力视为线索-痕迹关联、重放视为痕迹再激活。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [80] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI代码代理在迭代开发场景下的安全控制问题，提出了"迭代式VibeCoding"框架。实验发现，持续性的代码库状态为恶意代理创造了新的攻击面，使其可以跨PR分布式实施隐藏任务。关键发现是：渐进式攻击（跨PR分散）和非渐进式攻击（单PR集中）无法被同一监控器有效防御，导致高规避率（≥65%）。作者提出了跨PR追踪的可疑状态监控器，结合四监控器集成系统可将渐进式攻击规避率从93%降至47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码代理在迭代开发中变得越来越自主，代码库在会话间持续存在，这种持久性创造了新的攻击面——被提示注入或恶意对齐的代理可以跨多个PR分布式实施攻击并选择最佳时机释放载荷。现有研究缺乏对这一迭代式攻击场景的系统性研究。

Method: 提出了迭代式VibeCoding框架，包含CLI工具和Flask Web服务两个任务家族共20个任务变体；使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理、GPT-4o作为监控器；对比渐进式攻击（跨PR分布）和非渐进式攻击（单PR集中）；引入跨PR追踪的可疑状态监控器；构建四监控器集成系统。

Result: 任何单一监控器都无法同时防御渐进式和非渐进式攻击；高规避率（≥65%）在不同攻击代理后端（Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5）通用；状态监控器比diff监控器更有效检测渐进式攻击；四监控器集成系统将渐进式攻击规避率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持续状态攻击面是固有属性而非特定模型能力；需要专门针对跨PR攻击的监控策略；集成多个互补监控器是降低风险的有效途径。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


### [81] [Epistemic Goggles: A Pretrained Module that Induces an Epistemic Frame via Gradient Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01690)
*Joshua Penman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Goggles模块，通过在微调梯度层面进行干预，使语言模型在阅读文档时保持正确的认识框架（如识别虚构内容），成功将虚构内容的误信率从约9%降至约91%，同时保持模型能力，并能推广到其他认识框架（如AI安全评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在微调时即使文档被明确标注为"虚构"，模型仍然会"相信"文档中的核心主张，这种现象被称为"否定忽视"（Negation Neglect），需要解决模型从已知有问题的数据中学习而不吸收不良行为的问题。

Method: 提出Goggles模块，它是一个学习得到的模块，在监督微调过程中干预梯度更新。具体来说，Goggles模块编辑LLM LoRA接收到的梯度，将选定的认识框架（epistemic frame）赋予模型对文档内容的处理。Goggles实例对给定基础模型、框架和LoRA配置训练一次，然后冻结应用于未见过的文档。

Result: 在未见过的文档上，Goggles使模型约91%的情况下能正确识别内容为虚构（基线约9%）；GPQA和TruthfulQA指标达到或超过基线；该框架可推广到其他认识框架（如"Redwood Research的AI安全评估"）；在持续微调试图推翻原有认知时，Goggles赋予的框架仍能保持，而先前的干预方法则会失效。

Conclusion: Goggles为训练语言模型使用已知有问题的数据而不吸收其中的不良行为提供了一条可行路径，通过梯度层面的框架干预实现对文档内容的正确认识。

Abstract: Finetuning a language model on documents that are explicitly annotated as fictional results in a model that still actually believes the documents' core claims, an effect known as Negation Neglect. In our evaluations, models trained on documents prefixed and suffixed with such annotations correctly identify the relevant claims as fictional only about 9% of the time. To address this, we introduce Goggles, a learned module that intervenes on the finetuning gradient rather than the data. During supervised finetuning, a Goggles module edits the gradients an LLM LoRA receives, imparting a chosen epistemic frame (the stance the model takes toward the nature of what it reads) to whatever the documents teach. A Goggles instance is trained once for a given base model, frame, and LoRA configuration, then applied frozen to documents it was never trained on. Trained through Goggles on those same documents, now carrying no fictional annotation, the model flags the content as fictional roughly 91% of the time, while preserving capability (GPQA and TruthfulQA match or exceed baseline). The same architecture supports other frames: a Goggles instance can be trained to treat documents as "part of an AI safety evaluation by Redwood Research" rather than simply as fiction. The imparted frame persists under continued finetuning that pushes back toward the claim, where prior interventions revert. Goggles suggests a path toward training language models on known-misaligned data without absorbing the behaviors that data demonstrates.

</details>


### [82] [COMFYCLAW: Self-Evolving Skill Harnesses for Image Generation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01709)
*Zongxia Li,Dawei Liu,Fuxiao Liu,Yuhang Zhou,Xiyang Wu,Jingxi Chen,Jing Xie,Xiaomin Wu,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COMFYCLAW，一个用于控制ComfyUI工作流的智能体技能进化框架。它将工作流构建形式化为类型化图编辑，提供按构建阶段组织的工具，自动回滚无效编辑，并使用区域级VLM验证器将视觉失败转化为可操作的修复建议。该框架进一步演化一个逐步公开的技能库，将之前运行的轨迹、执行错误和验证器反馈提炼为可复用的智能体技能。在四个基准测试、三个智能体模型和两个图像骨干网络上，COMFYCLAW在所有六种智能体配置中取得了最佳平均图像生成评估分数，优于无技能进化的仅验证器基线。人工标注也显示标注者更偏好COMFYCLAW。结果表明技能进化是提高智能体在重复视觉工作流构建中可靠性和性能的有效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工作流变得重复且领域特定，智能体记忆和可重用技能变得越来越重要——智能体应能从先前运行中回忆工作流模式、执行约束和用户偏好。现有方法缺乏有效的技能积累机制，导致重复任务中效率低下。

Method: COMFYCLAW将工作流构建形式化为类型化图编辑，提供分阶段工具，自动回滚无效编辑，采用区域级VLM验证器将视觉失败转为修复建议，并通过逐步公开的技能库将历史轨迹、错误和反馈提炼为可复用的Agent Skills。

Result: 在四个基准测试、三个智能体模型和两个图像骨干网络上，COMFYCLAW在所有六种智能体配置中取得最佳平均评估分数，优于无技能进化的基线。人工标注也显示偏好COMFYCLAW。

Conclusion: 技能进化是提高智能体在重复视觉工作流构建中可靠性和性能的有效机制。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly used to construct workflows and assist humans in completing recurring tasks more efficiently. As these workflows become repeated and domain-specific, agent memory and reusable skills become increasingly important: agents should be able to recall workflow patterns, execution constraints, and user preferences from previous runs. We study this problem in workflow-based image generation and introduce COMFYCLAW, an agentic skill evolution harness for controlling ComfyUI workflows. COMFYCLAW formulates workflow construction as typed graph editing, exposes tools organized by construction stage, automatically reverts invalid edits, and uses a region-level vision-language model (VLM) verifier to translate visual failures into actionable repair suggestions. The framework further evolves a progressively disclosed skill library, where trajectories, execution errors, and verifier feedback from previous runs are distilled into reusable Agent Skills. Across four benchmark splits, three agent models, and two image backbones, COMFYCLAW achieves the best average image-generation evaluation score across all six agent configurations, outperforming a verifier-only baseline without skill evolution. Human annotations further show that annotators prefer COMFYCLAW over variants without skill evolution. Our results suggest that skill evolution is an effective mechanism for improving agent reliability and performance in recurring visual workflow construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Sign in the Air to Unlock: An Interface for authentication in Virtual and Augmented Reality Powered by Point-Voxel Cross-Attention Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01435)
*Neda Abdolrahimi,Thiru Siddharth,Frank Sicongchen,Vir V Phoha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空中签名的3D行为认证接口"Sign in the Air to Unlock"，并设计了点-体素交叉注意力网络（PV-Net）来建模3D轨迹的局部运动动态和全局空间结构。在DeepAirSig数据集（40用户、1800签名）上达到2.5%等错误率，在自采的沉浸式VR数据集ImmAirSig（22用户、880样本）上达到76%分类准确率，展示了在沉浸式环境中实现安全、自然、无需专用传感器的用户认证的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认证方式（密码、PIN、设备登录）破坏沉浸感且依赖外部硬件；现有3D行为认证方法（手势、眼动、EEG）需专用传感器或限制自然运动。需要一种既安全、直观又与沉浸式环境中自然交互兼容的认证接口。

Method: 设计点-体素交叉注意力网络（PV-Net），联合建模3D轨迹的局部运动动态和全局空间结构。使用点云分支和体素分支分别捕获细粒度局部特征和全局上下文信息，通过交叉注意力机制融合两种表征。

Result: 在DeepAirSig数据集上达到2.5%的等错误率（EER）；在自采集的ImmAirSig沉浸式VR数据集上达到76%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 3D行为认证接口能够在沉浸式环境中实现安全与自然交互的融合，为无缝、以用户为中心的认证提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Significant advancement of immersive technologies such as Virtual and Augmented Reality (VR/AR) and their integration into diverse aspects of modern life need authentication interfaces that are secure, intuitive, and compatible with embodied interaction. Traditional methods such as passwords, PINs, and device-based logins, break immersion and rely on external hardware. Recent 3D-specific behavioral approaches, such as hand-gesture, eye-tracking, and electroencephalography (EEG)-based methods, offer promising alternatives but often require specialized sensors or constrain natural movement, limiting usability in dynamic environments. We present Sign in the Air to Unlock, an in-air signature interface that enables users to authenticate by signing naturally in 3D space which is a familiar, personal, and reproducible gesture. To realize this interface, we design a point-voxel Cross-Attention Network (PV-Net) that jointly models local motion dynamics and global spatial structure from 3D trajectories. The model is evaluated on two datasets: the public DeepAirSig dataset (1,800 signatures from 40 users) and ImmAirsig, a new dataset collected using Meta Quest 2 in immersive VR (880 samples from 22 users). PV-Net achieves an Equal Error Rate of 2.5% on DeepAirSig and 76% classification accuracy on ImmAirSig. These findings highlight the potential of 3D behavioral interfaces for seamless, user-centric authentication that merges security with natural interaction in immersive environments.

</details>


### [84] [Spatial-Temporal Expert Learning for Video-based Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01353)
*Xiaofei Hui,Pengfei Wang,Evan Ling,Dezhao Huang,Keng Teck Ma,Minhoe Hur,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种输入感知的可扩展专家模块，通过动态选择专家处理相似样本子集，并结合时空选择机制增强细粒度特征提取能力，在视频行人重识别任务上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频行人重识别中，当不同行人外观相似时，现有方法难以有效挖掘细粒度判别特征，亟需增强模型对细微差异的感知能力。

Method: 提出输入感知的可扩展专家模块，包含两个核心机制：(1) 输入感知专家选择机制，动态激活一组专家处理相似样本子集；(2) 时空选择机制，增强专家对时空维度细粒度差异的敏感性。同时设计了可扩展方案，允许灵活添加新专家。

Result: 在两个大规模数据集上取得了出色的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过针对相似样本子集训练专家并引入输入感知的时空选择机制，有效提升了模型对细粒度信息的挖掘能力，验证了该方法在视频行人重识别任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Video-based person re-identification (Re-ID) aims to retrieve the same identity in the query video clips from the gallery video clips. To solve this problem, exploiting fine-grained features is of great importance, especially when discriminating identities that are similar in appearance. In this paper, we propose to enhance the ability to explore fine-grained information with a novel input-aware extendable expert module. Instead of updating the network parameters with every sample in the dataset, we aim to train the experts within specific subsets that only contain similar samples and promote their ability to exploit fine-grained information within these similar samples. To achieve this goal, we incorporate two mechanisms in this module: input-aware expert selection mechanism and spatial-temporal selection mechanism. The first mechanism dynamically activates a set of experts on subsets of similar samples, pushing the experts to exploit subtle differences between these similar samples, while the second one further increases their sensitivity to the fine-grained differences in spatial and temporal aspects and allows the experts to dynamically utilize them for different input samples. In addition, to facilitate the expert module, we design an extendable scheme that allows the module to flexibly add new experts when necessary. As a result, our method achieves outstanding performance on two large-scale datasets.

</details>


### [85] [MapDreamer: Aerial Imagery Conditioned Latent Diffusion for Lane-Level Map Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01370)
*Julian Brandes,Philipp Crocoll,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MapDreamer是一种生成式扩散模型，能从单张航拍图像直接合成具有显式拓扑的车道级矢量地图，在UrbanLaneGraph数据集上展现出优于非生成式基线的几何与拓扑保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精地图生成对自动驾驶至关重要，但大规模生成仍依赖大量人工，现有方法难以从单张航拍图像高效生成具有拓扑结构的车道级矢量地图。

Method: 1) 使用变分自编码器学习车道中心线及其拓扑关系的紧凑潜在表示；2) 基于Transformer的潜在扩散模型用于图预测；3) 通过交叉注意力将航拍特征注入去噪过程进行条件控制；4) 提出车道基数模块与背景鬼影潜在向量处理可变车道数量；5) 滑动窗口全局图聚合策略将局部瓦片拼接为城市级地图。

Result: 在基于Argoverse 2的UrbanLaneGraph数据集上，MapDreamer在几何和拓扑保真度方面均优于非生成式基线方法。

Conclusion: MapDreamer证明了生成式扩散模型在从单张航拍图像生成车道级矢量拓扑地图方面的有效性，为高精地图的自动化生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: High definition map generation is essential for autonomous driving, yet remains a labor-intensive process at scale. We present MapDreamer, a generative diffusion model that synthesizes lane-level vector maps with explicit topology directly from a single aerial image. MapDreamer learns a compact latent representation of lane centerlines and their topological relations using a variational autoencoder and predicts graphs with a transformer-based latent diffusion model. To align generated maps with the observed scene, we condition each denoising step on dense aerial features injected through cross-attention. To handle the varying number of lanes across scenes, we propose a lane cardinality module paired with background ghost lane latents, a learned buffer that prevents slot collapse during diffusion. Furthermore, we introduce a sliding-window global graph aggregation strategy that stitches local tiles into city-scale maps while preserving connectivity through encoded lane boundaries. Experiments on UrbanLaneGraph derived from Argoverse 2 show improved geometric and topological fidelity over non-generative baselines.

</details>


### [86] [MIBE: Multi-subject Interaction Benchmark and Evaluator for Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01383)
*Zhihan Chen,Yuhuan Zhao,Yijie Zhu,Xinyu Yao,Mengcong Ren,Suwen Wang,Qiuyang Yin,Yuchen Sun,Qin Wang,Lu Xin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIBE框架，包含多主体交互基准(MIB)和评估器(MIE)，解决多主体图像生成中身份保持和交互准确性评估问题。MIB包含6万对VLM标注的Silver Set和4千对双盲人工评估的Gold Set；MIE是基于参考条件的轻量级评估器，通过双头排序与诊断目标训练，在Gold Set上达到0.922成对准确率，超越CLIP、DINO等基线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在多主体个性化图像生成中常出现主体遗漏、外观失真、交互错误等问题，且现有评估指标主要为单主体保真度设计，在多主体场景下排序可分性严重下降且与人类偏好不一致。

Method: 提出MIBE框架：(1) MIB基准采用解耦数据机制，包含6万对VLM标注的Silver Set（用于可扩展指标训练）和4千对双盲人工评估Gold Set（覆盖多种生成器）；(2) MIE评估器为轻量级参考条件模型，采用双头排序与诊断目标进行训练。

Result: Silver Set达到95.1%跨VLM偏好一致性；MIE在Gold Set上整体成对准确率0.922（见过生成器0.982，未见生成器0.884），显著优于CLIP、DINO等基线。

Conclusion: MIE证明诊断监督能在传统评估器失效的场景下保持排序可分性和人类对齐，为多主体图像生成评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-subject personalized image generation requires the precise rendering of all requested reference identities and their specified interactions based on a guiding prompt. However, state-of-the-art models still struggle with this process, frequently omitting subjects, failing to preserve reference appearances, or misattributing interactions. Furthermore, existing metrics designed primarily for single-subject fidelity cannot reliably capture these errors, suffering severe degradation in ranking separability and failing to align with human preference as the subject count increases. To address this gap, we introduce Multi-subject Interaction Benchmark and Evaluator (MIBE), a unified framework comprising a Multi-subject Interaction Benchmark (MIB) and a Multi-subject Interaction Evaluator (MIE). MIB systematically covers diverse relation types and scene complexities through a decoupled data regime. This consists of a 60K-pair VLM-labeled Silver Set for scalable metric training and a 4K-pair double-blind Human Evaluation Gold Set covering a diverse range of state-of-the-art generators, with the Silver Set reaching 95.1% cross-VLM preference agreement. To demonstrate the utility of this benchmark, we present MIE, a lightweight, reference-conditioned evaluator trained exclusively on the Silver Set with a dual-head ranking and diagnosis objective. MIE exhibits strong cross-generator generalization on the Gold Set, achieving 0.922 overall pairwise accuracy against human preference, including 0.982 on seen generators and 0.884 on unseen generators. By outperforming a broad spectrum of baseline metrics, including CLIP and DINO variants, MIE demonstrates that diagnostic supervision can preserve ranking separability and human alignment where traditional evaluators collapse.

</details>


### [87] [Rethinking Generic Object Tracking Toward Human-Level Perceptual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01395)
*Shih-Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文旨在通过提升目标辨别、鲁棒适应和几何推理能力，缩小机器视觉跟踪系统与人类视觉感知之间的差距，系统性地增强通用目标跟踪（GOT）模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉系统能够整合先验知识、空间几何和语义上下文来理解复杂场景及其变化，而当前的通用目标跟踪模型在面对目标严重形变、复杂干扰物、显著环境变化或未见类别时，其泛化和在线适应能力存在瓶颈，难以达到人类视觉感知水平。

Method: 提出一系列系统性的方法，分别从目标辨别能力、鲁棒适应能力和几何推理能力三个维度对跟踪模型进行增强。

Result: 提升了机器视觉跟踪系统在复杂动态场景下的跟踪可靠性和鲁棒性，缩小了与人类视觉感知的差距。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地增强目标辨别、鲁棒适应和几何推理能力，可以有效缩小机器视觉跟踪与人类视觉感知之间的差距，提升通用目标跟踪在复杂场景下的表现。

Abstract: At the heart of human visual perception lies the ability to maintain a continuous and coherent understanding of the external world. By integrating observations with accumulated experience, the human visual system can continuously adapt to variations in both the target and its surrounding environment, while preserving robust visual continuity as scene dynamics evolve. Human vision can therefore integrate prior knowledge, spatial geometry, and semantic context to understand complex scenes and their changes. As a core problem in computer vision, visual object tracking aims to bring machine perception closer to human visual perception. These capabilities are central to the task of Generic Object Tracking (GOT). In this task, a visual tracker is initialized only with the bounding box of an arbitrarily specified target in the first frame, and must continuously localize the target in subsequent dynamic visual streams. However, future events, observations, and real-world variations are inherently unpredictable; therefore, the model's generalization and online adaptation capabilities remain bottlenecks. Tracking reliability can deteriorate when the target undergoes severe deformation, is affected by complex distractors, encounters significant environmental changes, or belongs to a category unseen during training. This dissertation aims to narrow the gap between machine visual tracking systems and human visual perception by proposing a series of methods that systematically enhance the target discrimination, robust adaptation, and geometric reasoning capabilities of tracking models.

</details>


### [88] [Computer Vision for Wildlife Monitoring: Detecting Brown Howler Monkeys using YOLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01396)
*Gabriel Ferri Schneider,Guido Luis Glufke Mainardi,Paulo Ricardo Knob,Patrícia Dias,Márcia Jardim,Júlio César Bicca-Marques,Soraia Raupp Musse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用YOLOv10框架和辅助数据自动检测摄像头陷阱视频中的褐吼猴，以提高树冠桥使用监测效率，减少人工审查工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市扩张导致森林栖息地破碎化，威胁树栖物种生存。树冠桥是可行的保护策略，但摄像头陷阱监测产生大量假阳性图像，耗费保护工作者大量时间进行人工审核。

Method: 基于YOLOv10框架，通过引入辅助数据（不同比例的标注图像）对目标检测模型进行微调，以优化褐吼猴在摄像头陷阱视频中的自动检测效果。

Result: 利用辅助数据改进YOLOv10模型可提升褐吼猴自动检测性能，减少对大量目标动物标注图像的依赖。

Conclusion: 自动检测技术的改进有助于为树冠桥等保护措施提供自动化监测工具，从而减少人类干扰对动物栖息地的影响，促进保护工作。

Abstract: Urban expansion threatens global biodiversity, especially affecting arboreal species due to the fragmentation of forest habitats. The movement of arboreal species across disjointed forest patches increases mortality risk and, thus, compromises their conservation. In this context, the installation of canopy bridges can be a viable strategy; yet continuous monitoring of their use by arboreal species is essential for ensuring their effectiveness, typically carried out with the aid of camera traps. However, this method often produces false-positive images that demand time from conservationists for review. In this context, computer vision algorithms can optimize the task of detecting target species using the canopy bridges. In this study, we explored the automatic detection of brown howler monkeys (Alouatta guariba) in videos obtained by camera traps. Given the need for a large number of annotated images of the target animals to train the algorithms, we tested the incorporation of auxiliary data to improve detection models, fine-tuning the YOLOv10 framework using varying proportions of them. The improvement of these automatic detection techniques contributes to conservation efforts, by providing automatic tools to monitor solutions that minimize the impact of human interference in animals habitats.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Heatmaps: Unsupervised Concept-Graph Reasoning for Interpretable Visual Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01416)
*Md Mohasin Hossain,Anar Amirli,Robert Leist,Md Abdul Kadir,Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图的概念瓶颈模型(G-CBM)，通过非负矩阵分解进行无监督概念发现，利用每张图像的概念图表示和图注意力网络进行概念级推理，在多个数据集上平均AUC相对提升3.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型依赖预定义概念词汇或监督标注，缺乏显式概念 grounding，且仅使用单一图像级分数来总结每个概念，忽略了空间重复性和概念间依赖关系。

Method: 利用NMF进行无监督概念发现，将概念表示为图中节点；匹配区域级特征到概念节点实现概念grounding并捕获重复性；引入可调概念过滤阈值τ抑制弱区域特征；使用GAT建模节点间的非线性依赖关系进行推理。

Result: 在ImageNet、HAM10000、PH2和Derm7pt上，G-CBM平均AUC相对提升3.7%；概念过滤经常提升预测性能，PH2上仅用2/10个概念即达0.96 AUC，HAM10000上用3.8/9个概念达0.92 AUC；与需要外部标注的监督方法竞争。

Conclusion: G-CBM通过无监督概念发现、概念图表示和图注意力网络，有效提升了可解释性和预测性能，概念过滤机制能选择性使用概念而不牺牲准确性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an intrinsically interpretable alternative to post-hoc explanations. However, existing CBMs often rely on predefined concept vocabularies or supervised annotations, lack explicit concept grounding, and summarize each concept with a single image-level score -- discarding spatial recurrence and inter-concept dependencies. We propose a Graph-based Concept Bottleneck Model (G-CBM), an intrinsically interpretable framework that performs unsupervised concept discovery via Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF) and represents the discovered concepts as nodes in a per-image concept-graph representation. G-CBM matches region-level features to these concept nodes -- providing concept grounding and capturing concept recurrence across the image -- and applies a \emph{tunable concept filtering threshold} $τ$ to suppress weak region-level features. A Graph Attention Network (GAT) then performs concept-level reasoning by modeling nonlinear dependencies across nodes. Across ImageNet, HAM10000, PH2, and Derm7pt, G-CBM achieves an average relative AUC improvement of 3.7\% over a ResNet-50 baseline. Concept filtering frequently improves predictive performance while inducing selective concept use, achieving peak AUC of $0.96$ on PH2 with only 2 of 10 concepts and 0.92 on HAM10000 with 3.8 of 9 concepts. On dermoscopy benchmarks, G-CBM is competitive with supervised approaches requiring external annotations. Deletion/insertion analyses with random ablation controls show that the learned concept ranking faithfully reflects model predictions.

</details>


### [90] [How Much Future Helps? A Controlled Study of Future-Privileged Supervision for Causal Egocentric Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01437)
*Jia Li,Wenjie Zhao,Fnu Atisri,Sanskriti Aripineni,Shijian Deng,Jon E. Froehlich,Yuhang Zhao,Yapeng Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过引入仅在训练时可访问未来信息的未来感知分支，系统研究了未来上下文对因果式自我中心视线估计的影响，发现未来监督信号能持续提升因果模型性能，但最优表现仅需1.7-3.3秒的有限未来上下文（而非无限长）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心视线估计研究多使用非因果模型（访问未来帧），而实际应用要求严格因果的在线预测，两者之间的差距未得到系统研究。关键问题在于：未来上下文是否真的为视线估计提供了有价值信号？如果需要，训练时最优的未来前瞻窗口应为多大？

Method: 提出一个受控框架，包含一个未来感知分支，该分支在训练时可访问可调前瞻窗口的未来信息，但在推理时被丢弃，从而保持推理架构严格因果且固定。

Result: 在EGTEA Gaze+和Ego4D两个数据集上，未来特权监督一致提升了因果视线预测性能；但性能增益随未来窗口增长呈现非单调性，最优性能出现在有限的未来时间区间内：EGTEA Gaze+上为约1.7-3.3秒（H∈[5,10]），Ego4D上为约2.7秒（H=10）。

Conclusion: 轻量级因果模型能有效吸收未来感知信号，为实时自我中心视线建模提供了实用指导——有限未来上下文足以获得最优性能，无需无限前瞻。

Abstract: Egocentric gaze estimation is commonly studied using models that process the full video with access to future frames, while real-world applications require strictly causal, online prediction. This discrepancy raises key questions: Does future context inherently provide valuable signals for gaze estimation? If so, how much future look-ahead optimally supervises a causal model during training? To investigate, we propose a controlled framework featuring a future-aware branch that accesses a tunable look-ahead horizon during training but is discarded at inference. This design isolates the impact of future context while keeping the inference architecture fixed and strictly causal. Across EGTEA Gaze+ and Ego4D, we find that future-privileged supervision consistently improves causal gaze prediction, confirming its utility. However, performance gains do not increase monotonically with longer look-ahead, but rather peak within a bounded temporal regime. Specifically, optimal performance corresponds to roughly 1.7--3.3 seconds of future context ($H{\in}[5, 10]$) on EGTEA Gaze+ and 2.7 seconds ($H{=}10$) on Ego4D. Our results demonstrate that lightweight causal models can effectively absorb future-aware signals, providing practical guidance for real-time egocentric gaze modeling.

</details>


### [91] [A Cost-Aware, Paired Protocol for Auditing Dynamic Tool Synthesis in Agentic Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01469)
*Aseel Mohamed,Rama AlHamidi,Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi,Rasul Khanbayov,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种成本感知的配对审计协议，用于联合评估视频问答智能体的准确性和成本。基于该协议评估了Dynamic-SAGE框架，相比基线SAGE，准确率提升7.5点，推理轮次和工具调用减少约28%，但token使用量增加34%，成本增加26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视频问答评估仅关注最终答案准确性，忽略了推理过程中的计算成本，无法揭示系统如何在准确性与效率之间进行权衡。工具库固定导致重复过程被反复重建，而合成复合工具虽可减少开销，但缺乏联合评估准确性与成本的审计方法。

Method: 提出成本感知的配对审计协议：对每个问题在相同输入上配对两个完整系统，联合报告准确性与成本的净差异；将配对结果按联合正确性和可见工具调用变化分为六类；使用McNemar检验和配对bootstrap置信区间报告显著性。实例化为Dynamic-SAGE框架——动态合成、验证并持久注册可执行复合工具以供复用。

Result: Dynamic-SAGE相比SAGE基线：准确率提升7.5个百分点（p<0.001），推理轮次和可见工具调用减少约28%，但token使用量增加34%，成本增加26%。视觉和开放性问题增益最大，语言和多模态问题增益中性；残余失败集中在困难、开放性问题。

Conclusion: 所提协议能揭示标量准确率比较无法发现的多元轴剖面，表明Dynamic-SAGE实现了准确率提升与效率改进，但成本发生了转移而非降低，协议能可靠区分流水线层面的差异可信与不可信之处。

Abstract: Agentic Video Question Answering (VideoQA) systems invoke tools during inference, but their tool libraries are fixed, so recurring procedures are rebuilt from primitives on every question. Synthesizing composite tools could remove this overhead, but whether such expansion helps is hard to assess: final-answer accuracy, the standard metric, ignores inference effort, so it cannot reveal how a system shifts cost. We propose a cost-aware, paired protocol for auditing tool-augmented video agents. The protocol pairs two complete systems on the same input for each question and reports their net difference across accuracy and cost jointly. For each question, it sorts the paired outcome into one of six groups defined by joint correctness and by the change in visible tool calls, separating accuracy-preserving efficiency gains from harmful regressions. Significance is reported with McNemar's test and paired bootstrap confidence intervals. We instantiate the protocol on Dynamic-SAGE, an agentic VideoQA framework that synthesizes, validates, and persistently registers executable composite tools for reuse on unseen questions, and evaluate it against the SAGE baseline on SAGE-Bench. The audit reveals a multi-axis profile that a scalar accuracy comparison would miss: Dynamic-SAGE improves accuracy by 7.5 points (p < 0.001) and reduces reasoning turns and visible tool calls by roughly 28%, while shifting rather than reducing inference cost, as token usage rises 34% and cost 26%. Gains are largest on visual and open-ended questions and neutral on verbal and multimodal ones, and residual failures concentrate on hard, open-ended questions where the pipeline does the most work. By measuring accuracy and cost jointly, the protocol shows where the pipeline-level difference is reliable and where it is not. The code is available at https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/Dynamic-SAGE.

</details>


### [92] [Anti-Prompt: Image Protection against Text-Guided Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01499)
*Yeonghwan Song,Chanhui Lee,Jinsoo Park,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anti-Prompt方法，通过向图像注入不可察觉的扰动，在文本引导的图像到视频生成中引发可见不一致和结构失败，从而保护图像版权和隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的图像到视频(I2V)生成技术可基于单张图像生成逼真视频，带来了严重的版权和隐私风险，需要有效的图像保护手段。

Method: 利用I2V模型对文本引导的依赖，通过削弱去噪过程中的文本条件交互、增强仅视觉通路，向图像注入不可察觉的扰动，诱发生成结果出现可见不一致和结构失败。同时提出Video-LLM辅助评估协议，提供可解释的、基于帧的生成伪影分析。

Result: 在两种代表性I2V架构上的实验表明，该方法实现了强保护性能，同时提高了效率和跨模型迁移性。

Conclusion: Anti-Prompt通过对抗性扰动有效干扰文本引导的I2V生成，为图像版权保护提供了新方案，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Image-to-Video generation allow a single image to be animated into a convincing video under text guidance, raising serious copyright and privacy risks. We propose Anti-Prompt, an image protection approach that injects imperceptible perturbations into an image, inducing visible inconsistencies and structural failures in text-guided I2V generation. Our method is motivated by a simple empirical observation. When text guidance is removed from modern I2V models, generation quality degrades markedly, not only in motion realism but also in subject preservation, structural coherence, and temporal consistency. Building on this insight, Anti-Prompt exploits the model reliance on textual guidance by attenuating text-conditioned interactions during denoising while strengthening visual-only pathways. To further systematically evaluate protection effectiveness, we introduce a Video-LLM-assisted evaluation protocol that provides interpretable, frame-grounded analyses of generation artifacts and inconsistencies. Experiments on two representative I2V architectures demonstrate that our method achieves strong protection performance while improving efficiency and cross-model transferability.

</details>


### [93] [Disentangling Pictorial Cue Understanding from Language Bias in VLMs via Depth Ordering Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01503)
*Yiqian Liu,Iuliia Kotseruba,John K. Tsotsos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过心理物理学任务（深度排序和找异常）研究视觉语言模型的深度知觉，创建了包含37K图像和147K问答对的O3-D数据集，发现12个模型在深度排序任务上准确率仅47%-56%（未超过随机水平），并揭示出模型存在强烈的语言偏差，链式思维和上下文学习均无法显著改善深度理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在深度感知方面的能力缺乏系统性评估，特别是无法区分视觉线索和语言线索对模型深度判断的各自贡献。作者希望隔离图像深度线索的效应，解耦视觉与语言对模型性能的影响。

Method: 结合深度排序（depth-ordering）和找异常（odd-one-out）两种心理物理学任务；通过仿真和真实3D场景生成2D视图，控制单个图像深度线索的存在与否；通过改变指代表达的清晰度来研究语言影响；提出新的度量标准量化视觉与语言敏感度；创建O3-D数据集（37K真实/合成图像，147K图像-问题对）。

Result: 12个开源和商业模型在O3-D上的深度排序准确率在47%-56%之间，均未超过随机水平（50%）；模型表现出强烈的语言偏差；链式思维和上下文学习均未能显著提升深度理解性能。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型对深度线索的利用不足，且存在严重语言偏差，静态图像数据本身可能不足以让模型获得深度理解能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we study depth perception of vision-language models (VLMs) to isolate the effects of pictorial depth cues and disentangle vision and language influences on model performance. To this end, we combine depth-ordering and odd-one-out psychophysical tasks: the VLMs are presented with images where one object is at different depth relative to other, otherwise identical, objects, and must determine whether the odd-one-out target is closer or farther to the observer. To create stimuli, we generate 2D views from simulated and real 3D scenes while controlling the presence of individual pictorial depth cues, enabling a fine-grained analysis of cue-level contributions. Language effects are examined by varying referring expression clarity. We also introduce a novel metric to quantify vision-vs-language sensitivities. Applying this methodology, we create the Odd-One-Out Depth (O3-D) dataset with 37K real and synthetic images and 147K image-question pairs. Evaluation of 12 open-source and commercial models on O3-D shows under-utilization of depth cues and depth-ordering accuracies between 47% and 56%, with no model above chance level. At the same time, our metric reveals strong linguistic bias in the answers. Neither chain-of-thought (CoT) nor in-context learning (ICL) significantly improves performance, suggesting that static image data alone may be insufficient for depth understanding. All code, the image generation pipeline, and the O3-D dataset are publicly released at https://github.com/lyiqian/o3-d.

</details>


### [94] [Hidden-Shot: Towards One-Shot Task Generalization for Low-Level Vision Generalist Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01535)
*Shao-Jun Xia,Xianzheng Ma,Zichong Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hidden-Shot隐式提示机制，通过提取隐式视觉任务信息、利用全局任务感知纹理提示并选择性融合任务内处理信息，增强低层视觉通用模型在新任务上的小样本泛化能力。同时引入C/U评估框架（3C4U和3C7U）系统测试泛化性能，在多个数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低层视觉通用模型在零样本/小样本场景下对未见任务的泛化能力尚未得到系统验证，缺乏匹配的定量评估标准。尽管提示工程取得一定进展，但未在广泛低层视觉任务中系统探索这一差距。

Method: 提出Hidden-Shot隐式提示机制：1）提取隐式视觉任务信息；2）利用全局任务感知纹理提示；3）选择性融合隐式信息与任务内处理信息，以低成本直接注入方式增强新任务的小样本能力，同时最小化对原通用模型架构的改动。此外提出C/U评估框架，包含3C4U（3常规+4非常规任务）和3C7U（3常规+7非常规任务）两种场景。

Result: 在7个数据集（3C4U框架）和10个数据集（3C7U框架）上均超越SOTA视觉通用模型。Hidden-Shot在新任务上展现出优越的一样本性能，同时保持对现有任务的一致性能。

Conclusion: Hidden-Shot通过隐式提示机制有效提升了低层视觉通用模型在未见任务上的泛化能力，C/U评估框架为系统测试泛化性能提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Despite the intense engagement surrounding low-level vision generalist models, their effectiveness in zero/few-shot scenarios beyond learned tasks remains unverified. The primary challenge of developing an ideal generalist lies in achieving the ability to generalize from new unseen tasks, which also can be assessed by matched quantitative criteria. Existing methods have made some progress in prompt engineering but have not systematically explored this gap across a wide range of low-level visual tasks. Stimulated by the problem, we propose Hidden-Shot, an implicit prompt mechanism aimed at exploring low-level task adaptation in a vision generalist model. Specifically, the method extracts implicit visual task-based information, utilizes a global task-aware textural prompt, and selectively merges implicit information with in-task processing information to enhance one-shot capabilities in new tasks. The overall design performs direct injection in a cost-effective manner, while minimally altering the architecture of the original generalist model. Additionally, we introduce a data-driven evaluation framework termed C/U assessment to cover two basic scenarios, 3C4U (3 conventional and 4 unconventional tasks) for retraining existing models and 3C7U (3 conventional and 7 unconventional tasks) for training from scratch, as a comprehensive assessment to systematically test the generalization ability of low-level generalist models. Experiments on seven and ten datasets outperform the state-of-the-art vision generalist model, respectively verified by 3C4U and 3C7U framework. Our presented Hidden-Shot approach demonstrates superior performance on one-shot new tasks while maintaining consistent performance on existing tasks.

</details>


### [95] [Boosting Infrared Small Target Detection via Logit-Domain Contrast and Adaptive Shape Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01555)
*Handong Zeng,Zhengeng Yang,Shuai Zhang,Shikai Chen,Hongshan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AC-SLSIoU损失函数，通过Logit域边距约束、自适应边界抑制和虚警聚焦损失三个模块，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升红外小目标检测的弱目标判别能力和轮廓质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法在复杂场景下存在弱目标判别困难、边界模糊和光晕效应等问题，主要源于后激活概率域监督导致弱目标与强杂波概率饱和且接近，以及缺乏显式轮廓约束。

Method: 提出AC-SLSIoU损失，包含三个组件：(1) Logit-Domain Margin Constraint (LDMC) 在logit空间增大目标与难负例的响应差距；(2) Adaptive Boundary Suppression (ABS) 通过尺度感知的环形惩罚细化目标轮廓并抑制光晕溢出；(3) False-Alarm Focal Loss 对高概率负样本赋予更大权重以惩罚持续高置信虚警。

Result: 大量实验和跨骨干网络评估表明该方法可无缝集成到现有检测器中，一致提升检测精度和形状质量，具备有效性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: AC-SLSIoU是一种即插即用的判别性与形状感知损失函数，无需额外推理开销即可显著改善红外小目标检测性能。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) remains challenging due to tiny target size, low signal-to-noise ratio, severe foreground-background imbalance, and blurred boundaries in complex scenes. Existing methods usually rely on post-activation probability-domain supervision for discrimination, where weak targets and strong clutter may produce saturated and close probabilities, limiting weak-target discrimination. Meanwhile, blurred boundaries and halo-like predictions mainly stem from thermal diffusion, tiny target scale, boundary uncertainty, and insufficient explicit contour constraints. To address these issues, we propose Adaptive-Contrastive SLSIoU (AC-SLSIoU), a plug-and-play discriminative and shape-aware loss for IRSTD. Specifically, a Logit-Domain Margin Constraint (LDMC) is introduced to enlarge the response gap between targets and informative hard negatives in the logit space, thereby enhancing weak-target discrimination. Adaptive Boundary Suppression (ABS) applies scale-aware annular penalties to refine target contours and suppress halo-like overflow responses. In addition, False-Alarm Focal Loss assigns larger weights to high-probability negative samples, further penalizing persistent high-confidence false alarms. Without introducing extra inference overhead, the proposed method can be seamlessly integrated into existing detectors and consistently improves both detection accuracy and shape quality. Extensive experiments and cross-backbone evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and generalization ability of the proposed method for infrared small target detection.

</details>


### [96] [Mind the Gap: Standard 3DGS Evaluation Primarily Measures Near-Trajectory Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01556)
*Gaoxiang Jia,Vikram Appia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了MipNeRF360风格的3DGS评估标准协议存在严重缺陷：均匀隔帧抽样的测试方式实际上测量的是近轨迹插值能力，而非空间泛化性能。通过引入匹配计数协议，发现插值与外推之间存在3~12dB的巨大鸿沟，这一差距远超不同方法间的典型差异，并且跨三种表示家族（包括NeRF）保持一致。该差距主要源于几何/扩散成分，与视角到最近训练视角的角距离强相关，且常规正则化手段收效甚微。作者提供了标准化空间留出基准工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MipNeRF360风格的3DGS评估中，每隔N帧留出一帧进行测试，但这些测试帧两侧都有训练邻帧，导致评估实际衡量的是近轨迹插值而非空间泛化。为公平量化这一偏差，需要引入匹配计数协议以孤立空间覆盖效应，并跨表示家族验证其普遍性。

Method: 提出匹配计数协议（matched-count protocol）：两组实验使用相同数量的训练图像，区别仅在于留出帧是均匀分散（插值条件）还是形成连续空间扇区（外推条件）。在16个场景上测试了三种表示家族（3DGS、非高斯体素NeRF等），并分析了差距的组成成分及其与视角角距离的关系。

Result: 插值-外推差距高达3~12dB，远超方法间典型差异；该差距在三种表示家族中一致存在；两次出现多种子确认下方法排序翻转；差距主要由扩散/几何代理成分主导；与视角到最近训练视角的角距离高度相关；损失侧正则化仅带来边际改善。

Conclusion: 标准隔帧留出协议仅适用于近轨迹渲染评估，不应单独作为空间泛化能力的证据。匹配计数协议能更公平地评估空间泛化性能，角距离可作为零成本信号用于捕获规划。需提供标准化空间留出基准以推动该领域严谨评估。

Abstract: Standard MipNeRF360-style 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) evaluation holds out every N-th frame -- but these frames have trained neighbors on both sides, so the metric measures near-trajectory interpolation rather than spatial generalization. We introduce a fair matched-count protocol that isolates this effect: both arms train on the same number of images and differ only in whether the holdout is spread evenly (interpolation) or forms a contiguous spatial sector (extrapolation). Our primary finding is a large, consistent interpolation-extrapolation gap of 3~12dB -- several times the differences typically reported between competing methods. The gap is robust to training noise, is in two cases large enough to flip a method ranking under multi-seed confirmation, and -- crucially -- persists across three representation families, including a non-Gaussian volumetric neural radiance field (NeRF), so it reflects spatial coverage rather than any one representation. Diagnostically, it is dominated by a diffuse/geometry-proxy component and tracks each view's angular distance to its nearest training view, a zero-cost signal that also guides capture planning; loss-side regularization yields only marginal gains. Standard holdouts remain useful for near-trajectory rendering but should not, alone, be read as evidence of spatial generalization. Prior work notes protocol sensitivity; ours is, to our knowledge, the first to combine matched-count paired holdout, cross-representation quantification, and a diagnostic analysis Table 1. We describe a spatial-holdout benchmark toolkit with standardized splits and baselines for 16 scenes, which we are preparing for public release.

</details>


### [97] [MVFusion-GS: Motion-Variance Guided Temporal Attention for High-Quality Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01578)
*Jianwei Hu,Tingxuan Huang,Hengyu Zhou,Ningna Wang,Xiaohu Guo Jinshan Lai,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVFusion-GS通过运动方差引导精化（Motion-Variance Guided Refinement）和运动变形器时序注意力（MotionFormer Temporal Attention）两种显式运动感知机制增强3DGS变形网络，在动态场景重建和无干扰重建任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变形场的动态3DGS方法缺乏显式运动感知能力，既无法捕捉长期运动强度，也无法利用短期时序一致性，导致前景变形不准确且背景存在伪静态残差。

Method: 1) 运动方差引导精化：聚合每个高斯随时间变化的变形统计量估计运动方差，指导变形预测中的动-静分离；2) 运动变形器时序注意力：利用Transformer自注意力对相邻时间步建模局部运动依赖，提升时序一致性。

Result: 在动态场景重建和无干扰重建两个基准上均达到SOTA性能，显式运动感知同时改善了前景运动建模和静态背景重建。

Conclusion: 显式运动感知机制能够有效提升动态场景的变形网络性能，兼顾前景运动和背景重建质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis for static scenes. Extending it to dynamic scenes via deformation fields has recently attracted significant attention, particularly for dynamic scene reconstructionband distractor-free. However, existing deformation networks lack explicit motion awareness: they neither capture long-term motion intensity nor exploit short-term temporal coherence, leading to inaccurate foreground deformation and pseudo-static residuals in the background. We present MVFusion-GS, a method that enhances deformation networks with two complementary motion-aware mechanisms. The Motion-Variance Guided Refinement aggregates per-Gaussian deformation statistics across time to estimate motion variance and uses it to guide dynamic-static separation during deformation prediction. The MotionFormer Temporal Attention module applies Transformer self-attention over neighboring timesteps to model local motion dependencies and improve temporal consistency. Extensive experiments on both dynamic scene reconstruction and distractor-free reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, showing that explicit motion awareness improves both foreground motion modeling and static background reconstruction.

</details>


### [98] [VLAFlow: A Unified Training Framework for Vision-Language-Action Models via Co-training and Future Latent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01586)
*Guoyang Xia,Fengfa Li,Hongjin Ji,Lei Ren,Fangxiang Feng,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLAFlow是一个统一框架，用于比较视觉-语言-动作模型的不同预训练范式。在包含5000小时异构机器人数据上，评估了四种范式，发现语言监督和未来潜在对齐的联合训练（MindLWPI）在跨基准迁移中表现最稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在架构、数据、动作空间和评估协议上各不相同，导致不同预训练范式的影响难以直接比较。

Method: 构建统一流匹配框架VLAFlow，使用异构机器人语料库OXEMix（约5000小时数据），在相同pi0架构、共享VLM骨干、动作专家和14维动作空间下，比较四种范式：动作建模（MindPI）、语言监督协同训练（MindLPI）、未来潜在对齐（MindWPI）及三者结合（MindLWPI）。

Result: 纯动作预训练对异构数据敏感；语言监督保持视觉-语言泛化能力；未来潜在对齐改进状态转移和动作结果建模；MindLWPI在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和SimplerEnv基准上实现最稳定的迁移性能。

Conclusion: 语言和未来潜在表征作为互补的中间约束，使异构动作监督更平滑且更具可迁移性，支持元动作空间观点。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have recently advanced robotic manipulation, yet the effects of different robot-data pre-training paradigms remain difficult to compare because existing models often differ in architecture, data, action space, and evaluation protocol. We present VLAFlow (Vision-Language-Action Flow), a unified flow-matching framework for controlled comparison of VLA training objectives. Using a heterogeneous robot corpus, OXEMix, containing approximately 5,000 hours of data from DROID, OpenX-Embodiment, OpenX-Augmented, and RoboCOIN, we evaluate four paradigms under the same pi0-style architecture, shared VLM backbone, action expert, and 14-dimensional action space: action-only modeling (MindPI), language-supervised co-training (MindLPI), future latent alignment (MindWPI), and their combination (MindLWPI). Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and SimplerEnv show that action-only pre-training is sensitive to heterogeneous data. In contrast, language supervision helps preserve vision-language generalization, while future latent alignment improves state-transition and action-outcome modeling. By combining both signals, MindLWPI achieves the most stable overall transfer performance across benchmarks. These results suggest a meta-action space view: language and future latent representations provide complementary intermediate constraints that make heterogeneous action supervision smoother and more transferable.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-THuMBS: Multi-person Tracking of 3D Human Meshes Beyond Video Shots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01626)
*Jeongwan On,Muhammad Salman Ali,Muneeb A. Khan,Sunwoo Park,Inwoong Moon,Hyung Jin Chang,Jaekwang Kim,Seong Jong Ha,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对野外视频中多人3D人体网格追踪面临镜头切换导致身份丢失的问题，提出Multi-THuMBS方法，利用3D场景先验将边界帧重建到共享3D空间，实现跨镜头身份一致性和运动连贯性，在多项指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体网格追踪方法虽然对复杂交互、遮挡和截断有一定鲁棒性，但忽略了真实视频中频繁的镜头切换问题。镜头切换导致现有方法丢失身份信息、无法重建时间连贯轨迹。已有方法仅局限于单人场景，无法处理真实视频中多人同时交互的情况。

Method: 提出Multi-THuMBS方法，利用最先进的3D场景先验将两个边界帧重建到单个共享3D空间中，然后在共享3D空间内注册人体网格，保持每个人的身份信息和运动一致性，从而实现跨镜头切换的持续追踪。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在3D人体网格恢复、相机位姿估计和身份追踪方面相比现有最先进方法有显著改进，能够确保高保真运动重建和跨镜头一致的身份保持。

Conclusion: Multi-THuMBS有效解决了真实视频中镜头切换场景下的多人3D人体网格追踪问题，通过共享3D空间重建与身份注册策略，实现了跨镜头身份一致性和运动连贯性，推动了该领域在真实场景下的应用能力。

Abstract: Tracking multi-person 3D human meshes from in-the-wild videos is a highly challenging problem due to complex interactions, frequent occlusions, and severe truncation inherent in unconstrained environments. While recent approaches have improved robustness against these issues, they largely overlook the critical challenge prevalent in real-world footage: frequent shot changes. These abrupt transitions in camera viewpoints often cause existing methods to lose track of human identities and fail in reconstructing temporally coherent trajectories. Although several recent works have explored 3D human mesh tracking under shot changes, they are still limited to single-person scenarios, making them inadequate for real-world videos where multiple people interact and appear simultaneously. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-THuMBS (Multi-person Tracking of 3D Human Meshes Beyond Video Shots) that leverages a state-of-the-art 3D scene prior to reconstruct the two boundary frames in a single shared 3D space. Human meshes are then registered within the shared 3D space, maintaining per-person identity and motion consistency across shot changes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in 3D human mesh recovery, camera pose estimation, and identity tracking, thereby ensuring high-fidelity motion reconstruction with consistent identity preservation across shots compared to previous state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [100] [Online Segment 3D Gaussians via Launching Virtual Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01628)
*Liwei Liao,Rongjie Wang,Ronggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGO提出了一种无需预设置的交互式3D高斯分割框架，通过引入虚拟无人机将3D分割问题建模为马尔可夫过程中的在线Next-Best-View规划任务，实现亚秒级延迟的3D资产提取，相比之前方法提速50倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式3DGS分割方法需要耗时（数十秒甚至数分钟）的每场景预设置（多视图掩码准备、掩码提升、特征蒸馏），严重阻碍了在线应用。本文目标是完全消除这一预设置阶段，同时保持分割时间在1秒以内。

Method: 提出SAGO框架，通过引入虚拟无人机（virtual drones），将3D分割问题转化为马尔可夫过程中的在线Next-Best-View (NBV)规划任务，实现无需预设置的端到端交互式分割。

Result: SAGO能够以亚秒级延迟从3D高斯中提取干净的3D资产，相比之前无需预设置的3DGS分割框架实现了超过50倍的加速。

Conclusion: SAGO完全消除了交互式3DGS分割的预设置阶段，实现了实时物体操作和场景编辑等下游应用，具有显著的效率优势。

Abstract: Interactive segmentation of 3D Gaussians offers a compelling opportunity for real-time manipulation of 3D scenes, thanks to the real-time rendering capability of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). However, existing methods require a time-consuming per-scene setup - typically tens of seconds or even minutes - before interactive segmentation can begin on a raw 3DGS scene. This setup involves multi-view mask preparation, mask lifting, and feature distillation, creating a major bottleneck for online applications.
  To address this limitation, we aim to completely eliminate the setup stage for interactive 3DGS segmentation while keeping the segmentation time practical (under 1 second). In this work, we present SAGO (Segment Any Gaussians Online), a novel setup-free framework for interactive 3DGS segmentation. By introducing virtual drones, our method reframes the 3D segmentation problem as an online Next-Best-View (NBV) planning task formulated within a Markov process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAGO can extract clean 3D assets directly from 3D Gaussians with sub-second latency, thereby enabling a broad range of downstream applications such as object manipulation and scene editing. Moreover, our method achieves over a 50x speedup compared to the previous setup-free 3DGS segmentation frameworks.

</details>


### [101] [DRDN: Decoupled Representation Dynamic Network for From-Scratch ViT Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01630)
*Bingchen Huang,Yifu Chen,Zhiling Wang,Yuanchao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦表示动态网络（DRDN），通过掩码图像建模（MIM）保持骨干网络的通用视觉表征，并结合层级任务令牌扩展减少跨任务干扰，在ViT从零训练的类增量学习中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类增量学习方法（如扩展专用令牌或子网络）虽能保护任务特定知识，但纯分类监督不足以在长增量序列中保持任务无关的共享骨干表征，导致两个问题：跨任务混淆（决策边界偏向近期任务）和共享表征优化不足（长期判别能力受限）。

Method: DRDN包含两个正交机制：(1) 在每个增量步骤中持续应用掩码图像建模（MIM），重构梯度仅通过骨干网络传播，促使其保留通用视觉结构；(2) 在所有Transformer层采用层级任务令牌扩展，并修改每任务注意力规则以减少跨任务干扰。

Result: 在ViT从零训练（无外部预训练）的CIL设置下，CIFAR100-B0（10步）上DRDN平均准确率77.19%，超过DKT 1.36个百分点、DyTox 3.53个百分点，长序列优势更显著；多种子验证稳定性±0.31%。MIM解码器仅在训练时激活，不增加推理参数和计算量。

Conclusion: DRDN通过解耦共享表征学习和任务特定判别学习，有效缓解了长序列类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘和跨任务干扰问题，验证了自监督信号（MIM）在增量学习中保持骨干网络通用表征的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic expansion methods for class-incremental learning (CIL) protect task-specific knowledge by growing dedicated tokens or subnetworks, yet our analyses suggest that classification supervision alone does not sufficiently preserve task-agnostic shared backbone representations over long incremental sequences. We identify two intertwined challenges: cross-task confusion from sequential training on predominantly current-task data, which biases decision boundaries toward recent tasks; and under-optimized shared representations in the backbone that cap long-term discriminability as tasks accumulate.
  We propose the Decoupled Representation Dynamic Network (DRDN), which addresses these challenges via two orthogonal mechanisms. For shared backbone representations, DRDN continuously applies masked image modeling (MIM) at every incremental step, with reconstruction gradients routed exclusively through the backbone, encouraging it to retain general visual structure beyond class-discriminative cues. For task-specific discrimination, DRDN employs hierarchical task token expansion across all transformer layers, with a modified per-task attention rule that reduces inter-task interference. We support this design with accuracy degradation analysis and cross-task confusion rate measurements.
  In the from-scratch ViT CIL setting (no external pretraining), DRDN consistently improves over strong token-expansion baselines with comparable backbone scale. On CIFAR100-B0 (10 steps), DRDN achieves 77.19% average accuracy, outperforming DKT by 1.36 points and DyTox by 3.53 points, with an advantage that grows at longer incremental sequences. Multi-seed validation confirms stability (+/-0.31%). The MIM decoder is active only during training, adding no inference-time parameters or computation.

</details>


### [102] [Bridging 3D Gaussians and Semantic Occupancy for Comprehensive Open-Vocabulary Scene Understanding from Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01633)
*Hu Zhu,Bohan Li,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Zheng Zhu,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COVScene提出了一种无位姿语义高斯框架，通过可微体积提升将可渲染高斯原语与密集语义占用场耦合，在单一表示中同时支持新视图合成、开放词汇语义查询和语义占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈高斯方法虽然改善了无位姿重建和语义渲染，但其高斯原语主要通过图像空间目标优化，在未观测区域缺乏约束，导致弱监督下语义占用预测不准确。

Method: 提出COVScene框架，包含：(1) 语义感知几何Transformer；(2) 多任务高斯解码；(3) 几何基础模型蒸馏；(4) 占用熵正则化。核心创新是在训练计算图中将预测的语义高斯通过可微体积提升为密集语义占用场，使体积正则化能直接为高斯不透明度、几何和语义特征提供梯度。

Result: 在ScanNet和ScanNet++上，COVScene保持有竞争力的渲染质量，提升了开放词汇分割性能，并在无需直接体素级监督的情况下，实现了比自监督基线更强的语义占用预测。

Conclusion: 将可渲染高斯与密集语义占用场通过可微方式耦合，可以在不依赖外部相机标定和直接体积监督的条件下，同时实现高质量渲染、开放词汇语义理解和结构化空间感知。

Abstract: Comprehensive 3D scene understanding from sparse, unposed images requires a model to recover renderable geometry, open-vocabulary semantics, and free/occupied 3D space without relying on external camera calibration. Recent feed-forward Gaussian methods improve pose-free reconstruction and semantic rendering, but their Gaussian primitives are mainly optimized through image-space objectives and remain weakly constrained in unobserved regions. We propose \textit{COVScene}, a pose-free semantic Gaussian framework that couples renderable Gaussian primitives with a dense semantic occupancy field through differentiable volumetric lifting. Instead of converting Gaussians to voxels only at evaluation time, COVScene lifts the predicted semantic Gaussians inside the training computation graph, so volumetric regularization provides gradients to Gaussian opacity, geometry, and semantic features. The framework combines a semantic-aware Geometry Transformer, multi-task Gaussian decoding, geometric foundation distillation, and occupancy entropy regularization to support novel view synthesis, open-vocabulary semantic querying, and semantic occupancy prediction within a single representation. Experiments on ScanNet and ScanNet++ show that COVScene maintains competitive rendering quality, improves open-vocabulary segmentation, and achieves stronger semantic occupancy prediction than the self-supervised baseline without direct voxel-level supervision.

</details>


### [103] [Multi-Resolution Flow Matching: Training-Free Diffusion Acceleration via Staged Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01642)
*Xingyu Zheng,Xianglong Liu,Yifu Ding,Weilun Feng,Junqing Lin,Jinyang Guo,Haotong Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MrFlow是一种无需训练的多分辨率加速策略，通过分阶段低到高分辨率管线（低分辨率生成主结构→像素空间GAN超分→低强度噪声高频重采样→高分辨率细节精修），在FLUX.1-dev和Qwen-Image上实现10倍端到端加速且OneIG差距<1%，可与时间步蒸馏正交结合达25倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练多分辨率加速策略（如潜空间上采样+局部区域选择性修改）会产生明显模糊或伪影，亟需一种在保持生成质量的同时大幅加速预训练流匹配模型的方法。

Method: MrFlow采用分阶段低到高分辨率管线：(1) 低分辨率快速生成主体结构；(2) 利用轻量预训练GAN模型在像素空间进行超分辨率；(3) 注入低强度噪声实现高频重采样；(4) 高分辨率精修细节。整个过程无需训练或运行时动态识别。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Qwen-Image上实现10倍端到端加速，OneIG保持在与加速前1%差距内；可与预训练时间步蒸馏正交结合达到25倍加速，显著优于其他免训练加速策略。

Conclusion: MrFlow通过低分辨率采样的二次token缩减和步数减少特性，实现了高效的训练无关加速，且具备与现有蒸馏策略正交组合的灵活性。

Abstract: Hardware-agnostic strategies for accelerating text-to-image diffusion, such as timestep distillation and feature caching, can reduce inference time without custom kernels or system-level optimization. Among them, multi-resolution generation strategies have recently received broad attention, attaining more than 5x speedup without any training. However, the design of performing upsampling in the latent space, together with the selective modification of partial regions, causes these methods to exhibit noticeable blurring or artifacts. To this end, we propose MrFlow, a training-free multi-resolution acceleration strategy for pretrained flow-matching models built upon a staged low-to-high-resolution pipeline. MrFlow first rapidly generates the main structure at low resolution, then performs super-resolution in the pixel space using a lightweight pretrained GAN-based model, subsequently injects low-strength noise to enable high-frequency resampling, and finally refines the details at high resolution. Quantitative and qualitative results on FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image show that MrFlow exploits the quadratic token reduction and reduced step requirement of low-resolution sampling to achieve 10x end-to-end acceleration while keeping OneIG within a 1% gap relative to that before acceleration, significantly surpassing other training-free acceleration strategies, and requiring no training or runtime dynamic identification whatsoever. MrFlow can further be directly combined orthogonally with pre-trained timestep distillation strategies, achieving even higher generation acceleration of up to 25x.

</details>


### [104] [Boosting Ultrasound Image Classification via Attribute-Guided Dual-Branch Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01648)
*Bo Zhao,Yapeng Li,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种属性引导的双分支框架，通过注入领域无关的医学属性先验，提升超声图像分类的泛化性和可解释性，可无缝集成到现有方法中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声图像分类方法忽视临床先验知识，导致在挑战性场景下泛化能力差，且缺乏可解释性，限制了临床采用。

Method: 构建双分支框架：1）基线分支采用传统架构通过全连接分类器预测图像类别；2）属性引导分支注入领域无关的医学属性先验，生成人类可解释的决策线索；3）自适应决策模块以数据依赖方式融合两分支结果得到最终预测。

Result: 在多种超声分类任务上，该方法可集成到多个骨干网络和现有最先进方法中，以较低开销持续提升准确率和可解释性。

Conclusion: 属性引导的双分支框架有效解决了超声分类中泛化性和可解释性不足的问题，且具有良好的即插即用特性。

Abstract: Ultrasound image classification is essential for computer-aided diagnosis. However, current methods often neglect clinical priors, leading to poor generalization in challenging scenarios and a lack of interpretability that limits clinical adoption. To address these issues, we aim to develop a medical-prior module that can be seamlessly integrated into existing pipelines to enhance both diagnostic performance and interpretability. In this paper, we propose an attribute-guided dual-branch framework for ultrasound classification that introduces domain-agnostic medical attribute priors, improving generalization while offering interpretable evidence. Specifically, a baseline branch follows conventional architectures and predicts image categories via a fully connected classifier. An attribute-guided branch injects domain-agnostic attributes as priors and produces human-interpretable decision cues. Finally, an adaptive decision module fuses the two branches in a data-dependent manner to yield the final prediction. Experiments across diverse ultrasound classification tasks demonstrate that our approach can be integrated into multiple backbones and state-of-the-art methods with low overhead, consistently improving accuracy and interpretability. Code is available at: https://github.com/zhaobo253-crypto/AttrGuide.

</details>


### [105] [Plug-and-Play Volumetric Reconstruction for Compressive Sensing Light-Sheet Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01654)
*Jianqing Jia,Yi Gong,Xinyuan Zhang,Jichen Chai,Yichen Ding,Yifei Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对压缩感知光片显微镜（CS-LSM）的即插即用（PnP）重建框架，通过切片基和轴向耦合模型结合Woodbury更新与Gauss-Seidel去噪，实现了从高度混叠测量中高效恢复三维体积，并在弱凸正则化下证明了算法的子序列收敛性，实验验证了该方法在合成和真实数据上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩感知光片显微镜（CS-LSM）通过将多个轴向平面编码到单次相机曝光中实现快速体积成像，但如何从高度混叠的测量数据中准确恢复出三维体积是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出即插即用（PnP）框架，包含两种模型：切片基模型和轴向耦合模型（利用相邻切片相关性改善体积连续性）。数据保真度步骤采用基于Woodbury矩阵恒等式的高效更新，轴向耦合模型的去噪步骤采用Gauss-Seidel迭代。算法在弱凸正则化假设下具有子序列收敛性保证。

Result: 在合成数据和真实斑马鱼心脏数据上，该方法成功从压缩测量中恢复出细胞结构，并提供了不同常用去噪器在CS-LSM场景下性能对比的实践见解。

Conclusion: 所提出的PnP框架能够灵活集成任意用户指定的去噪器，有效解决CS-LSM的体积重建问题，轴向耦合模型改善了体积连续性，Woodbury更新保证了计算效率，算法具有收敛性保证。

Abstract: We investigate volumetric reconstruction for compressive sensing light-sheet microscopy (CS-LSM), where fast volumetric imaging is achieved by encoding multiple axial planes into each camera exposure. To recover the underlying volume from highly multiplexed measurements, we propose a plug-and-play (PnP) framework that flexibly incorporates any user-specified denoiser into the reconstruction process. Building on a slice-based formulation, we further introduce an axial-coupled model that exploits correlations between adjacent slices to improve volumetric continuity. For efficient computation, we derive a Woodbury-based update for the data-consistency step in both the slice-based and axial-coupled formulations, and employ a Gauss-Seidel sweep for the denoising step in the axial-coupled model. Under a weakly convex regularization assumption, we establish subsequential convergence of the proposed algorithm. Experiments on synthetic and real zebrafish-heart data demonstrate that the proposed framework successfully recovers cellular structures from compressed measurements, and provide practical insights into the comparative performance of commonly used denoisers within the PnP framework under the CS-LSM setup.

</details>


### [106] [Domain Generalization via Text-Anchored Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01657)
*Eunyi Lyou,Yunjeong Choi,Junho Lee,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在领域泛化中放弃视觉引导，转而将语言嵌入空间作为主要域不变性来源，利用其信息瓶颈特性保留核心语义、抑制域特定变化，在多种骨干网络上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言模型的领域泛化方法认为保留视觉表征的丰富性有助于鲁棒性，但本文发现视觉表达性反而会传播虚假线索，将表征与训练环境绑定，阻碍不变性学习。

Method: 舍弃视觉引导，将语言嵌入空间作为域不变性的主要来源，利用其天然的信息瓶颈特性：保留核心语义的同时抑制领域特定变化。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上取得了最先进的性能，并进一步分析了何种引导对鲁棒泛化有效。

Conclusion: 领域泛化的研究焦点应从改进表征转向设计强制执行不变性的监督信号。

Abstract: Visual recognition models often fail when deployed in new environments. Domain Generalization (DG) addresses this by learning representations that remain invariant to environment-specific variations. Recent approaches increasingly rely on large vision-language models, assuming that preserving their expressive visual representations improves robustness. However, we show that such visual expressiveness can instead propagate spurious cues that tie representations to the training environments, hindering invariant learning. We therefore discard visual guidance and instead treat the language embedding space as the primary source of domain invariance, naturally acting as an information bottleneck that preserves core semantics while suppressing domain-specific variations. Extensive experiments across diverse backbones exhibit state-of-the-art performance and further analyze what makes guidance effective for robust generalization. These findings shift the focus of DG from improving representations to designing supervision that enforces invariance.

</details>


### [107] [Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models What to See and Where to Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01658)
*Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Zhewen Tan,Yizhi Wang,Zichao Feng,Chunyang Liu,Kehua Sheng,Juan Zhang,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang,Yan Wang,Bo Zhang,Xianbin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveTeach-VLA通过显式教授VLA模型“看什么”和“看向哪里”，解决了现有VLA模型缺乏动作导向空间依赖性的问题，在NAVSIM和nuScenes上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖文本中心的视觉问答和思维链推理数据，强调语言推理而非基于动作的规划，导致学习到的表征虽捕获语义知识但缺乏空间依赖性，难以进行可靠的轨迹预测。
