  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的甲状腺结节超声分割方法虽取得高Dice分数，但存在参数量大、依赖GPU反向传播训练、数学可解释性差等问题，限制了在资源受限环境中的部署。本文旨在探索一种无需反向传播、轻量级、数学可解释的分割方案。

Method: 采用Green Learning范式，构建无反向传播分割框架MedSaab-US：1）通过多级离散小波变换(DWT)结合多尺度(5×5, 11×11, 21×21)通道Saab变换提取多尺度空间-频率特征；2）使用标签辅助贪婪(LAG)特征选择保留最具判别性的特征；3）输入XGBoost分类器进行逐像素预测。Saab变换参数从数据统计中解析确定，XGBoost通过迭代贪心树构建无需反向传播。

Result: 在TN3K数据集（2879训练/614测试图像）上，MedSaab-US达到平均Dice 0.4784±0.2190、精确率0.5768、召回率0.5604。模型参数量低于500K，仅CPU推理速度约0.3秒/张。消融实验量化了各组件贡献及LAG特征选择和训练集大小的影响。

Conclusion: MedSaab-US作为甲状腺超声分割的非DL探索性基线，展示了Green Learning在医学影像分割中的潜力。分析了等回声结节带来的特殊挑战。未来可在此基线基础上进行改进。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) methods dominate thyroid nodule segmentation in ultrasound (US) images, achieving high Dice scores but at the cost of millions of parameters, GPU-dependent training via backpropagation, and limited mathematical tractability. These limitations impede deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we propose MedSaab-US, a backpropagation-free segmentation framework grounded in the Green Learning paradigm. MedSaab-US extracts multi-scale spatial-frequency features by combining multi-level Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) with multi-scale channel-wise Saab (Subspace Approximation with Adjusted Bias) transforms at patch sizes of 5 x 5, 11 x 11, and 21 x 21 pixels. Label-Assisted Greedy (LAG) feature selection retains the most discriminative features, which are fed to an XGBoost classifier for pixel-wise prediction. The Saab transform parameters are determined analytically from data statistics, while XGBoost employs iterative greedy tree construction without requiring backpropagation. Evaluated on the TN3K dataset (2,879 training and 614 test images), MedSaab-US achieves a mean Dice coefficient of 0.4784 +/- 0.2190, precision of 0.5768, and recall of 0.5604, with a model footprint under 500K parameters and CPU-only inference in approximately 0.3 seconds per image. We present this result as an exploratory non-DL baseline for thyroid ultrasound segmentation and analyze the specific challenges posed by isoechoic nodules. An ablation study further quantifies the contribution of each pipeline component, including separate evaluations of LAG feature selection and training-set size.

</details>


### [178] [DetailAnywhere: Fashion Detail Generation via Cross-Modal Feature Alignment Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02220)
*Zijun Li,Yimin Zhou,Jia Sun,Honglie Wang,Pengcheng Wei,Junlong Wu,Yongrui Heng,Jiyuan Wang,Huan Ouyang,Boheng Zhang,Huaiqing Wang,Dewen Fan,Qianqian Gan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向电商服装细节生成的新任务Fashion Detail Generation，并构建了首个包含4万+人工验证参考-细节对的基准FDBench。针对该任务中聚焦标记与真实感特写视图之间的语义鸿沟问题，提出跨模态特征对齐蒸馏方法CFAD，通过DINOv3教师网络进行双分支蒸馏，并引入一致性奖励模型通过强化学习优化生成。提出的DetailAnywhere模型在所有指标和人类评估上显著优于现有开源方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在电商应用中（虚拟试穿、海报生成等）取得了成功，但消费者在线购买服装时需要自由检查特定细节区域（如衣领、袖口、面料纹理），而现有方法未明确研究这一设置。

Method: 1）形式化定义了非模板化任务：基于聚焦条件（focus conditioning）的时尚细节生成。2）构建FDBench基准，含4万+人工验证的参考-细节对，覆盖41个类别。3）提出CFAD（跨模态特征对齐蒸馏），使用微调后的DINOv3教师网络通过双分支蒸馏将多模态扩散Transformer的两个分支对齐到共享语义空间。4）引入一致性奖励模型，沿三个质量轴联合评分图像对，并通过强化学习优化生成。

Result: DetailAnywhere模型在所有指标和人类评估上显著优于所有最先进的开源方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了电商服装细节生成任务，提出的CFAD方法和DetailAnywhere模型有效弥合了聚焦标记与细节视图之间的语义鸿沟，为该领域提供了新的基准和解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative AI has achieved remarkable success in e-commerce applications such as virtual try-on, poster generation, and product background synthesis. However, when making online purchasing decisions for apparel, consumers also desire the freedom to examine specific detail regions of interest, such as collars, cuffs, and fabric textures, yet existing methods have not explicitly studied this setting. We therefore formalize a new, non-template task: Fashion Detail Generation with focus conditioning, and release FDBench, the first benchmark comprising 40K+ human-verified reference-detail pairs across 41 different categories. This task poses a unique semantic gap challenge: the model must bridge the correspondence between a focus marker on a product reference image and a photorealistic close-up view of the indicated region, while faithfully preserving the garment's identity, without any precise prompt. To bridge this gap, we propose Cross-modal Feature Alignment Distillation (CFAD), which leverages a fine-tuned DINOv3 teacher to align both branches of a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer in a shared semantic space via dual-branch distillation. To further improve consistency between generated details and reference images, we introduce a consistency reward model that jointly scores image pairs along three quality axes and optimizes generation via reinforcement learning. Experiments show that our model DetailAnywhere significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art opensource methods across all metrics and human evaluations.

</details>


### [179] [Efficient Waste Sorting for Circular Economy: A Confidence-guided comparison between One-Vs-All and One-Vs-Rest Classification Strategies with Human-in-the-Loop for Automated Waste Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02230)
*Mohammed Fahad Ali,Dominique Briechle,Marit Briechle-Mathiszig,Tobias Geger,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文评估了两种分类策略(OvA和OvR)在德国城市戈斯拉尔垃圾分类中的应用，通过置信度阈值识别不确定样本进行人工审核，以平衡误分类与人工标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲各国废物处理法规复杂，德国各城市垃圾分类方案不一致，导致居民难以正确分类垃圾，阻碍循环经济发展。需要可配置的AI垃圾分类方案来适应不同城市的分类规则。

Method: 构建符合德国戈斯拉尔市垃圾分类标准的专用数据集；对比OvA(一对全部)和OvR(一对一)两种分类策略；通过设置不同置信度阈值识别可能误分类的样本，将不确定样本提交人工审核，评估误分类数量与人工标注成本之间的权衡。

Result: 论文比较了OvA和OvR两种分类策略在垃圾分类任务中的整体表现，以及在不同置信度阈值下识别不确定样本的误分类与人工审核成本的平衡效果。

Conclusion: AI垃圾分类方案需要可配置以适配不同地区的分类规则；通过置信度阈值筛选不确定样本进行人工审核，可在减少误分类和控制人工成本之间取得平衡，从而更有效地支持循环经济。

Abstract: The complexity of waste disposal regulations across European countries poses significant challenges for the residents and hinders the transition to a Circular Economy. In Germany, the proper sorting and disposal of household waste remains challenging across municipalities. Consequently, substantially reducing incorrectly disposed waste is vital for improving waste management and advancing the Circular Economy. AI-based waste sorting solutions can support residents through user-friendly tools, such as mobile applications, that guide proper waste disposal. To be effective in supporting the Circular Economy, however, these solutions must be configurable to reflect the specific waste sorting scheme of individual municipalities in Germany. In the scope of this work, an evaluation and analysis are performed of two prominent classification strategies: OvA and OvR. The research uses a dataset constructed in alignment with the waste categories and sorting scheme of the city of Goslar in Germany. Moreover, this work aims to extend beyond the overall performance by examining the behavior of OvA and OvR classification strategies in identifying samples likely to be misclassified. These classification strategies are compared by applying varying confidence thresholds to identify uncertain samples for subsequent human review. This evaluation aims to balance the number of misclassifications against the human effort required for data annotation.

</details>


### [180] [When Token Compression Breaks: Structural Pruning vs. Token Reduction for Robust ViT Segmentation under High Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02237)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了ViT语义分割中两种压缩范式：token压缩在低压缩率下有效但高压缩时性能急剧下降，结构剪枝退化更平滑且高压缩时更稳定；两者结合（剪枝后合并）能在高压缩下取得更好的精度-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token压缩方法评估主要聚焦于低到中等压缩率，缺乏对激进压缩和损坏输入下行为的理解；同时结构剪枝作为正交的高效化途径，鲜少在统一协议下与token压缩进行比较。

Method: 在ADE20K/Cityscapes及其损坏变体上，以匹配FLOPs为条件，系统比较了代表性的token压缩方法与结构剪枝方法；并进一步设计了一种"先剪枝后合并"（prune-then-merge）的组合策略。

Result: Token压缩在温和压缩下效果显著，但压缩率过大时性能急剧下降，对应激进token缩减导致的信息严重损失；结构剪枝则退化曲线更平滑，在高压缩下更稳定；组合策略在高压缩下实现了更优的精度-鲁棒性权衡。

Conclusion: 对于面向部署的ViT语义分割，在适度的剪枝骨干基础上施加适度的token压缩，可以在高计算压缩率下获得更好的精度-鲁棒性折衷，是一种实用的组合方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are strong backbones for semantic segmentation, but their computational cost limits deployment. Recent token compression methods for efficient transformer-based segmentation reduce this cost by decreasing the number of tokens. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on low-to-moderate compression, leaving their behavior under aggressive compression and corrupted inputs unclear. Meanwhile, structural pruning provides an orthogonal route to efficiency by removing redundant components in the ViT architecture, but is rarely compared to token compression under a unified protocol. To bridge this gap, we benchmark representative token compression and structural pruning methods for ViT-based semantic segmentation under matched FLOPs on ADE20K and Cityscapes, together with their common-corruption variants ADE20K-C and Cityscapes-C. Our results reveal a consistent trend on both clean and corrupted inputs: token compression is highly effective at mild reductions but degrades sharply when compression becomes severe, consistent with substantial information loss from overly aggressive token reduction. In contrast, structural pruning exhibits a smoother degradation curve and is more stable at high compression. Motivated by these findings, we study a prune-then-merge pipeline that applies moderate token compression on top of a moderately pruned backbone. At comparable FLOPs, this combined strategy consistently achieves a better accuracy-robustness trade-off at high compression, offering a practical recipe for deployment-oriented ViT segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/phatnguyencs/vit-seg-compression.

</details>


### [181] [ArcAD: Anomaly-Rectified Calibration for Cold-Start Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02252)
*Ningning Han,Lei Fan,Jia Guo,Yunkang Cao,Xiu Su,Feng Cao,Donglin Di,Tonghua Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ArcAD，一种即插即用的校准框架，用于解决工业异常检测中的冷启动瓶颈（少量正常样本和极少量异常样本）。通过推-拉学习范式：将正常样本投影到超球面并聚为多个紧致簇（拉），同时合成伪异常并利用真实异常向内推决策边界，显著提升数据稀缺下的异常检测性能。在MVTec-AD、VisA、Real-IAD、MANTA上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测在实际部署中面临冷启动瓶颈：有限的正常样本无法代表完整正态分布，且仅有极少量异常样本可用。现有方法难以形成紧致的正常边界，也无法有效利用罕见缺陷的监督信号。

Method: 提出ArcAD，即插即用的校准框架，基于推-拉学习范式：（1）将有限正常样本投影到超球面，拉向多个紧致簇以最大化覆盖正常流形；（2）在超球面上合成伪异常，并利用真实异常向内推决策边界，增强异常判别能力。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA、Real-IAD、MANTA四个数据集上，冷启动条件下单类和多类设置中均显著优于现有监督和无监督SOTA方法。

Conclusion: ArcAD有效解决了冷启动工业异常检测中数据稀缺问题，通过推-拉范式构建紧致且具判别性的正常边界，是一种通用且高效的即插即用解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) in real-world manufacturing frequently encounters a challenging cold-start bottleneck, in which limited normal samples fail to represent the full normal distribution and only a few anomalies are available. Under such a regime, existing methods struggle to form compact normal boundaries and fail to effectively exploit supervised signals from rare defects. To address this challenge, we propose Anomaly-Rectified Cold-start AD (ArcAD), a plug-and-play calibration framework for reconstruction-based IAD baselines. ArcAD follows a push-pull learning paradigm to construct a compact and discriminative normal boundary under data scarcity. On the one hand, ArcAD projects limited normal samples onto a hypersphere and pulls them into multiple compact clusters to maximize coverage of the normal manifold. On the other hand, it synthesizes pseudo-anomalies on the hypersphere and leverages real anomalies to push the boundary inward and sharpen anomaly discrimination. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD, VisA, Real-IAD, and MANTA demonstrate that ArcAD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and unsupervised methods in both single-class and multi-class settings under cold-start conditions. Code is available at: https://github.com/LGC-AD/ArcAD.

</details>


### [182] [AnyGroundBench: A Specialized-Domain Benchmark for Video Grounding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02269)
*Rintaro Otsubo,Ryo Fujii,Reina Ishikawa,Taiki Kanaya,Kanta Sawafuji,Hiroki Kajita,Shigeki Sakai,Hideo Saito,Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AnyGroundBench基准，将STVG评估范式从静态零样本测试转向严格的域适应评估，覆盖5个专业领域，揭示现有VLM在专业领域中零样本和上下文学习适应的严重失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前STVG评估局限于通用日常场景的零样本测试，与实际应用（专业领域中的罕见视觉概念和复杂时空动态）严重脱节；由于无法穷举预训练，模型必须具备域适应能力，但缺乏相应的评估基准。

Method: 构建AnyGroundBench基准，涵盖动物、工业、体育、手术和公共安全5个专业领域，将新拍摄视频（如专家标注的小鼠行为）与已有数据集配对，通过密集高保真时空标注统一它们，并提供专用训练子集以系统测量域适应能力。

Result: 评估15个SOTA VLM的零样本泛化和上下文学习能力，发现当前模型在专业领域中的零样本和基于ICL的适应均失败，暴露出时空推理的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在专业领域的时空推理上存在根本性缺陷，亟需未来研究解决；AnyGroundBench为评估和推动域适应能力提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated immense promise in Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG). However, current evaluation protocols are largely confined to zero-shot assessments on general, daily-life benchmarks. This creates a critical disconnect from real-world applications in specialized fields, where models inevitably encounter rare visual concepts and complex spatio-temporal dynamics. Since exhaustive pre-training across infinite data distributions is infeasible, the ability to adapt to novel domains is essential. To bridge this gap, we introduce AnyGroundBench, a domain-adaptation benchmark designed to shift the STVG evaluation paradigm from static zero-shot testing to rigorous domain adaptation. Targeting five specialized domains (animal, industry, sports, surgery, and public security), AnyGroundBench pairs newly captured videos such as expert-annotated mouse behaviors with established datasets, unifying them through dense, high-fidelity spatio-temporal annotations. Crucially, the benchmark provides dedicated training subsets to systematically measure domain adaptability. We extensively evaluate 15 state-of-the-art VLMs, assessing their zero-shot generalization and In-Context Learning (ICL) capabilities under practical computational constraints. Ultimately, our findings reveal that current models fail in both zero-shot and ICL-based adaptation when confronted with specialized domains, exposing critical flaws in spatio-temporal reasoning that future research must address.

</details>


### [183] [AGVBench: A Reliability-Oriented Benchmark of Data Augmentation for Vein Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02271)
*Haiyang Li,Yuming Fu,Qun Song,Hongchao Liao,Jing Chen,Mounim A. EI-Yacoubi,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGVBench是一个评估30种数据增强策略在静脉识别（手掌和手指静脉）中效果的基准平台，涵盖7种骨干网络。研究发现多图像混合方法（如MixUp）识别性能最佳，但校准差且易受对抗攻击；严重几何变换会降低识别效果。结论指出仅关注准确率的评估不足以衡量生物特征增强的实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静脉识别常受限于标注数据不足和成像变化，现有数据增强策略多针对自然图像设计，可能破坏静脉识别所需的细粒度拓扑结构和纹理特征，缺乏针对静脉识别的系统评估基准。

Method: 在5个公开手掌和手指静脉数据集上，使用7种骨干网络（包括经典CNN、视觉Transformer和静脉专用识别模型），系统评估30种代表性数据增强策略。

Result: 多图像混合方法（MixUp、PuzzleMix、StarMixup）识别性能最优，但校准性差且易受对抗扰动攻击；严重几何变换因特征错位或空间裁剪导致识别性能下降；增强效果在不同静脉数据集上存在差异。

Conclusion: 仅以准确率为中心的评估不足以衡量生物特征增强的实际效果，AGVBench提供了标准化协议以支持可重复研究，并指导设计可靠、安全且鲁棒的静脉识别系统。

Abstract: Vein recognition is a secure biometric technology often constrained by limited annotated data and imaging variations. While data augmentation mitigates this, strategies designed for natural images may disrupt the fine-grained topology and textures essential for identity discrimination. We present AGVBench, which evaluates 30 representative augmentation strategies on five public palm- and finger-vein datasets with seven backbone architectures, covering classic CNNs, vision transformers, and vein-specific recognition models. Our results show that multi-image mixing methods (e.g., MixUp, PuzzleMix, StarMixup) generally provide the strongest recognition performance. However, they are often poorly calibrated and vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, revealing a clear inconsistency between clean accuracy and adversarial security. We also find that severe geometric transformations frequently degrade recognition, which is potentially due to feature misalignment or spatial cropping, and that augmentation effectiveness varies across palm and finger vein datasets. These findings prove that accuracy-centric evaluation is insufficient for biometric augmentation. AGVBench provides standardized protocols to support reproducible research and guide the design of reliable, secure, and robust vein recognition systems. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/Advance-VeinTech-Innovators/AGVBench.

</details>


### [184] [FlowCIR: Semantic Transport via Flow Matching for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02284)
*Zhenqi He,Ziqi Jiang,Yuanpei Liu,Yanghao Wang,Teng Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowCIR提出了一种基于条件流匹配的新范式，将零样本组合图像检索视为参考图像与目标嵌入之间的条件语义传输。该方法仅训练一个小型传输模块，无需更新视觉语言模型编码器，相比基于文本反转的方法节省约10倍训练资源。同时提出Multi-Negative Steering策略，缓解VLM在否定/移除语义上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZS-CIR方法依赖文本反转将参考图像转换为伪文本标记，再与指令简单拼接，这种方式在细粒度语义上存在信息损失且脆弱。

Method: 1) 将ZS-CIR重构为条件语义传输问题，利用条件流匹配学习一个轻量传输场，将指令表示映射为目标对齐的查询嵌入（以参考图像为条件）。2) 在预提取的VLM嵌入上操作，仅训练小型传输模块，不更新图像或文本编码器。3) 提出推理阶段的Multi-Negative Steering策略，引导含否定词的指令远离被否定的语义。

Result: 在标准CIR基准上取得有竞争力且稳健的性能，训练资源约为先前文本反转方法的1/10。

Conclusion: FlowCIR通过条件流匹配实现了高效、鲁棒的零样本组合图像检索，尤其通过Multi-Negative Steering弥补了VLM在否定语义处理上的短板。

Abstract: Zero-shot composed image retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image by editing a reference image with a natural-language instruction, without relying on domain-specific annotated triplets. Most existing ZS-CIR methods rely on textual inversion to translate the reference image into pseudo-text tokens and then compose them with the instruction via simple concatenation in the text space, which can be lossy and brittle for fine-grained semantics. In this work, we propose a new paradigm, namely FlowCIR, that casts ZS-CIR as conditional semantic transport between reference and target embeddings. Leveraging \emph{conditional flow matching}, our model learns a lightweight transport field that maps the instruction representation toward a target-aligned query embedding conditioned on the reference image. Since FlowCIR operates on pre-extracted VLM embeddings and trains only a small transport module without updating the image or text encoder, it offers a computationally efficient training protocol compared with prior textual-inversion-based approaches. The resulting framework is training-efficient, requiring roughly $10\times$ fewer training resources than prior textual-inversion-based approaches. We further identify negation and removal as a major failure mode of VLM-based composition. To address this, we propose an inference-only Multi-Negative Steering strategy that steers a negation-containing relative instruction away from its negated semantics, mitigating the limited negation handling of VLMs and improving robustness on negation-heavy queries. Extensive experiments on standard CIR benchmarks demonstrate that FlowCIR achieves strong and competitive performance compared with recent ZS-CIR methods.

</details>


### [185] [DisciplineGen-1M: A Large-Scale Dataset for Multidisciplinary Visual Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02290)
*Zhaokai Wang,Mingxin Liu,Zirun Zhu,Ziqian Fan,Yiguo He,Mohan Zhang,Leyao Gu,Xiangyu Zhao,Ning Liao,Shaofeng Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Junchi Yan,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DisciplineGen-1M，一个包含120万样本、覆盖10个学科的大规模多学科数据集，并基于此开发了学科感知的推理-生成模型，在知识密集型图表生成和编辑任务上显著优于现有方法，推动了图像生成从美学合理性向可验证的知识驱动视觉创作转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成和编辑模型在生成自然图像时表现良好，但在处理知识密集型图表（如学科概念、符号结构和精确空间关系）时可靠性不足，缺乏大规模、高质量的多学科数据集来支持该领域的研究。

Method: 构建了包含120万样本、覆盖10个学科的DisciplineGen-1M数据集，采用可扩展框架结合矢量图形渲染、基于OCR的编辑、程序化合成和大规模文本到图像筛选等多种管道生成数据。在此基础上提出学科感知的推理-生成模型，用于文本到图像生成和图像编辑。

Result: 在学科相关基准GenExam和GRADE上相较于开源基线有显著提升，在通用推理基准WISE和RISE上也表现出更广泛的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 大规模结构化学术视觉数据是推动图像生成从美学合理性迈向可验证知识驱动视觉创作的关键。数据集、模型和代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Recent image generation and editing models can produce visually appealing natural images, yet they remain unreliable when the target image is a knowledge-intensive diagram whose correctness depends on disciplinary concepts, symbolic structure, and precise spatial relations. We introduce DisciplineGen-1M, a million-scale multidisciplinary dataset that supports text-to-image generation and image editing. It contains 1.2M samples spanning mathematics, physics, chemistry, biology, geography, computer science, economics, history, music, and sports. To construct the dataset, we design a scalable framework that combines vector-graphics rendering, OCR-based editing, curated programmatic synthesis, and large-scale text-to-image filtering. These pipelines produce captions, editing instructions, structured annotations, and paired images with controllable semantic differences. Building on DisciplineGen-1M, we further introduce a discipline-informed reasoning-generation model for both text-to-image generation and image editing. Experiments on discipline-related benchmarks, GenExam and GRADE, show substantial improvements over open-source baselines, while evaluations on general reasoning-informed benchmarks, WISE and RISE, further indicate broader transfer. The results suggest that large-scale structured academic visual data is a key ingredient for moving image generation from aesthetic plausibility toward verifiable knowledge-grounded visual creation. We will publicly release our dataset, model, and source code of the data curation pipeline to ensure reproducibility and benefit future research.

</details>


### [186] [Dual-Selective Network for Domain-Incremental Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02299)
*Yuzhi He,Junxi Huang,Haorui Wu,Jiahui Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSINet提出了一种基于状态空间模型的双选择性增量网络，通过选择性空间状态单元和浓度平衡蒸馏策略，在领域增量变化检测中保持空间表征的稳定性，有效缓解长领域序列中的知识退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域增量变化检测中存在结构不匹配问题：标签空间固定而领域特征剧烈变化，现有重放和正则化方法难以扩展到长领域序列，导致知识退化或计算成本增加。

Method: 提出DSINet框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 选择性空间状态单元(S3U)，利用Mamba的输入依赖选择机制，在特征传播中保留稳定空间变化结构同时过滤领域特异性变化；(2) 浓度平衡蒸馏(CBD)策略，平衡硬度和置信度浓度效应，防止蒸馏过程中的过平滑或模式崩溃。

Result: 实验表明DSINet能在长领域序列中有效缓解知识退化，同时保持状态空间模型的线性计算效率。

Conclusion: DSINet通过选择性状态空间建模和平衡蒸馏机制，为领域增量变化检测提供了一种可扩展且计算高效的统一解决方案，在长序列场景下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Domain-incremental change detection (DICD) continuously adapts models to new geographic domains while preserving prior knowledge. However, a structural mismatch exists: the label space remains fixed while domain characteristics vary drastically. Consequently, incremental models struggle to maintain stable spatial change representations across domains. Existing strategies, such as replay-based or regularization-based methods, often fail to scale to long domain sequences, leading to knowledge degradation or increased computational cost. We propose Dual-Selective Incremental Network (DSINet), a unified framework built on visual state space models. DSINet leverages Mamba's input-dependent selective mechanism through a selective spatial state unit (S3U). This unit preserves stable spatial change structures while filtering domain-specific variations during feature propagation. As a result, spatial representations remain stable across domains, preventing the accumulation of feature confusion over incremental steps. Additionally, we employ a concentration-balanced distillation (CBD) strategy to stabilize knowledge transfer across domains. It balances hardness and confidence concentration effects during incremental updates. This ensures reliable probability mass allocation and prevents over-smoothing or mode collapse during distillation. Together, these mechanisms maintain stable learning dynamics throughout incremental stages. Experimental results demonstrate that DSINet mitigates knowledge degradation across long domain sequences while maintaining the linear computational efficiency of state space models.

</details>


### [187] [Search-based Testing of Vision Language Models for In-Car Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02300)
*Lev Sorokin,Chen Yang,Ken E. Friedl,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ISU-Test，一种结合渲染场景生成与搜索测试的自动化测试方法，用于系统评估车载场景理解（ISU）系统中的视觉语言模型（VLM）。该方法将测试视为优化问题，通过系统修改场景参数生成多样化车内场景。在工业原型和开源VLM上的问答与字幕生成两项案例研究中，相比随机场景生成，ISU-Test实现了高达10倍的故障发现率和3.6倍的故障覆盖率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载场景理解（ISU）系统依赖视觉语言模型（VLM）解释摄像头记录的车内场景，但VLM可能生成不完整、错误或误导性描述。真实车内数据采集成本高、难以规模化且在早期设计阶段不可行，因此需要一种自动化、可扩展的系统化测试方法。

Method: 提出ISU-Test框架，将测试形式化为优化问题，结合基于渲染的场景生成与搜索测试（search-based testing），通过系统修改场景参数（如光照、姿态、物体位置等）自动生成多样化车内场景配置，用于评估VLM在不同场景下的表现。

Result: 在工业原型和开源VLM上，针对问答和字幕生成两项任务，ISU-Test相比随机场景生成基线，故障发现率最高提升10倍，故障覆盖率最高提升3.6倍。

Conclusion: ISU-Test是一种有效的自动化测试方法，能够系统性地暴露VLM在车内场景理解中的缺陷，且优于随机基线方法。

Abstract: In the automotive domain, in-car scene understanding (ISU) enables the detection of safety-critical events, such as driver distraction, and supports drivers or passengers by analyzing the in-car scene and adapting the environment (e.g., ambient lighting). The industry is increasingly exploring vision-language models (VLMs) to interpret camera-recorded in-car scenes and extract information for downstream reasoning tasks. However, VLMs may generate incomplete, erroneous, or misleading scene descriptions, highlighting the need for systematic testing. Collecting real in-vehicle data is costly, difficult to scale, and often infeasible, particularly in early design stages. In this paper, we present ISU-Test, an automated testing approach that combines rendering-based scene generation with search-based testing to evaluate ISU systems. By framing testing as an optimization problem and systematically modifying scene parameters, our method generates diverse in-car scenarios and explores a wide range of configurations. We evaluate ISU-Test on both an industrial prototype and open-source VLMs across two case studies: question answering and captioning, comparing against randomized scenario generation. Results show that ISU-Test significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving up to 10 times higher failure rates and up to 3.6 times higher failure coverage.

</details>


### [188] [InvSplat: Inverse Feed-Forward Scene Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02301)
*Polina Karpikova,Wenjing Bian,Haofei Xu,Hendrik Lensch,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InvSplat，一种前馈多视图逆渲染框架，可直接预测带有材质属性的结构化3D高斯表示，实现几何与反射参数的单次前向联合预测，支持重光照和新视图合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆渲染方法中，基于优化的方法需要逐场景拟合，成本高昂；基于图像空间学习的方法存在多视图不一致且缺乏显式3D表示，导致新视图渲染不稳定。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出前馈多视图重建框架，将每个高斯基元参数化为均值、法向、不透明度、旋转、尺度、反照率、金属度和粗糙度，融合材质估计网络先验与多视图3D重建骨干，单次前向预测几何与反射参数。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，相比2D基线方法，多视图一致性提升，材质恢复更准确，新视图渲染更稳定，且支持基于物理的重光照和更逼真的视相关效应建模。

Conclusion: 提出的结构化3D高斯表示与前馈框架有效结合了逆渲染的多视图一致性与物理可解释性，在重光照和新视图合成任务上优于现有RGB前馈重建方法。

Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to recover both 3D geometry and physically meaningful material properties from images, enabling applications such as relighting and novel view synthesis. Optimization-based methods achieve high fidelity but require costly per-scene fitting, while image-space learning-based approaches often suffer from multi-view inconsistencies and lack an explicit 3D representation for stable novel view rendering. We present a feed-forward multi-view reconstruction framework for inverse rendering that directly predicts a structured 3D Gaussian representation with intrinsic material attributes. Each Gaussian primitive is parameterized by mean, normal, opacity, rotation, scale, albedo, metallic, and roughness, enabling a disentangled and physically grounded scene representation. Our model integrates priors from a material estimation network with a multi-view 3D reconstruction backbone, allowing joint prediction of geometry and reflectance parameters in a single forward pass. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate improved multi-view consistency compared to 2D baselines, accurate material recovery, and stable novel view rendering. Our representation further supports physically-based relighting and more faithful modeling of view-dependent effects compared to existing RGB-based feed-forward reconstruction methods. Our project webpage is: $\href{https://poliik.github.io/invsplat/}{\text{https://poliik.github.io/invsplat/}}$.

</details>


### [189] [NEvo: Neural-Guided Evolutionary Video Synthesis for Dynamic Visual Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02317)
*Yingtian Tang,Sogand Salehi,Ming Zhou,Amir Zamir,Leyla Isik,Martin Schrimpf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经引导的视频合成框架，通过进化搜索结构化提示空间，结合动态脑编码模型，自动生成针对视觉皮层特定脑区的最优动态刺激视频。合成的超激活视频在腹侧、背侧和外侧通路中恢复了已知的选择性，并揭示了对外侧流中社会-动态特征的敏感性差异，为动态视觉加工提供了可验证的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大脑编码模型主要局限于静态图像，而视觉输入本质上是动态的，动态视觉加工通路（如背侧、外侧流）的选择性尚未被充分探索。需要一个能够生成动态刺激的框架来系统性地探究不同脑区的动态视觉选择性。

Method: 提出神经引导视频合成框架：(1) 构建动态编码模型，预测体素级对视频输入的响应；(2) 在结构化提示空间中进行进化搜索，最大化目标ROI的预测活动；(3) 生成超激活的动态刺激视频，并通过搜索光分析探索沿外侧流的特征进展。

Result: 合成视频在腹侧、背侧和外侧通路中恢复了已知的选择性，超越了手工制作的定位视频；揭示了沿外侧流对社会-动态特征敏感性的系统性差异；通过合成的抽象非自然刺激进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了对动态视觉选择性的计算机内探索，为未来的活体实验提供了新的可验证预测，弥补了静态图像研究向动态视觉加工扩展的空白。

Abstract: The human brain processes dynamic visual input through hierarchically organized, functionally specialized regions. While recent in silico brain encoding models can synthesize optimal stimuli to probe selectivity in different brain regions, prior work has been largely limited to static images, leaving dynamic visual processing underexplored. We introduce a novel neural-guided video synthesis framework that generates stimuli optimized for target brain regions across visual cortex. Our method performs evolutionary search over a structured prompt space, guided by a dynamic encoding model that predicts voxel-level responses to video inputs. By maximizing predicted activity for a target ROI, the framework efficiently discovers hyper-activating dynamic stimuli that consistently surpass handcrafted localizer videos. The synthesized videos recover known selectivities across ventral, dorsal, and lateral pathways, and further reveal systematic differences in sensitivity to temporal dynamics. A searchlight analysis provides new insight into the progression toward increasingly complex social-dynamic features along the lateral stream, further supported by probing with synthesized abstract, non-naturalistic stimuli. Taken together, our framework enables in silico exploration of dynamic visual selectivity, with new predictions for in vivo experiments

</details>


### [190] [GAP-GDRNet: Geometry-Aware Monocular Visual Pose Sensing on a Single-Target Synthetic Spacecraft Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02360)
*Yonglong Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GAP-GDRNet是一种基于注意力增强的几何感知框架，用于单目RGB图像中非合作航天器的6D位姿估计，通过引入注意力特征细化模块和补丁级几何自注意力模块，解决了弱纹理、薄附件和遮挡等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非合作交会和在轨服务中，单目相对位姿感知是核心问题。然而航天器图像存在弱表面纹理、薄附件、光照变化和部分遮挡等问题，导致几何特征稀疏且不稳定，现有方法难以有效处理。

Method: 基于GDR-Net的几何引导直接回归范式，进行两点改进：1) 在密集几何预测前插入基于注意力的特征细化(AFR)模块，增强全局航天器结构和局部弱纹理线索；2) 在Patch-PnP中插入补丁级几何自注意力(PGSA)模块，关联下采样后的几何补丁。使用Blender生成目标掩码、可见区域掩码、密集模型坐标图、相机内参和6D位姿标签进行监督训练。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体数值结果，但提出该方法能有效应对弱纹理、薄附件、光照变化和遮挡等挑战。

Conclusion: GAP-GDRNet通过注意力机制增强了GDR-Net的几何感知能力，为单目非合作航天器位姿估计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Monocular relative pose sensing is a central perception problem in non-cooperative rendezvous and on-orbit servicing. In spacecraft images, however, weak surface texture, thin appendages, illumination changes, and partial occlusion often leave only sparse and unstable geometric evidence. This article presents GAP-GDRNet, a geometry-aware attention-enhanced framework for monocular RGB-based 6D pose sensing. The method follows the geometry-guided direct regression paradigm of GDR-Net and modifies two points in the pipeline: an attention-based feature refinement (AFR) module is placed before dense geometric prediction, and a patch-level geometric self-attention (PGSA) module is inserted into Patch-PnP. AFR reinforces global spacecraft structure together with local weak-texture cues; PGSA then relates downsampled geometric patches before final pose regression. A Blender-based annotation process supplies target masks, visible-region masks, dense model-coordinate maps, camera intrinsics, and 6D pose labels for supervised training.

</details>


### [191] [VisionAId: An Offline-First Multimodal Android Assistant for People with Visual Impairment, Featuring Personalized Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02371)
*Cristian-Gabriel Florea,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionAId是一款Android应用，将普通智能手机变为实时视觉助手，通过6个端侧深度学习模型（单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉/面部嵌入、人脸检测、自定义纸币检测器）在ONNX Runtime上运行，并可选云端大语言模型进行场景描述。其核心创新是少样本个人物品追踪管道，支持用户通过拍照注册个人物品，随后系统通过AR标记、空间音频和距离触觉反馈引导用户找到该物品。在Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra上，INT8量化将深度估计延迟从~1200ms降至~491ms，纸币检测器mAP@50达0.986，3米内深度误差<1cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过2.85亿视障人士在日常生活中面临行动障碍、物品寻找、人脸识别和现金处理等困难。现有辅助应用受限于预定义类别、依赖云端连接或需要专用硬件，缺乏一个集成化、实时、端侧运行的通用视觉辅助系统。

Method: 开发Android应用VisionAId，集成6个ONNX Runtime端侧深度学习模型：度量单目深度估计、实例分割、视觉嵌入、面部嵌入、人脸检测和自定义纸币检测器。核心创新是少样本个人物品管道：用户多角度拍照注册后，系统通过视觉嵌入匹配定位该实例，结合AR标记、空间音频和距离比例触觉反馈引导用户。可选云端LLM用于场景描述和自动标注。所有反馈支持罗马尼亚语语音合成、语音命令和振动。

Result: 在Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra上：INT8量化将深度估计延迟从~1200ms降至~491ms；自定义纸币检测器mAP@50达0.986；度量深度在3米内校准误差<1cm。

Conclusion: VisionAId展示了将多种端侧深度学习模型集成到普通智能手机中实现实时视觉辅助的可行性，尤其在少样本个人物品定位和低延迟深度估计方面取得显著效果，为视障人士提供了无需专用硬件、低依赖云端的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Over 285 million people worldwide live with a visual impairment, for whom everyday tasks such as avoiding obstacles, locating personal belongings, recognizing familiar faces, or handling cash remain persistent obstacles to personal autonomy. Existing assistive applications are typically limited to recognizing predefined categories, depend heavily on cloud connectivity, or require dedicated hardware. We present VisionAId, an Android application that turns a commodity smartphone into a real-time visual assistant. The system integrates six on-device deep learning models (metric monocular depth estimation, instance segmentation, visual and facial embeddings, face detection, and a custom banknote detector) running entirely through ONNX Runtime, with an optional cloud large language model (Google Gemini Flash) used only for narrative scene description and automatic object labeling. A distinctive contribution is a few-shot pipeline for personal objects: the user photographs an object from several angles, and the system later locates that specific instance in the environment, guiding the user toward it with augmented-reality markers, spatial audio, and distance-proportional haptics. All feedback is multimodal (Romanian speech synthesis, voice commands, vibration). On a reference device (Samsung Galaxy S21 Ultra), INT8 quantization reduces depth latency from ~1200 ms to ~491 ms, the custom banknote detector reaches an mAP@50 of 0.986, and metric depth is calibrated to below 1 cm of error within 3 m.

</details>


### [192] [Learning Spectral and Polarimetric Clues for One-to-Multimodal Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02372)
*Federico Lincetto,Gianluca Agresti,Mattia Rossi,Piergiorgio Sartor,Pietro Zanuttigh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPoILeR方法，通过多模态预训练学习不同模态间的相关性，仅用RGB图像即可在微调阶段预测红外、偏振、多光谱等非传统模态的多视图一致渲染，无需昂贵传感器和标定设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染技术虽能扩展到多光谱、红外或偏振等成像模态，但都需要昂贵的传感器和标定设置来捕获每个新场景的多模态数据。作者旨在降低对特殊传感器的依赖，使仅基于RGB图像也能生成高质量的非常规模态渲染。

Method: 提出SPoILeR方法，包含两个阶段：1)多模态预训练阶段，学习不同模态之间的相互相关性；2)微调阶段，仅以RGB图像作为监督信号，预测非常规模态的多视图一致渲染。模型利用预训练学到的模态间关联实现跨模态推理。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够在未提供任何红外、偏振或多光谱传感器输入样本的场景中，准确渲染出这些模态的帧。

Conclusion: SPoILeR成功实现了仅依赖RGB图像即可预测非常规成像模态的渲染，显著降低了对特殊硬件和标定过程的需求，拓展了神经渲染在多种成像模态下的应用可能性。

Abstract: Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.

</details>


### [193] [Representation Distribution Matching for One-Step Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02375)
*Lan Feng,Wuyang Li,Eloi Zablocki,Matthieu Cord,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了表示分布匹配（RDM）范式，通过优化分布比较方式和表示空间两个设计轴，提出改进型RDM（iRDM），在ImageNet上达到单步生成SOTA，并能将FLUX.2四步模型后训练为单步生成器，性能超越原版。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并优化表示分布匹配（RDM）这一单步图像生成范式的设计空间，解决经典MMD在十年前无法训练令人信服的生成器、单一表示易被攻击（图像虽假但分布分数正常）等核心问题。

Method: (1) 采用MMD作为分布匹配目标，但通过精确估计使其可扩展；(2) 发现生成批次大小是关键变量，最优值超过2048；(3) 使用平衡的编码器组合（14个编码器）进行匹配，并用SW_r14（基于14个编码器的Sliced-Wasserstein距离）作为独立评估指标，抵抗单一表示被"欺骗"的问题。

Result: iRDM在ImageNet上SW_r14达1.30，实现单步SOTA；PickScore（未优化的代理指标）在71.2%的匹配样本中偏好iRDM；后训练FLUX.2为单步生成器，GenEval 0.826（vs 四步0.794），PickScore 22.76（vs 四步22.58），仅用90 H200 GPU小时。

Conclusion: RDM设计空间的系统优化显著提升了单步生成质量，证明了分布匹配方法在大规模生成任务中的有效性，且训练高效、可迁移至已有扩散模型。

Abstract: We elucidate the design space of Representation Distribution Matching (RDM), our name for the paradigm that trains a one-step image generator by matching generated and reference feature distributions under frozen pretrained encoders. We identify two design axes, how the distributions are compared and the representations they are compared in, and controlled studies along them yield three findings. First, the classical MMD, which could not train convincing generators a decade ago, becomes a strong and scalable objective once estimated right. Second, the generated batch is then the operative variable, with an optimum above 2048, far beyond customary batch sizes. Third, any single representation can be gamed, driven below the real score while images stay visibly fake, so we match against a balanced battery of encoders and evaluate with SW_r14, a Sliced-Wasserstein distance over 14 encoders that is independent of the training loss and resists gaming. Combining the preferred choices yields improved RDM (iRDM): it sets the one-step state of the art on ImageNet at SW_r14 1.30, corroborated by PickScore, a human-preference proxy our objective never optimizes, which prefers it over the prior best one-step generator on 71.2% of matched samples. The same recipe post-trains the four-step FLUX.2 [klein] into a one-step generator, surpassing the four-step version on GenEval, 0.826 to 0.794, and on PickScore, 22.76 to 22.58, in 90 H200 GPU-hours. Project page: https://alan-lanfeng.github.io/rdm/.

</details>


### [194] [Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02402)
*Nick Stracke,Kolja Bauer,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Miguel Angel Bautista,Josh Susskind,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对VLM难以从图像集合中推断视觉概念的问题，提出VICIS任务及相应的训练框架和架构，使模型能从示例图像集中提取共享概念并应用于新查询，在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet上实验证明生成更准确多样，且能泛化到未见概念和草图模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM虽能遵循复杂文本指令，但难以纯粹从视觉上下文中推理，尤其是无法从示例图像集中推断共享概念并应用于新输入。

Method: 提出VICIS任务（从集合中进行视觉概念推断），设计训练框架和架构，学习从图像集中推断视觉概念，并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入。

Result: 在合成数据和ImageNet/WordNet大规模数据上，模型生成更准确、更多样的输出，并能泛化到未见概念和草图等模态。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了VLM在视觉概念推理上的不足，实现了从示例集合中学习并应用视觉概念到新输入的能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.

</details>


### [195] [Wavelet-Guided Semantic Signal Compensation for Inversion-Free Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02421)
*Anqi Tang,Wenhao Sun,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对FlowEdit等免反转图像编辑框架在早期高噪声阶段编辑轨迹受数据流形主导、导致全局属性修改受限的问题，提出一种免反转的频率感知语义补偿策略，在生成早期增强有效信号的同时保持背景结构一致性，从而在不牺牲背景保真度的前提下提升全局编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免反转编辑框架（如FlowEdit）在全局属性偏移场景下，早期高噪声阶段的主导流形趋向会削弱文本条件方向的影响，导致全局修改不足而背景结构仅中等程度保留。

Method: 提出一种免反转的频率感知语义补偿策略，在生成早期阶段增强有效信号，同时维持背景结构一致性。

Result: 所提方法在不牺牲背景保真度的前提下，提升了全局编辑能力。

Conclusion: 频率感知的语义补偿策略有效解决了免反转编辑框架在全局属性修改上的局限性，在保持背景的同时实现了更强的编辑效果。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing aims to modify visual content according to a target prompt while preserving the background. Recent inversion-free image editing frameworks such as FlowEdit have demonstrated strong editing capability without requiring inversion. Empirically, FlowEdit can achieve substantial semantic changes under appropriate hyperparameter settings. However, we observe that under certain global attribute shifts, the editing trajectory may not effectively move away from the source distribution in the early timesteps. Our analysis suggests that in the high-noise regime, the dominant manifold-seeking flow toward the data manifold can reduce the influence of the text-conditioned direction, leading to limited global modification while background structures remain only moderately preserved. Inspired by this observation, we propose an inversion-free, frequency-aware semantic compensation strategy that strengthens the effective signal in the early stage of generation, while maintaining structural consistency in the background. The proposed method improves global editing capacity without sacrificing background fidelity.

</details>


### [196] [Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02425)
*Francesca Pistilli,Simone Alberto Peirone,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SG-Ego，一个基于Ego4D的大规模时空场景图标注集，以及GLEN模型，用于对场景图序列进行推理，实现活动驱动的图编辑预测（A-GEF），从而显式建模人-环境交互的时序变化。实验表明该方法在多项基准上优于视频基线，尤其在推理任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式视觉或语言对齐表示，缺乏对场景动态的结构化推理。本文认为显式、可组合、可编辑的人-环境交互表示对于丰富的具身活动理解至关重要。

Method: 1) 构建SG-Ego，在Ego4D上扩展时空场景图标注，将关系三元组随时间整合为显式的场景状态描述。2) 提出GLEN，一个基于图的模型，操作场景图序列以对齐文本动作并建模时序演化。3) 提出活动驱动图编辑预测（A-GEF）任务，将场景动态建模为以动作为条件的结构化变换序列。

Result: GLEN在EgoMCQ、EgoCVR、EXPLORE-Bench及新提出的A-GEF基准上均取得强结果，尤其在推理场景中超越了视频基线，达到与仅用MLLMs才能处理的推理能力相当的水平，同时支持可控和结构化的场景动态预测。

Conclusion: 时空场景图及其推理模型是视频理解中强有力且可组合、可解释的表示方法，其潜力可能超越视频领域。

Abstract: Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.

</details>


### [197] [MARVEL: Margin-Aware Robust von Mises-Fischer Expert Learning for Long-Tailed Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02435)
*A. S. Anudeep,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MARVEL框架，针对医学影像中类别不平衡和分布外检测难题，通过非线性von Mises-Fisher分类器、多专家分工和异常值专家三个组件，在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTC-RC数据集上分别将FPR95降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法多在平衡数据集上评估，使用粗粒度或非临床的OOD来源，缺乏对医学真实场景中类别不平衡和多样OOD情况的全面评估，导致深度模型在临床部署中面对未见样本时仍以高置信度错误分类。

Method: 提出包含三个组件的MARVEL框架：(1) 非线性von Mises-Fisher(NvMF)分类器，可学习非线性决策边界，并理论上证明其与余弦分类器的渐近联系；(2) 多专家框架，让边界感知的NvMF分类器各自专攻标签分布的不同区域以处理不平衡；(3) 异常值专家，专门训练以区分内点和外点数据，强化OOD检测能力。

Result: 在RFMiD、ISIC2019和NCTC-RC三个医学数据集上，相比现有方法，平均FPR95分别降低8.45%、13.02%和36.90%，消融实验验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架能可靠识别不熟悉病例以便转诊给临床医生，在真实工作流中支持更安全的AI辅助诊断。

Abstract: For clinical deployment, it is essential that automated diagnostic systems remain reliable when confronted with previously unseen cases, yet deep models routinely misclassify out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs with high confidence, underscoring the need for more robust OOD detection methods. Although substantial effort has been devoted to improving model robustness, most of the existing literature assumes balanced datasets, evaluates OOD detection on coarse or non-clinical OOD sources, or lacks comprehensive assessment across diverse OOD scenarios. To address the gaps, we propose a novel methodology trained on diverse and imbalanced medical datasets and evaluated across a clinically reflective OOD spectrum. Our framework comprises three key components: (1) a Nonlinear von Mises-Fisher (NvMF) classifier capable of learning non-linear decision boundaries, with theoretical proof of its asymptotic connection to cosine classifiers; (2) a multi-expert framework in which margin-aware NvMF classifiers specialise in different regions of label distribution to better handle imbalance; and (3) an outlier expert trained explicitly to distinguish inlier from outlier data, thereby strengthening OOD detection. Evaluation on RFMiD, ISIC2019, and NCTCRC datasets demonstrates consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods, achieving mean FPR95 reductions of 8.45%, 13.02%, and 36.90% respectively. These gains are further supported by comprehensive ablations that validated the contributions of each component. This enables reliable identification of unfamiliar cases for deferral to clinicians, supporting safer AI-assisted diagnosis in real-world workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/redboxup/MARVEL.

</details>


### [198] [Interpretation-Oriented Cloud Removal via Observation-Anchored Residual Flow with Geo-Contextual Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02471)
*Ziyao Wang,Maonan Wang,Yucheng He,Xianping Ma,Ziyi Wang,Hongyang Zhang,Yirong Cheng,Man-on Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR)，一种联合保证云移除后影像重建质量和下游任务可解释性的统一框架，通过Observation-Anchored Residual Flow实现物理引导的残差逆重建，结合Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment保持语义完整性，在六个数据集和十二个下游任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云移除方法过分追求视觉真实感，忽视了其对后续语义分割、变化检测等下游分析任务的影响，导致语义漂移和下游性能退化。

Method: 提出GACR框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow)，将云移除建模为物理引导的残差逆过程，以含云观测而非纯噪声为锚点引导生成轨迹，实现快速稳定重建；(2) Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA)，利用视觉基础模型构建语义流形约束，保持重建影像的空间-语义完整性。

Result: 在六个云移除数据集和十二个下游任务上的大量实验表明，GACR在产生更优重建质量的同时，持续提升下游任务准确性。

Conclusion: GACR通过物理引导重建与语义先验约束的统一框架，有效解决了云移除中视觉真实感与下游可解释性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Cloud removal (CR) is essential for optical remote sensing, serving as a prerequisite for reliable downstream interpretation, such as semantic segmentation and change detection. However, existing CR approaches often prioritize visual realism while overlooking their impact on subsequent analytical tasks, leading to semantic drift and degraded downstream performance. To address this issue, we propose Geo-Anchored Cloud Removal (GACR), a unified framework that jointly ensures faithful reconstruction and robust interpretability. At its core, GACR incorporates Observation-Anchored Residual Flow (OAR-Flow), which reformulates CR as a physically grounded residual inversion process. By anchoring the generative trajectory to the cloudy observation rather than pure noise, OAR-Flow enables fast, stable, and faithful reconstruction. To further preserve semantic structures critical for downstream interpretation, GACR integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA) to constrain the reconstruction within a semantic manifold induced by a Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Consequently, GACR strictly maintains the spatial-semantic integrity of complex landscapes. Extensive experiments across six CR datasets and twelve downstream tasks demonstrate that GACR produces superior reconstruction quality while consistently improving downstream task accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR.

</details>


### [199] [EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02479)
*Jingtao Xu,Zizhuo Lin,Jianwen Sun,Yi Yang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对标准多模态大模型在360°全景主动视觉搜索中的不足（极坐标畸变、连续拓扑建模困难、局部视图碎片化导致的低效探索），本文提出EAGLE-360框架，通过全局到局部的探索范式、RoPE Rolling位置编码和SFT+GRPO训练策略，在14K+4K全景图和70K+VQA对话数据集上训练，实现了近8倍的准确率提升和更高的探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLLM无法有效建模全景的极坐标畸变和连续圆柱拓扑结构，导致目标检测精度显著下降；现有全景搜索方法依赖碎片化局部视角、初始化固定且缺乏全局先验，导致近视低效的探索和鲁棒性差的错误恢复能力。

Method: 提出EAGLE-360框架：(1) 全局到局部（Global-to-Local）的主动探索范式，利用全局先验建立初始全景视角，逐步迭代推理缩小搜索空间；(2) RoPE Rolling坐标偏移位置编码，无缝建模全景的连续拓扑结构；(3) 构建包含14,000+张4K全景图和70,000+轮高质量VQA对话的大规模数据集；(4) 采用SFT+GRPO的训练流程，激发复杂空间推理和工具调用能力。

Result: 在360°视觉搜索任务上取得了新的最优结果，相比基础模型准确率提升近8倍，同时显著提高了探索效率。

Conclusion: EAGLE-360通过全局先验引导的渐进式搜索、针对全景特性的位置编码改进以及专用的训练策略，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，为全景主动视觉搜索提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.

</details>


### [200] [Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02484)
*Xuehui Wang,Xuankun Yang,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EADP框架通过熵感知的文本噪声过滤和子模最大化令牌选择，在严格预算下保留细粒度视觉线索，实现视觉语言模型的最优加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌剪枝方法在密集指令和细粒度查询场景下无法保留关键线索，原因在于文本噪声污染跨模态评分以及标准令牌选择导致特征碎片化。

Method: 提出EADP框架：1) 利用统计熵量化并过滤文本噪声，生成鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性分数；2) 将令牌选择重构为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，确保整体且非冗余的视觉表示。

Result: 在严格令牌预算下鲁棒保留细粒度视觉线索，在多模态基准上达到SoTA性能，显著改善准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 将剪枝重构为结构化压缩问题，通过熵感知去噪和子模优化选择有效解决现有瓶颈，为VLM高效推理提供新范式。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [201] [GeoMix: Descriptor-Free Visual Localization via Global Context and Multi-Detector Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02486)
*Yejun Zhang,Xinjue Wang,Zihan Wang,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMix提出了一种无描述符的2D-3D匹配框架，通过局部方向与距离嵌入、全局上下文节点以及多检测器混合训练（Mix-Training）三层次增强几何判别性，在多个基准上显著缩小了与基于描述符方法的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无描述符视觉定位因避免高维描述符存储、保护场景隐私并简化地图维护而具有优势，但其精度远落后于基于描述符的管线。现有方法在纯几何匹配中未能充分利用局部几何线索、缺乏关键点间的全局上下文，且过拟合于单一关键点检测器，导致几何判别性不足。

Method: GeoMix从三个层面增强几何判别性：(1) 局部层面：方向与距离感知嵌入丰富邻域聚合的细粒度空间结构；(2) 全局层面：可学习上下文节点通过交叉注意力聚合并分发场景范围信息，解决局部感受野之外的歧义；(3) 训练层面：Mix-Training利用检测器无关的几何空间，跨多个关键点检测器学习表示。

Result: 在MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes和Aachen Day-Night数据集上，GeoMix在无描述符方法中达到最优，相比此前最佳方法，75%分位数旋转误差降低89%，平移误差降低高达90%，且零样本泛化到未见过的检测器，缩小了与描述符管线的差距。

Conclusion: GeoMix通过多层次几何判别性增强和跨检测器混合训练，有效缩小了无描述符定位与描述符定位之间的精度鸿沟，为隐私保护和轻量化视觉定位提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Descriptor-free visual localization eliminates high-dimensional descriptor storage, preserves scene privacy, and simplifies map maintenance, yet its accuracy still lags far behind descriptor-based pipelines. We identify this gap to insufficient geometric discriminability in geometry-only matching. Without visual appearance, current methods underutilize local geometry cues, lack the global context among keypoints, and overfit to a single keypoint detector. We further observe that descriptor-free matching naturally enables multi-detector training, as heterogeneous keypoints can be optimized in a shared geometry-only space without aligning descriptor spaces. Building on these insights, we propose GeoMix, a descriptor-free 2D-3D matching framework that strengthens geometric discriminability at three levels. Locally, directional and distance-aware embeddings enrich neighborhood aggregation with fine-grained spatial structure. Globally, learnable context nodes aggregate and redistribute scene-wide information via cross-attention to resolve ambiguities beyond local receptive fields. At the training level, Mix-Training exploits this detector-agnostic geometry space to learn representations across multiple keypoint detectors. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night show that GeoMix sets a new state of the art among descriptor-free methods, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89\% and translation error by up to 90\% over the previous best, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors and narrowing the gap to descriptor-based pipelines. Code is available at $\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{this links}}$.

</details>


### [202] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的可解释方法，通过采样解读隐藏状态表征并定位编码词汇信息的注意力头，对CLIP模型实施电路级干预，有效提升对印刷体攻击的鲁棒性，并在多个LVLM上验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型作为多数LVLM的基础视觉编码器，存在一个重要缺陷：图像中的无关文本会误导视觉表征偏离真实语义而偏向词汇含义，这种印刷体攻击（TA）在自动驾驶等安全关键应用中构成重大风险，而现有方法缺乏可解释性和有效性。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的可解释机制方法：(1) 基于采样的隐状态表征解读，量化注意力头对语义/词汇关注的归因；(2) 通过概率分析和电路挖掘，分离出ViT中过度编码词汇信息的特定组件；(3) 对识别出的电路施加无需训练的简单干预（如选择性调整注意力权重）。

Result: 在目标分类任务中，该方法在抵御印刷体攻击方面优于有监督和无训练防御方法；应用于多个LVLM视觉编码器后，在RIO-Bench的VQA任务中显著提升了TA干扰下的准确率。

Conclusion: 确认了所提机制方法的有效性与泛化性，通过识别并干预词汇编码电路，无需额外训练即可实现可解释且有效的TA鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.

</details>


### [203] [Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02497)
*Song Tang,Shuming Hu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出主动全景指代分割任务（APRS），并设计了PanoSeeker智能体，通过EgoSphere空间视觉记忆和VLM实现高效360°环境探索与目标分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代分割模型仅处理固定视角的静态图像，无法应用于具身AI中智能体需要在连续360°环境中进行主动感知的场景。为弥合这一差距，作者提出了APRS任务。

Method: 提出PanoSeeker，包含：1) EgoSphere显式空间视觉记忆，将局部观测逐步整合为统一360°表征，规划高效非冗余搜索轨迹；2) 集成VLM进行主动视角调整（Δθ, Δφ）；3) 构建专家标注搜索轨迹数据集进行监督微调，再通过强化学习后训练优化探索效率。

Result: 在建立的APRS基准上，PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度方面显著优于改进的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: PanoSeeker通过记忆增强和强化学习后训练，有效解决了主动360°环境中的目标搜索与分割问题，为具身AI中的主动视觉感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($Δθ, Δφ$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [204] [From SRA to Self-Flow: Data Augmentation or Self-Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02508)
*Dengyang Jiang,Mengmeng Wang,Harry Yang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过提出注意力分离（Attention Separation）方法，揭示了Self-Flow中双时间步调度带来的性能提升主要源于数据增强而非token间交互，并基于此设计了一种结合自表示对齐、双时间步和注意力分离增强的方法，在ImageNet上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Self-Flow中双时间步调度机制，厘清其性能增益究竟来自不同噪声级别token间的交互还是噪声维度的数据增强。

Method: 提出Attention Separation方法，在保持Self-Flow双时间步输入的同时，阻断不同噪声级别token之间的注意力交互，以解耦交互效应与数据增强效应。

Result: 去除token间交互不仅不降低性能，甚至能提升性能，证明Self-Flow的改进主要来自数据增强；Attention Separation本身通过将单张图像拆分为多个有效训练部分实现数据扩充。

Conclusion: Self-Flow相比SRA的提升主要源于数据增强而非token交互；结合自表示对齐、双时间步和注意力分离增强的设计在ImageNet上有效。

Abstract: Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion transformer training and improve generation quality. Recent self-alignment methods, such as SRA and Self-Flow, further remove the dependency on external pretrained encoders by constructing alignment within the diffusion model itself. However, the mechanism behind the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow, dual-time scheduling, remains under-examined: Self-Flow attributes its gain to interactions between tokens at different noise levels, where cleaner tokens help infer noisier ones. In this work, we revisit this explanation and ask whether the gain instead comes from data augmentation along the noise dimension. To disentangle these factors, we introduce Attention Separation, which preserves the same dual-timestep input as Self-Flow while blocking attention between tokens assigned to different noise levels. Surprisingly, removing such interaction does not degrade performance and can even improve it, suggesting that the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow mainly comes from data augmentation. Furthermore,We show that Attention Separation itself provides an augmentation effect by splitting a single image into multiple effective training parts to expand the training data. Based on these observations, we combine self-representation alignment with dual-timestep and attention-separation augmentation, and demonstrate the effectiveness of this design on ImageNet.

</details>


### [205] [PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02515)
*Haofei Xu,Rundi Wu,Philipp Henzler,Nikolai Kalischek,Michael Oechsle,Fabian Manhardt,Marc Pollefeys,Andreas Geiger,Federico Tombari,Michael Niemeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种极简的像素空间扩散Transformer（Plain ViT），直接在原始3D点图块上操作，无需混合架构、复杂损失函数或点图分词器，在单图像3D重建任务上超越了现有潜在扩散模型和混合方法，尤其在透明物体等高度模糊区域表现更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法过度依赖复杂的混合架构和损失函数，或将几何信息压缩到潜在空间以利用预训练潜在扩散模型，这些架构开销和复杂损失设计实际上是不必要的。

Method: 提出一种极简的像素空间扩散Transformer，基于普通ViT架构，直接在原始3D点图块上操作，以预训练DINOv3的图像令牌为条件，从头训练扩散骨干网络，无需点图分词器。

Result: 超越复杂的潜在扩散模型，显著简于混合替代方案，生成更锐利的几何结构，在透明物体等高模糊区域更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 证明了单图像3D重建中复杂的混合架构和损失函数并非必需，极简的像素空间扩散Transformer即可取得更优性能。

Abstract: State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.

</details>


### [206] [Alignment Is All You Need For X-to-4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02516)
*Qiaowei Miao,Kehan Li,Yawei Luo,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align4D是一个灵活的框架，能够将任意模态输入转换为一致的视频-3D对，通过Object Distance Alignment、Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment和Asynchronous Optimization三项关键技术实现高质量的X-to-4D生成，在X4D和Consistent4D数据集上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意用户定义的模态到4D（X-to-4D）生成仍然具有挑战性，主要因为构建多样化数据集的成本高昂以及现有方法的可扩展性有限。

Method: (1) Object Distance Alignment：搜索视频对齐和多视角对齐的目标距离（VAOD/MAOD），协调4D渲染与视频及多视角扩散模型的先验；(2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment：通过同步的视频和3D输入约束已知和未知视角，确保一致的4D生成；(3) Asynchronous Optimization：解耦高斯属性与形变网络训练，提升运动和几何保真度。

Result: 在X4D和Consistent4D数据集上的实验表明，Align4D在X-to-4D生成中达到了最先进的质量和一致性。

Conclusion: Align4D有效解决了任意模态到4D生成的难题，提出的三项关键技术和X4D数据集为未来研究提供了新的基准和方向。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models excel at synthesizing high-quality images, videos, and 3D content under multimodal control. However, arbitrary user-defined modality-to-4D (X-to-4D) generation remains challenging due to the high cost of constructing diverse datasets and the limited scalability of existing methods. This paper presents Align4D, a flexible framework that translates any-modal input into coherent video-3D pairs, using video to guide 4D motion and 3D data to shape 4D geometry. Align4D introduces three key techniques: (1) Object Distance Alignment, which searches Video-Aligned and Multiview-Aligned Object Distances (VAOD/MAOD), respectively, to reconcile 4D renderings with video and the priors of multiview diffusion models; (2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment, which constrains known and unknown views through synchronized video and 3D inputs, ensuring consistent 4D generation; and (3) Asynchronous Optimization, which decouples Gaussian attribute and deformation network training to enhance motion and geometry fidelity. We further propose the X4D dataset, which integrates prompt, image, video, and 3D data for benchmarking. Experiments on X4D and Consistent4D demonstrate that Align4D achieves state-of-the-art quality and consistency in X-to-4D generation. Project page: https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/.

</details>


### [207] [WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02517)
*Hanlin Wang,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Wen Wang,Qingyan Bai,Ka Leong Cheng,Yue Yu,Yixuan Li,Yihao Meng,Zichen Liu,Yanhong Zeng,Yujun Shen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldDirector提出了一种高度可控的视频世界模型框架，通过将语义运动编排与视觉生成解耦，利用LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，实现了持久动态物体记忆和无限制视角探索，即使物体长时间离开视野后重新进入，也能保持其精确的视觉身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型将物理动力学与像素渲染纠缠在一起，依赖连续视觉观察来维持运动，无法在物体长时间离开视野后保持其视觉身份的一致性，限制了复杂长事件的合成能力。

Method: 显式地将语义运动编排与视觉生成解耦，利用LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，然后将编排好的轨迹作为控制信号输入视频生成模型。

Result: 支持合成复杂且延展的事件，具有前所未有的可控性和持久动态物体记忆能力，能在外观稳定性、物理逻辑和视觉身份保持方面达到优异效果。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了动态物体在长时间离场后重新进入场景时的视觉身份保持问题，为可控视频世界模型提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Embedding Inference Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01276)
*Cedric Fitiavana Raelijohn,Sébastien Gambs,Jean-Francois Rajotte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在黑盒设定下，仅通过观察检索到的文档集合（无排序和相似度分数）来推断信息检索系统所用嵌入模型的攻击方法（EIA），并验证了该方法在包含重排序器或RAG系统中依然有效，同时提出了相似度阈值等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型通常隐藏在API背后，而目前的嵌入反转攻击大多需要攻击者已知所用模型。本文旨在探索更严格的黑盒设定下（仅能观察到检索到的文档集合，无排名或分数），攻击者是否仍能识别出目标系统使用的嵌入模型。

Method: 设计针对性查询（tailored queries），从候选模型集合中识别目标系统所使用的嵌入模型；在包含重排序器的系统中验证查询的判别力；在真实RAG系统中测试，利用精心设计的查询绕过LLM的拒绝机制；提出相似度阈值等缓解策略并评估其效果。

Result: 在黑盒设定下，仅凭检索到的文档集合即可有效识别嵌入模型；即使系统包含重排序器，某些查询仍保持判别力；在真实RAG系统中，针对性查询能够绕过LLM的拒答机制；相似度阈值等策略可提供一定缓解。

Conclusion: 嵌入模型推断攻击（EIA）在严格黑盒设定下是可行的，对IR系统的安全性构成威胁，但通过相似度阈值等方法可以在一定程度上防御此类攻击。

Abstract: Embedding models are essential components of modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems, yet they are typically hidden behind APIs. Recent works have shown that dense IR system can lead to security vulnerabilities such as embedding inversion attacks. However, such attacks usually require that the attacker knows the embedding model for the attack to be applicable. In this paper, we study IR systems under a black-box setting in which the adversary observes only the unordered set of retrieved documents, without ranking or similarity scores. We demonstrate that in such contexts, tailored queries allow an adversary to identify which embedding model is in use from a set of known model candidate, which we coin as an embedding inference attack (EIA). We also show that certain queries remain discriminative even when the system includes a reranker as a potential defense mechanism. We further validate our method on a real Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system, in which the tailored queries bypass the LLM's tendency to reject inputs it does not recognize as well-formed questions. Finally, we propose and evaluate other mitigation strategies such as similarity thresholds.

</details>


### [209] [Cognitive Firewall: A Proactive, Zero-Trust, Multi-Gate Framework for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01277)
*Michele Guida,Ruslan Shikhhamzayev,Sindhuja Penchala,Stefano Iannucci,Jiacheng Li,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognitive Firewall，一种将安全评估分解为四个分类门（意图门、零信任上下文门、一致性门、输出风险门）并通过升级机制而非分数平均来组合决策的主动运行时监督框架，在多个越狱基准测试上将攻击成功率降至2%以下，同时保持8%的过度拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全机制通常将提示或响应作为孤立消息评估，无法恢复跨轮次累积的恶意意图、验证声称的权限或检测跨对话分解的有害目标，导致多轮对话中LLM可能被诱导产生有害内容。

Method: 提出Cognitive Firewall框架，在用户与受保护目标模型之间插入独立监督模型，将安全评估分解为四个分类门：意图门（识别请求的操作目标）、零信任上下文门（将声称角色和权限视为未验证证据）、一致性门（检测跨轮次的升级和分解）、输出风险门（发布前检查候选响应）。门决策通过升级机制而非分数平均进行组合。

Result: 在四个越狱基准测试和一个良性安全测试集上，Cognitive Firewall将攻击成功率降低至三个攻击集上的2%或以下，在最困难的人工构建攻击集上降至14%，同时保持8%的过度拒绝率。

Conclusion: 分解的、对话级别的监督可以改进LLM安全的主动遏制和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be induced to produce harmful content through multi turn strategies in which no single user message appears clearly unsafe. Existing runtime safeguards commonly evaluate prompts or responses as isolated messages, which limits their ability to recover ac-cumulated intent, verify asserted authority, or detect harmful objectives decomposed across a dialogue. This paper presents the Cognitive Firewall, a proactive runtime oversight framework that interposes an independent oversight model between a user and a protected target mod l. The framework decomposes safety assessment into four categorical gates: an intent gate that identi-fies the operational objective of a request, a zero trust context gate that treats claimed roles and permissions as unverified evidence, a consistency gate that detects escalation and decomposition across turns, and an output risk gate that inspects candidate responses before release. Gate decisions are combined through escalation rather than score averaging, allowing any confident danger signal to block an interaction while preserving an auditable rationale. Experiments on four jailbreak benchmarks and a benign safety test set show that the Cognitive Firewall substantially reduces attack success across single turn, multi turn, authority based, and human crafted attacks. It lowers attack success to 2 percent or below on three attack sets and to 14 percent on the most difficult human crafted set, while maintaining an 8 percent over refusal rate. These results indicate that decomposed, conversation level oversight can improve proactive containment and auditability for LLM safety.

</details>


### [210] [Generative AI and Federated Learning for Intrusion Detection Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01305)
*Jiefei Liu,Abu Saleh Md Tayeen,Pratyay Kumar,Qixu Gong,Wenbin Jiang,Huiping Cao,Satyajayant Misra,Jayashree Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了生成式AI与联邦学习在入侵检测系统中的应用，涵盖生成模型（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）和FL技术如何解决IDS面临的攻击演化、数据稀缺、类别不平衡、隐私限制等挑战，并讨论了合成数据质量、非IID分布、通信效率、双用对抗风险等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDS面临攻击行为演化、真实数据集获取困难、流量记录不完整、攻击类别不平衡以及隐私约束导致集中式数据收集受限等多重挑战，亟需新方法突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，首先总结IDS代表性研究方向（对抗机器学习、异常检测、物联网IDS、可解释IDS、基准数据集），然后按模型家族（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）和任务目标分类生成式AI在IDS中的应用，最后综述生成式AI与FL集成的研究。

Result: 发现生成式AI可支持异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强、数据填补、对抗流量生成和IDS告警解释；FL可实现隐私保护下的分布式IDS训练；两者集成在隐私敏感和地理分布式场景中具有巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 指出该领域面临合成数据质量、真实流量生成、双用对抗风险、非IID客户端分布、通信高效模型共享、联邦IDS基准测试和面向网络安全的领域特定LLM等开放挑战。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for monitoring network traffic and identifying malicious activities in modern cyber-physical, Internet of Things (IoT), enterprise, and distributed network environments. However, developing reliable IDS models remains challenging because attack behaviors evolve over time, realistic datasets are difficult to obtain, traffic records may be incomplete, attack classes are often imbalanced, and privacy constraints limit centralized data collection. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and Federated Learning (FL) provide new opportunities to address these limitations. Generative models can support anomaly detection, synthetic traffic generation, data augmentation, data imputation, adversarial traffic generation, and IDS alert explanation. FL enables distributed IDS training without directly sharing local network traffic, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive and geographically distributed environments. This survey provides a structured review of generative AI and FL techniques for IDS. We first summarize representative IDS research directions, including adversarial machine learning, anomaly-based detection, IoT-oriented IDS, explainable IDS, and benchmark datasets. We then categorize generative AI applications in IDS according to model families and task objectives, covering autoencoder-based models, Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), diffusion models, and Large Language Models (LLMs). Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

</details>


### [211] [An alternative approach towards attacks against fully-split PLWE instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01340)
*Iván Blanco-Chacón,Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma,Raúl Durán Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于根的密码攻击的泛化问题，证明在完全分裂设置下，通过构造显式同构来扩展攻击无法产生新的漏洞，因为同构会扭曲样本使其无法用于区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答关于基于根的攻击泛化的关键问题，特别是验证是否可以通过构造显式同构从易受攻击实例扩展攻击。

Method: 构造了完全分裂多项式环与先前攻击适用多项式环之间的显式同构，证明该同构必然扭曲样本使其无法用于区分，并证明了完全分裂多项式环之间的任何同构都必须具有所构造的形式。

Result: 在完全分裂设置下，通过构造显式同构扩展基于根的攻击不会产生新的漏洞。

Conclusion: 完全分裂设置下的同构方法无法用于扩展根攻击，因为同构会破坏样本的区分性。

Abstract: In the present work we address some key questions regarding the generalization of root-based attacks presented in a recent work by the authors. In particular, we analyze potential root-based attacks extensions via the construction of explicit isomorphisms from vulnerable instances, and provide a formal proof that this approach will not yield any new vulnerabilities under a fully-split setting. To do so, we first construct an explicit isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings and polynomial rings where previous attacks apply and show that the application of such an isomorphism will always distort the samples in a way that the resulting samples cannot be used to distinguish. Then, we prove that any isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings must be of the form of the constructed isomorphism.

</details>


### [212] [Chameleon: Recovering Cyber-Physical Systems from Memory Corruption Attacks via ML Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01356)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Chameleon是一个利用机器学习训练的代理模块自动恢复受内存破坏攻击的CPS系统的框架。它在检测到攻击时用代理替换受损组件，在七种机器人平台上验证了有效性（平均R²=0.96），能在不中断任务的情况下从真实攻击中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS安全方案在检测到内存破坏攻击后通常终止或暂停系统，这在安全关键任务中可能导致灾难性后果；其他方案用简化默认组件降级系统行为或重启系统，均无法在保证任务连续性的同时有效恢复。

Method: Chameleon利用LLVM编译器框架，在分区粒度上训练基于机器学习的代理模块，这些代理近似原始分区的行为但不包含相同的内存破坏漏洞。当攻击被检测到时，系统将受损分区切换至其训练好的代理模块继续运行。

Result: 在七个模拟和真实机器人平台上评估，代理模块对原始分区的近似度达平均R²=0.96；能成功从真实世界内存破坏攻击中恢复系统并完成任务；性能与内存开销低。

Conclusion: Chameleon提供了一种可行的CPS安全恢复方案，通过ML代理模块替代受损组件，在不中断任务的前提下从内存破坏攻击中恢复，优于现有终止或降级方案。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly deployed in every aspect of our lives and can be compromised through memory corruption vulnerabilities, allowing attackers to hijack the control flow and take over the system.
