Existing techniques mostly focus on detecting such attacks but respond by terminating or halting execution upon attack detection, which is not acceptable in CPSs used in safety-critical tasks, as interrupted tasks can have catastrophic consequences. Other techniques replace compromised CPS components with simplified defaults that degrade system behavior, or reboot the system upon attack detection.
  We propose Chameleon, a novel framework for automatically recovering CPSs from memory corruption attacks using machine learning (ML)-based surrogates trained at compartment granularity that nearly replicate their original compartments' behavior but do not have the same memory corruption vulnerabilities. Upon attack detection, Chameleon replaces the compromised compartment with its trained surrogate. We implemented Chameleon using the LLVM compiler and evaluated its efficiency and effectiveness on seven different robotic vehicles (RVs), including simulated and real ones. We found that Chameleon can generate surrogates that closely approximate the original compartments (with an average R$^2$=0.96), successfully recover the system despite real-world memory corruption attacks unlike prior approaches, and complete their tasks while incurring low performance and memory overhead.

</details>


### [213] [From Forgeries to Foundation Models: A Systematic Survey of Identity Document Attack and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01442)
*Gourab Das,Pavan Kumar C,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个统一的身份证件防伪威胁模型，涵盖呈现攻击、数字注入攻击和生成式AI合成，系统回顾了2019-2025年的检测方法与数据集，发现基准测试与实际部署之间存在持续性的现实差距，并揭示了非拉丁文字生成中的脚本依赖生成不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具使得高保真文档合成和字段级篡改变得极易操作，而现有检测方法仍受限于未能反映这一威胁的基准测试，形成了巨大的攻击面和安全缺口。

Method: 采用统一的威胁模型框架，系统梳理了从基于规则的启发式方法到特征定位、注入感知流水线、基础模型和少样本框架的检测技术演进；对2019-2025年间的公开数据集进行系统审计；分析大型多模态模型在身份证件篡改中的表现；在未见过的合成身份证上进行零样本基准测试。

Result: 发现了基准条件与部署环境之间的持续现实差距；识别出非拉丁文字修补中反复出现的脚本依赖生成不稳定性问题；最强的公开模型在安全导向操作条件下APCER值仍超过25%，泛化能力显著不足。

Conclusion: 需要构建法医可追溯、隐私保护且法律上可问责的身份验证系统，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Identity document forgery has undergone a fundamental capability shift: generative AI tools now enable high-fidelity document synthesis and field-level manipulation with minimal technical expertise, while detection methods remain constrained by benchmarks that do not reflect this threat. The resulting attack surface spans physical presentation, digital injection, and fully generative synthesis, introducing distinct forensic failure modes that require a unified threat model and evaluation framework. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified treatment of Presentation Attacks, Digital Injection Attacks, and GenAI-driven synthesis within a single identity verification threat model. We trace detection methodologies from rule-based heuristics through forensic localisation, injection-aware pipelines, foundation models, and few-shot frameworks. A systematic audit of public datasets from 2019--2025 exposes a persistent Reality Gap between benchmark conditions and operational deployment. We further analyse large multimodal models for identity document manipulation, identifying Script-Dependent Generative Instability (SDGI) as a recurring typographic failure mode in non-Latin script inpainting. Finally, zero-shot benchmarking on unseen synthesised ID cards shows that even the strongest publicly available models achieve APCER values above 25% under security-oriented operating conditions, highlighting substantial limits in cross-domain generalisation. We conclude by outlining future directions toward forensically grounded, privacy-preserving, and legally accountable identity verification systems.

</details>


### [214] [Hamm-Grams: An Algorithm for Mining Regular Expressions of Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01445)
*Derek Everett,Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 hamm-grams 的鲁棒性静态特征（固定长度含通配符的正则表达式），用于恶意软件检测与分类。通过设计新的局部敏感哈希来高效挖掘常见 hamm-grams，实验证明了其相比传统 n-gram 特征的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 n-gram 静态特征在恶意软件检测中虽然有效，但本质上是脆弱的（brittle），容易被绕过或变形。因此需要构造更鲁棒的特征来应对恶意软件的持续演化。

Method: 提出 hamm-grams 特征——一类固定长度、包含单字符通配符的正则表达式。设计了一种新的局部敏感哈希（LSH），使小汉明距离的字符串对更容易发生碰撞；并在哈希桶内进行聚类以确定通配符位置，从而高效挖掘常见 hamm-grams。

Result: 在恶意软件分类和检测任务上，hamm-grams 特征相比传统 n-gram 特征表现出更好的鲁棒性和性能优势。

Conclusion: hamm-grams 作为一种更鲁棒的静态特征，能够有效提升恶意软件检测系统的性能，且所提出的高效挖掘算法使其具有实用价值。

Abstract: Malware poses a critical and ever-evolving threat, and robust and effective systems for detecting and classifying malware are of essential importance. $n$-grams features are among the common static features used in effective machine learning systems for malware, but these features are inherently brittle. We propose an algorithm for constructing more robust features, hamm-grams, which are a special class of regular expressions having a fixed length and single-character wildcards. We devise an efficient algorithm for finding common hamm-grams using a new locality-sensitive hash designed to produce collisions among pairs of small Hamming distance and a clustering within hash buckets to place wildcards. We then demonstrate the advantages of these features in malware classification and detection tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Overthink-Triggered Slowdown Attacks on LVLM-Based Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01518)
*Qiang Han,Jie Wu,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地识别并验证了大型视觉-语言模型(LVLM)在机器人系统中的"过度思考"行为，发现通过嵌入精心设计的场景文本可以触发该行为，导致推理延迟放大高达6.96倍，即使在黑盒环境下也能有效攻击，且触发器可在不同LVLM模型间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM集成到机器人系统后表现出过度思考行为（生成长推理轨迹导致推理时间过长），这种过度思考行为可能被对手恶意利用，通过故意触发来减缓受害机器人系统的决策速度，导致各种安全问题。目前缺乏对这种"过度思考引发的减速攻击"的系统性研究。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) 构建推理密集型场景文本语料库，从短响应前缀中提取与过度思考相关的词汇特征；2) 通过基于前缀的代理分数进行高效黑盒搜索，并用完整延迟测量确认少数候选；3) 在未见图像和多个LVLM上评估黑盒迁移性，报告延迟放大比和攻击成功率。

Result: 在三个代表性LVLM上，所有触发器均产生大于1.0倍的减速比，最强单触发器达到6.96倍；物理打印文本触发器仍造成高达4.74倍的延迟放大。触发器可在多个LVLM模型间迁移并持续导致显著减速。

Conclusion: 发现的触发器可在多个LVLM模型间迁移，持续导致机器人系统显著减速，验证了这种过度思考诱导攻击的有效性和威胁性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have been increasingly integrated into robotic systems. However, these models may exhibit overthinking behaviors, where they generate excessively long reasoning traces, incurring an excessive inference time. This overthinking behavior poses a serious risk to robotic systems, as the adversary can deliberately trigger overthinking to slow down the decision making of a victim robotic system, causing a variety of safety issues (i.e., an overthinking-induced slowdown attack). To initiate this attack, an adversary can embed carefully crafted, human-readable scene text into the visual scene observed by a victim robotic agent, causing significant inference delays even under a strict black-box setting. Therefore, the embedded scene text serves as a significant "trigger" for the attack.
  This work systematically identifies and validates transferable triggers of overthinking in robotic systems by introducing a three-stage framework. First, we construct a diverse corpus of reasoning-intensive scene text and extract overthinking-correlated lexical features from short response prefixes. Second, we perform an efficient black-box search guided by a prefix-based proxy score while selectively confirming a small set of top candidates with full latency measurements. Third, we evaluate black-box transfer using a fixed pool of triggers on unseen images and multiple LVLMs, reporting latency amplification and attack success rates under standard thresholds. Across three representative LVLMs, all triggers yield slowdown ratios greater than 1.0x, with the strongest single-trigger case reaching 6.96x. The physical printing of the text trigger still causes up to 4.74x latency amplification. These results demonstrate that our discovered triggers are transferred between multiple LVLM models and consistently cause significant slowdowns in robotic systems.

</details>


### [216] [LIB-TRAP: Standard Cell Library Hardware Trojan Risk Assessment and Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01526)
*Harish Kumar Dharavath,Md Muhtasim Alam Chowdhury,Rozhin Yasaei,Soheil Salehi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于标准单元库恶意篡改的新型硬件木马威胁模型，通过将标准单元视为不可信组件，利用被篡改的库在设计中隐藏硬件木马，并在AES-128、以太网控制器等基准电路上验证了其有效性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件木马研究多关注标准单元间空隙的插入，但缺乏对构成数字设计基本单元——标准单元本身安全漏洞的探究，因此需要研究将标准单元视为不可信的新威胁模型。

Method: 利用开源和工业级EDA工具，将Saed32nm和Sky130nm标准单元库转换为恶意库，使设计者在综合时使用含失活HT的库，而代工厂在制造时将其替换为激活版HT单元；在AES-128、以太网控制器、WISHBONE DMA引擎三个基准电路上分别用Synopsys 32nm和SkyWater 130nm工艺进行综合对比。

Result: 在三款基准电路上成功实现了基于标准单元库的HT攻击，提取了包括总单元数、总面积、动态功耗和静态功耗在内的设计级特征用于二元分类，证明了该攻击向量的有效性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 标准单元库可作为新的硬件木马攻击面，设计者需警惕来自不可信标准单元的安全威胁，所提方法可有效隐藏HT并使现有检测手段难以识别。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities inherent to the fabless semiconductor manufacturing model have significantly increased the risk of malicious Hardware Trojan (HT) insertion, posing severe threats to hardware security. Several HT mitigation and detection strategies have been developed, and existing works explore the insertion of HTs in the space between standard cells in an integrated circuit. However, there is a lack of research into the vulnerabilities posed by the building blocks of most digital designs on the market today, the standard cells. This work investigates a novel threat model in which standard cells are considered untrusted. Our proposed threat model provides the design house with a tampered standard cell library. The intended netlist is synthesized and implemented using the tampered library. During fabrication, a nefarious foundry replaces the library's deactivated HT cells with activated counterparts. Using open-source and industry-standard Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tools, existing standard cell libraries, Saed32nm and Sky130nm, are converted into malicious libraries capable of masking the presence of arbitrary HTs from IC designers. The malicious library is then applied and characterized in multiple standard benchmark designs. To demonstrate the efficacy and stealthiness of this standard cell-based attack vector, three benchmark circuits, an AES-128 encryption core, an Ethernet controller, and a WISHBONE DMA engine, were synthesized using both clean and Trojan-infected libraries across Synopsys 32nm and SkyWater 130nm technologies. Design-level features, including total cell count, total area, dynamic power consumption, and static power, were extracted from these synthesized circuits to serve as inputs for binary classification

</details>


### [217] [VeriChat: An Agentic Conversational AI Assistant for Hardware Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01668)
*Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Shams Tarek,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriChat是一个面向硬件安全验证的领域专用对话助手，采用检索增强的多智能体架构，在减少幻觉的同时集成开源EDA工具进行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证，在忠实度评分上达到87.73%，显著优于主流闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全验证流程复杂，工程师缺乏结构化的安全指导支持，而通用聊天机器人存在幻觉和知识陈旧问题，无法可靠地提供硬件安全领域的上下文感知帮助。

Method: 采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，三个专用智能体协同工作以最小化幻觉并提升响应透明度和可靠性；集成Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys等开源EDA工具，直接在用户提供的RTL设计上执行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证。

Result: 忠实度评分达到87.73%，显著优于领先的专有模型；在AES S-Box IP的硬件木马检测案例中，VeriChat通过多轮对话工作流自主识别、仿真并形式化证明了隐蔽的密钥泄露漏洞。

Conclusion: VeriChat作为补充而非替代现有验证工作流的专用对话助手，能够有效提供上下文感知的安全指导，并通过多智能体架构和EDA工具集成实现高可靠性的硬件安全验证。

Abstract: Hardware security verification is a multi-stage process in which engineers must navigate complex design analyses, threat considerations, and verification strategies. They often need security-focused guidance, yet current verification environments provide little structured support for such assistance. Although conversational AI could offer such on-demand assistance, directly using general-purpose chatbots like ChatGPT or Gemini is risky due to their tendency to hallucinate and their reliance on static, outdated knowledge. We present VeriChat, a domain-specialized conversational assistant designed to support, rather than replace, existing verification workflows by providing context-aware security guidance. VeriChat employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent workflow in which three specialized agents collaboratively minimize hallucinations while improving the transparency and reliability of the response. Beyond question answering, VeriChat integrates open-source EDA tools, including Icarus Verilog, Yosys, and SymbiYosys, to perform syntax checking, synthesis analysis, simulation, and formal verification directly on user-provided RTL designs. Evaluated using a comprehensive methodology, VeriChat achieves a Faithfulness score of 87.73%, significantly outperforming the leading proprietary models. We demonstrate the framework through a hardware Trojan detection case study on an AES S-Box IP, where VeriChat autonomously identifies, simulates, and formally proves a covert key-leakage vulnerability through a multi-turn conversational workflow.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Gradient-Based Attacks: Adversarial Robustness and Explainability Stability in Cybersecurity Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01679)
*Mona Rajhans,Vishal Khawarey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释性稳定指数(ESI)，评估对抗攻击对树模型SHAP解释的破坏，发现预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是独立维度，需联合测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗攻击不仅降低分类器性能，还会破坏安全分析人员依赖的SHAP解释，现有研究缺乏统一评估解释稳定性的指标。

Method: 将MLP研究扩展至Random Forest和XGBoost，在四个表格安全数据集上评估五种攻击（含三种黑盒方法），提出ESI度量TreeSHAP归因漂移，并与鲁棒性指数(RI)在同一[0,1]尺度上对比。

Result: 基于梯度的ZOO攻击对XGBoost产生退化结果（RI~0.98），而得分型Square Attack揭示真实脆弱性（RI~0.36）；XGBoost的ESI为0.06-0.16，RF为0.14-0.29，表明二者鲁棒性与稳定性不同步。

Conclusion: 预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是独立维度，需联合测量；提出梯度依赖性与查询效率的双轴框架解释攻击排序，给出树模型集成评估的实用建议。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on cybersecurity classifiers pose a dual threat: degrading predictions and destabilising the SHAP-based explanations that security analysts rely on to understand and triage alerts. We extend our prior MLP conference study to Random Forest and XGBoost across four tabular security datasets (phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021), evaluating five attacks including three black-box methods applicable to non-differentiable tree models. We introduce the Explainability Stability Index (ESI), a scalar metric computed from TreeSHAP attribution drift under adversarial perturbation, reported on the same [0,1] scale as the Robustness Index (RI). A key finding is that gradient-based black-box attacks (ZOO) produce degenerate results against XGBoost (apparent RI ~0.98) due to piecewise-constant prediction surfaces, while score-based Square Attack reveals genuine vulnerability (RI ~0.36). These degenerate perturbations still drive substantial attribution drift: XGBoost ESI ~0.06-0.16 despite near-perfect ZOO robustness, versus 0.14-0.29 for RF, showing that prediction robustness and explanation stability are distinct axes requiring joint measurement. A two-axis framework (gradient dependence, query efficiency) explains the observed attack ranking and yields practical guidance for tree ensemble evaluation. A step-size ablation explains a counterintuitive PGD anomaly on z-score normalised tabular data.

</details>


### [219] [Pmeta-TLA: Backdoor Attacks for Speech Classification Models via Meta-Learning with Timbre Leakage Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01702)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA)的隐蔽后门攻击方法，通过在深度自监督特征的帧级别中嵌入音色信息来生成自然感知的毒化样本。同时引入Pmeta-TLA，一种基于元学习和PCGrad的多后门注入训练机制，实现一次性嵌入多个后门。实验表明该方法在关键词识别任务中相比基线方法具有更好的攻击效果、隐蔽性、鲁棒性和更低的攻击成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后端攻对语音分类模型构成严重安全威胁，但现有语音触发器容易被深度神经网络防御者检测到，因此有必要探索更多潜在的攻击技术来暴露和防范此类风险。

Method: 提出TLA攻击方法，将音色信息分散嵌入到深度自监督特征的帧级别中，使毒化样本在人类感知上显得自然。进一步提出Pmeta-TLA，利用元学习和Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad)实现多后门一次训练注入，并将TLA作为其中的多目标攻击工具。

Result: 在关键词识别任务的多项深度神经网络模型上进行数据投毒后门攻击实验，结果表明所提策略相比基线方法实现了更高的攻击效能、更强的隐蔽性、更好的鲁棒性以及更低的攻击成本。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了语音后门攻击的新途径，通过帧级音色信息扩散和元学习多后门注入策略显著提升了攻击的隐蔽性和效率，为后续防御研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Recently, speech classification methods have gained widespread adoption in intelligent gadgets. Current study indicates that backdoor attacks provide a substantial security concern to these models, underscoring the pressing necessity to investigate additional potential attack techniques to expose and prevent such risks. This work discusses the vulnerability of current speech triggers to detection by deep neural network defenders and introduces the Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA). The suggested trigger disseminates timbre information at the frame level within the deep self-supervised features, producing poisoned samples that appear natural to human perception. Furthermore, we introduce Pmeta-TLA, an innovative training mechanism for embedding numerous backdoors one time. This method proposes a multi-backdoor injection training strategy using meta-learning and Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad) and introduces TLA as a multi-target attack tool within it. We performed tests on data-poisoning backdoor attacks in keyword spotting tasks utilizing some deep neural network models. Experimental results indicate that the proposed strategy attains superior Attack efficacy, enhanced stealthiness, robustness, and a reduced attack cost relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [220] [Trust Boundary Semantic Gaps: A Multi-dimensional Analysis and Mitigation for Security-by-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01711)
*Doyeon Kim,Jin-Young Choi,Junghee Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出信任边界语义鸿沟（TBSG）概念，指 artifact 通过语法验证但未能满足接收域语义安全需求的问题。基于75起安全事件的边界级分析，构建了四维分析模型（MDTBSG），并开发了设计时框架TBSAM用于识别、优先级排序和缓解TBSG。在SolarWinds/SUNBURST攻击的回顾性重建中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代系统依赖格式、协议和签名等语法层机制来接受跨信任边界的artifact，但这些机制仅验证形式合规性，无法确保artifact满足接收域所需的语义安全属性。已签名的更新或认证令牌可能被接受却导致系统受损。现有方法缺乏对"通过语法验证但语义不匹配"这一鸿沟的系统性分析和缓解手段。

Method: 1) 分析2014-2025年间75个公开报道的安全事件（边界级分析），将语义错位归纳为四维分析模型MDTBSG：身份（Identity）、空间（Spatial）、时间（Temporal）和解释（Interpretation）。2) 基于该模型开发TBSAM框架，从设计规范中识别TBSG、优先级排序、追踪传播的鸿沟至其源边界，并映射到候选架构控制措施。3) 对SolarWinds/SUNBURST供应链攻击进行回顾性重建评估。

Result: TBSAM能够显式化接收域假设，区分本地起源的鸿沟与传播而来的鸿沟，并识别出能够中断攻击路径的控制措施。证明了语法验证在信任边界处是不够的，显式化信任边界假设可以补充Security-by-Design方法。

Conclusion: 信任边界处仅靠语法验证不足以保障安全；系统设计时需要显式化信任边界处的语义假设，从而弥合语义鸿沟，提升系统安全性。TBSAM框架为设计时识别和缓解这类问题提供了系统化的方法。

Abstract: Modern systems use format-, protocol-, and signature-based mechanisms before accepting artifacts across trust boundaries. These mechanisms are necessary: they show that an artifact is well formed, protocol-compliant, or properly authenticated. They do not, however, show that the artifact satisfies the semantic security properties required by the receiving domain. A signed update or an authenticated token may therefore be accepted yet enable compromise. We call this condition a Trust Boundary Semantic Gap (TBSG): an artifact crosses a trust boundary and passes correctly implemented syntactic validation, but the assertions established by that pass are insufficient to satisfy the receiving domain's security requirements. TBSG concerns what remains unestablished after a syntactic pass, not absent checks or implementation bugs. Analyzing 75 publicly reported security incidents (2014-2025) at the boundary level, we organize semantic misalignment into a four-dimensional analysis model: Identity, Spatial, Temporal, and Interpretation (MDTBSG). Building on it, we develop Trust Boundary Semantic Analysis and Mitigation (TBSAM), a design-time framework that identifies TBSGs from design specifications, prioritizes them, traces propagated gaps to their originating boundary, and maps each to candidate architectural controls. We apply TBSAM to a retrospective reconstruction of the SolarWinds/SUNBURST supply-chain attack, showing how it makes receiving-domain assumptions explicit, separates locally originating from propagated gaps, and identifies controls that interrupt the path. These results suggest that syntactic validation, while necessary, is not sufficient at trust boundaries, and that making trust-boundary assumptions explicit can complement Security-by-Design.

</details>


### [221] [Resilient Liquid Democracy: Mitigating Voting Power Imbalances via Secure Delegation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01730)
*Zhuolun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全的液态民主机制，通过去中心化定时释放加密隐藏委托选择以防止从众和胁迫，同时引入排名多重委托和个人备用选票应对代表失效。在多项真实数据集上评估发现，委托是否能提升代表性准确性遵循"可恢复缺口定律"，密封委托的主要收益是结构性降低权力集中度，而排名多重委托能显著减少投票损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的液态民主部署依赖透明委托，导致选民暴露于从众效应、可验证的胁迫以及高支持率代表弃权带来的系统性脆弱性。本文旨在解决知情专家路由与系统稳健性之间的张力。

Method: 采用去中心化定时释放加密实现密封委托，在形成阶段加密隐藏委托选择以防止从众和胁迫，最终计票阶段恢复公开可审计性；扩展排名多重委托和个人备用选票机制应对代表失效；通过形式化证明保证预揭示秘密性和重新提交收据无关联性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上评估发现：(1) 委托提升代表性准确性遵循"可恢复缺口定律"——仅在弃权规模大且系统性不具代表性时有效，否则中性或有害；(2) 密封委托的主要收益是结构性降低权力集中度而非直接提升准确性；(3) 排名多重委托与个人备用选票在现实和针对性代表失效场景下显著减少投票损失。

Conclusion: 安全的液态民主机制能够缓解透明委托的固有缺陷，但委托决策需谨慎：代表式委托比能力精英式委托更安全，密封形成的价值体现在系统结构性稳健性上。

Abstract: Liquid democracy promises to improve collective decision-making by allowing voters to vote directly, delegate their voting power to trusted participants, or combine both approaches through fallback mechanisms. However, existing deployments typically rely on transparent delegation, which exposes voters to popularity-driven herding, makes coercion verifiable, and introduces systemic fragility when highly-backed delegates abstain. In this paper, we propose a secure liquid democracy mechanism that resolves the tension between informed expertise routing and systemic robustness. We introduce a sealed delegation regime using decentralized timed-release encryption, which cryptographically hides delegation choices during the formation phase to prevent herding and coercion, while restoring full public auditability for the final tally. To address delegate failures, we extend the protocol with ranked multi-delegation and personal fallback ballots. We formally prove pre-reveal secrecy and resubmission receipt-freeness for our protocol. Finally, we evaluate the mechanism on four real datasets, a municipal participatory-budgeting election with a calibration survey, twenty further participatory-budgeting elections, and 60,000 US voters with an objective competence measure. They show that whether delegation improves representational accuracy follows a recoverable-gap law; it helps only when abstention is large and systematically unrepresentative, and is otherwise neutral or harmful, with representative-style delegation safer than delegating to a competence elite. The benefit of sealed formation is primarily structural, sharply reducing power concentration rather than directly improving accuracy; and ranked multi-delegation with personal fallback ballots sharply reduces vote loss under realistic and targeted delegate failures, a result that replicates across all twenty elections.

</details>


### [222] [Knowledge Over Parameters: Evolving Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01742)
*Yuqiang Sun,Han Liu,Ying Li,Yiran Zhang,Zong Cao,Ziyun Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoVuln是一个自动化框架，将智能合约漏洞检测转化为程序性知识演化问题，通过IoC架构的运行时和两阶段演化流水线，仅需极少标注样本即可合成并优化检测逻辑，在五种真实漏洞类型上达到71%的宏平均F1分数，且演化知识可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能合约漏洞检测方法存在两大瓶颈：基于提示的方法依赖人工设计的检测规则，而微调方法需要大量标注数据，但智能合约漏洞标注数据天然稀缺。因此需要一种能自动合成和优化检测逻辑、且仅需极少标注样本的方法。

Method: EvoVuln包含两个核心机制：(1) 基于控制反转(IoC)架构的运行时，将检测规则编译为可执行策略，解耦确定性控制流与LLM语义推理，产生密集诊断遥测用于精确定位错误；(2) 两阶段演化流水线：冷启动阶段通过自动合成的边界用例引导和压力测试初始规则，少样本演化阶段仅用每种漏洞类型5个脆弱和5个安全示例将策略锚定于真实语义。

Result: 在五种真实漏洞类型上，EvoVuln达到71%宏平均F1分数，超越所有基线方法。演化出的程序性知识可跨模型移植：使轻量低成本模型超越更大的零样本模型19个百分点，且无需重新训练即可迁移到其他LLM，单次演化成本低于50美元。

Conclusion: EvoVuln证明了将漏洞检测重新定义为程序性知识演化问题的有效性，通过解耦LLM语义推理与确定性控制流，结合高效的少样本演化机制，能够在极低成本和少量标注样本下生成高质量、可迁移的检测逻辑。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities are predominantly logic bugs whose detection requires structured, step-by-step procedural knowledge of attack patterns and contract semantics. Existing LLM-based methods struggle to generate this knowledge automatically: prompt-based methods rely on manually crafted detection rules, while fine-tuning requires massive labeled datasets that are inherently scarce in this domain. We present EvoVuln, an automated framework that reformulates vulnerability detection as a procedural knowledge evolution problem, synthesizing and refining detection logic using only a minimal number of labeled samples.
  To achieve this, EvoVuln introduces two key mechanisms. First, a Runtime with an Inversion of Control (IoC) architecture compiles detection rules into Executable Policies. This strictly decouples deterministic control flow from LLM semantic reasoning, ensuring faithful logical adherence and producing dense diagnostic telemetry for precise error localization. Second, a two-phase evolution pipeline refines the rule via abductive semantic debugging without any parameter updates: Cold Start bootstraps and stress-tests an initial rule using auto-synthesized corner cases; Few-Shot Evolving then grounds the policy in real-world semantics using only five vulnerable and five safe examples per vulnerability type.
  Evaluated across five real-world vulnerability types, EvoVuln achieves a 71% macro-average F1-score, outperforming all baselines. The evolved procedural knowledge is portable across models: it enables a lightweight, low-cost model to surpass a much larger zero-shot model by 19 percentage points, and transfers to other LLMs without retraining, at a one-time evolution cost under $50.

</details>


### [223] [Has This Checkpoint Been Abliterated? A Two-Signal Audit and Its Failure Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01854)
*Gabriel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需阈值、结合两种廉价内部信号（参考锚定激活拒绝差距和权重恢复能量）的检查点审计方法，在273个检查点的注册表中以AUROC 0.95区分57个公开去拒绝机制检查点与37个良性微调/合并/指令微调检查点，优于单一信号，但存在两种失败模式（欺骗参考和白盒攻击），该审计是有效的分诊工具而非防篡改方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台在部署前无法判断开放权重检查点是否被剥离了拒绝机制，因为运行时防护措施只评分生成内容而非工件本身，需要一种部署前的审计方法。

Method: 结合两种廉价内部信号：参考锚定的激活拒绝差距（提供拒绝特异性）和基础到候选权重差异的权重恢复能量（提供召回率），两者负相关且标签互补，通过z分数求和进行无阈值审计。

Result: 在包含Qwen、DeepSeek-distilled Qwen、Llama和Gemma的273个检查点注册表上，z分数求和以AUROC 0.95区分57个公开去拒绝检查点与37个良性检查点，显著优于单一信号（0.84和0.90）；Youden校准阈值在保留族上达到平衡准确率0.89（FPR 0.11），仅漏检4/57个。

Conclusion: 该审计是有效的分诊工具而非防篡改方案：它假定一个经过认证的参考点，其声明受评估注册表的限制。存在两种失败模式：欺骗参考（无需训练即可规避两个信号）和白盒攻击（训练检查点越过阈值但保持无防护且连贯）。

Abstract: Can a platform tell, before deployment, whether an open-weight checkpoint has had its refusal mechanism stripped? Runtime guards cannot: they score generations, not the artifact. We combine two cheap internal signals, a reference-anchored activation refusal-gap and a weight-recovery energy of the base-to-candidate weight difference, into a threshold-free checkpoint audit. The two are negatively correlated and label-complementary: the gap supplies refusal-specificity and the weight energy supplies recall. On a 273-checkpoint registry spanning Qwen, DeepSeek-distilled Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, their z-sum separates 57 public abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes, merges, and instruction-tunes at AUROC 0.95, significantly above either signal alone (0.84, 0.90), and a Youden-calibrated threshold transfers to held-out families at balanced accuracy 0.89 (FPR 0.11), missing only 4 of 57. We then map two failures, in order of severity: a spoofed reference evades both axes with no training (ΔW=0, \r{ho}=1 by construction), and a white-box owner trains a checkpoint past the threshold while it stays guard-unsafe and coherent. The audit is effective triage, not tamper-proofing: it presumes an attested reference, and its claims are bounded by the registry we evaluate it on.

</details>


### [224] [Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02121)
*William Hackett,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出首个黑盒护栏侦察方法，通过监测HTTP、词汇和时间信号检测AI系统中是否存在护栏，区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝。实验达到100%检测准确率，98%的F1区分分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒对抗测试中，研究人员难以区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝，而两者绕过技术不同，影响攻击技术选择和优化，亟需一种护栏侦察方法。

Method: 利用行为监测方法，分析HTTP响应、词汇特征和时间信号，在黑盒条件下检测目标AI系统中是否存在护栏，并识别护栏设计拦截的内容类别。

Result: 护栏检测准确率100%，良性/恶意交互行为分离统计显著（q<0.001），区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝的平均F1分数达98%。

Conclusion: 提出的黑盒护栏侦察方法高效可靠，可帮助攻击研究者准确识别防护机制，从而优化攻击策略，同时也可用于AI安全评估。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出策略耦合覆盖（Policy-Coupled Coverage）概念，作为不确定性信息与反事实决策之间的最优接口，并开发了PC-RACP两阶段过程，在保证有限样本覆盖保证的同时提升决策效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）虽能提供统计覆盖保证，但在反事实决策场景中，结果的实现取决于所采取的动作，不确定性不能独立于决策规则指定，存在决策最优性与统计有效性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 构建决策理论框架；提出策略耦合覆盖概念；采用最大最小规则作为分布模糊下的最小最大最优策略；推导总体最优预测集的显式形式；设计两阶段过程PC-RACP以近似最优集合并提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: PC-RACP在模拟和真实邮件营销实验中相比现有方法获得更高效用同时维持有效覆盖；忽略决策问题的反事实结构对有效性和效用均非最优。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性与反事实决策的最优无损接口，PC-RACP在实践中能兼顾统计有效性与决策最优性。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [226] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: X-VAE通过用预训练自编码器的经验均值和标准差构建数据自适应高斯先验，替代传统VAE的标准正态先验，并引入潜在缩放因子以平衡生成样本的多样性与保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE假设各向同性标准高斯先验，但这种假设对复杂数据集往往不成立，导致重构精度下降、样本质量降低以及潜在空间表达力受限。

Method: 提出X-VAE框架，使用预训练自编码器的潜在编码的经验均值和标准差参数化数据自适应高斯先验，并引入潜在缩放因子实现生成时对方差的可控调节。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验表明，X-VAE在保持重构质量的同时，产生的潜在表示更符合经验数据分布，提升了可控性和生成样本的真实性。

Conclusion: X-VAE有效解决了标准先验与真实数据分布不匹配的问题，特别适用于工程设计中需同时满足严格约束和设计探索的应用场景。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [227] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为LF-IBIS的无似然贝叶斯强化学习算法，通过结合近似贝叶斯计算和迭代批量重要性采样，在环境似然函数不可得或难以处理的情况下进行完整的贝叶斯推断，并在临床试验的自适应随机化模拟中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯强化学习方法通常需要显式的似然函数，但在许多现实场景中，环境动力学无法用显式或可处理的似然函数描述。这一局限性阻碍了BRL在更广泛实际问题中的应用。

Method: 将近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS）相结合，提出LF-IBIS算法。该算法在智能体与环境交互过程中在线更新信念，无需显式似然函数，即可获得环境参数和最优策略的近似后验分布。

Result: 在临床试验响应自适应随机化的模拟研究中进行了验证，该场景具有封闭形式后验可用于验证。额外实验针对后验无封闭形式的场景进行测试，展示了基于最优策略后验分布的在线策略更新能力。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS成功实现了在无似然环境下的完整贝叶斯推理，能够量化策略不确定性，为探索-利用权衡提供贝叶斯处理方案，拓展了贝叶斯强化学习在真实场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [228] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了$\sqrt{n}$-超额风险界和估计聚类中心的一致性，在MCAR机制下推导了$\sqrt{n}$-收敛速度和渐近正态性，并给出了估计聚类中心收敛到真实聚类中心的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-means聚类无法直接处理不完整数据，现有基于k-means的缺失数据聚类方法主要关注提升实际聚类精度，但大多缺乏渐近意义上的理论保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为缺失数据下的k-means聚类提供统计性质的严格理论分析。

Method: 首先建立了一般缺失机制下$\sqrt{n}$-超额风险界并证明估计聚类中心的一致性；在MCAR机制下进一步推导了$\sqrt{n}$-收敛速度和渐近正态性。分析了不完整数据估计的聚类中心收敛到完整数据真实聚类中心的条件，给出了关于缺失概率和真实聚类间分离度的充分条件。

Result: 在MCAR机制下，达到$\sqrt{n}$-收敛速度以及收敛到真实聚类中心均要求$k$个真实中心在每个维度上互不相同，这揭示了高维场景下应用的显著挑战。数值模拟结果支持了理论分析。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据下的k-means聚类提供了理论保障，同时指出了其在高维应用中的局限性。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [229] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的方法，结合Ghost变量、Shapley值和加性重要性度量，通过自相关和偏自相关函数来评估单变量时间序列预测模型中滞后的重要性，并引入了一种用模型单步预测替代缺失特征的新方法。实验验证了该方法在多种模拟和真实数据场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型缺乏通用的、模型无关的方法来量化和解释不同滞后项对预测结果的重要性，需要一种能够适用于不同模型（如ARMA和RNN）的滞后重要性评估框架。

Method: 采用模型无关的方法论，利用Ghost变量和Shapley值框架，结合加性重要性度量，定义自相关函数和偏自相关函数作为滞后重要性指标；同时提出用模型自身的单步预测替代基于联合方法的缺失特征。

Result: 在季节性ARMA和循环神经网络的模拟和真实数据实验中，所提出的相关性度量在几乎所有情况下都能成功展示预期的滞后结构。

Conclusion: 该结合框架特别适用于时间序列分析，为理解机器学习预测模型中滞后项的重要性提供了有效且通用的工具。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [230] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题替换为连续变分代理，在加权Sobolev空间中定义了λ-凸泛函族，证明该变分问题具有全局适定性、稳定性和几乎C^3正则性，最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，无需迭代优化，并建立了显式的泛化误差控制和有限宽度网络向连续最优解的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的优化原理在理论上仍难以解释，面临非凸景观和随机启发式方法的挑战，缺乏严谨的理论基础。

Method: 将离散训练问题转化为加权Sobolev空间上的连续变分代理，利用λ-凸泛函分析和椭圆正则性理论，通过求解单个线性系统获得最优参数密度。

Result: 证明了变分问题的全局适定性、稳定性和几乎C^3正则性；建立了1/α量级的显式泛化误差控制；证明了宽度为N的有限宽度网络以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了神经正切核（NTK）与特征学习机制之间的差距，为通过变分学理解过参数化提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [231] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种加法深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符（化学分支）和分子图拓扑（结构分支）分离训练，仅在预测阶段组合，实现了对水溶性预测中化学与结构贡献的可分解解释，并在两个数据集上取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水溶性预测模型将物理化学描述符和分子图信息融合为单一表示，无法区分预测是由全局化学性质驱动还是分子结构驱动，缺乏透明性和可解释性。

Method: 构建加法深度学习框架：化学分支用MLP编码物理化学描述符，结构分支用GNN编码分子图拓扑，两分支输出在预测阶段通过加法模型（可选乘法交互）组合；在大数据集AqSolDB上预训练，在较小数据集BigSolDB2上微调。

Result: 预训练+微调显著提升精度并降低运行间差异；化学分支与熟悉的物理化学描述符对齐，结构分支捕获图拓扑和官能团模式；在两个数据集上取得具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，使化学信息和结构信息的各自作用更加透明，为药物发现中的可解释AI提供了新思路。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [232] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 通过操纵提问顺序，发现LLM人格表达包含两个可分离的成分：聚合特征（大五人格得分）对框架稳健但随机化下降21%；几何特征（SPD流形）在框架错位时崩溃（下降42%），但在共享框架下显著恢复至84%，表明人格几何不是内在固有属性，而是依赖框架的协调模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM人格表达中的几何结构（within-instance相关矩阵）是内在固有属性还是依赖于提问框架（问题顺序）的产物。

Method: 使用IPIP-50问卷构建within-instance相关矩阵，在SPD流形上分析GPT-4o模拟美国华裔和华裔美国人 personas的几何结构，同时操纵问题顺序。

Result: 聚合特征（大五人格得分）在随机化下下降21%，对框架稳健；几何特征（SPD流形）在框架错位时下降42%，在共享框架下恢复至84%，优于聚合特征的76%。

Conclusion: LLM人格几何不是内在固有属性，而是依赖框架的协调模式，且编码了聚合特征无法捕捉的信息。需要建立双重本质框架——框架依赖的几何与框架稳健的聚合，挑战静态特质概念。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Beyond Degree: Rooted Motif Signatures for Latent Position Identifiability in Graphon Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01358)
*Roland Boniface Sogan,Tabea Rebafka*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出根植于子图模体签名（rooted motif signatures）作为图on的高阶节点级表示，证明在有限秩图on中，这些签名能区分度函数无法区分的潜在位置，并给出了统计估计的一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于度的图on可识别性假设度函数严格单调，这对于常数度或非单射度函数的图on（即使潜在位置具有不同的连接轮廓）失效。需要更高阶的节点表示来克服这一限制。

Method: 提出根植子图模体签名（包括三角形、路径、环等局部子图模式的密度），在有限秩图on上证明其可区分性，并从单张观测图中构造经验估计量，给出均匀集中界。

Result: 对于一般有限秩图on，合适的根植子图模体签名能确定潜在位置的连接轮廓。提供了经验签名的浓度界。模拟实验表明该方法在度表示无效的场景（如等度随机块模型）中仍能揭示潜在结构。

Conclusion: 根植子图模体签名是对度函数的有效推广，能在不假设度函数单调的条件下实现图on的节点区分，具有理论保证和实用价值。

Abstract: Graphon estimation requires structural assumptions to address its intrinsic non-identifiability. A standard approach is degree-based identifiability, where the degree function is assumed to be strictly monotonic. This assumption is rather restrictive and fails for graphons with constant or non-injective degree function, even when distinct latent positions have different connectivity profiles. In this paper, we introduce \emph{rooted motif signatures} as higher-order node-level representations for graphons. They extend the degree function by recording, at each latent position, the densities of rooted motifs such as triangles, cycles, paths, and other local subgraph patterns. We study the extent to which these signatures can distinguish latent positions beyond degree information. For generic finite-rank graphons, we prove that suitable rooted motif signatures determine the connectivity profiles of latent positions. We also explain why such a property cannot hold for arbitrary graphons without additional assumptions, since different latent positions may have identical rooted motif signatures. On the statistical side, we define empirical rooted motif signatures from a single observed graph and prove uniform concentration bounds for these estimators. Simulation experiments illustrate that rooted motif signatures can reveal latent structure in settings where degree-based representations are uninformative, including graphons with constant or non-injective degree functions and stochastic block models with equal block degrees.

</details>


### [234] [Transferring supremum-norm rates and weak convergence of covariance kernel estimators to functional principal components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01931)
*Hajo Holzmann,Kevin Wilk*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明$L_2$-扰动理论可将协方差核估计量的收敛速度（一致范数）和弱收敛性（连续函数空间）迁移至泛函主成分（FPCs）。在固定同步设计的离散观测误差模型下，获得了FPCs估计的一致范数最优收敛速度（含极小化下界）及渐近正态性。稀疏到稠密转变现象同样适用于FPCs；特征值估计在稀疏设计下存在离散化主导区。结果还适用于交叉协方差、长程协方差及导数过程协方差核的估计。数值实验使用Nyström方法计算FPCs和特征值，并以日温度曲线序列进行实证说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注均值函数和协方差核的估计性质，但将$L_2$-扰动理论系统应用于泛函主成分（FPCs）在一致范数下的收敛速度和弱收敛性尚不充分；同时，离散观测模型中FPCs估计在稀疏到稠密转变下的行为及其极小化最优性尚未被完全刻画。

Method: 利用$L_2$-扰动理论将协方差核估计量的收敛性质（一致范数收敛速度和弱收敛）迁移至对应的FPCs；在固定同步设计的离散观测误差模型中建立理论结果，并通过Nyström方法进行数值实现。

Result: 获得了FPCs估计在一致范数下的最优收敛速度（含极小化下界）及渐近正态性；发现稀疏到稠密转变同样适用于FPCs，且特征值估计在稀疏设计下存在离散化主导区；理论结果扩展到交叉协方差、长程协方差及导数过程协方差核的估计。

Conclusion: $L_2$-扰动理论是研究FPCs估计性质的有效框架，建立了从协方差核估计到FPCs估计的收敛性传递机制，揭示了稀疏设计下特征值估计的离散化主导现象，并提供了数值和实证支持。

Abstract: We show that $L_2$-perturbation theory can be used to transfer rates of convergence in the supremum norm as well as weak convergence in the space of continuous functions from covariance kernel estimators to the associated functional principle components (FPCs). As an application we obtain optimal rates of convergence in sup-norm, including minimax-lower bounds, as well as asymptotic normality for estimating the FPCs in a discrete observational model with errors under fixed, synchronous design. The sparse to dense transition which has previously been observed for mean function and covariance kernel estimators also applies to the FPCs. Surprisingly, eigenvalue estimation exhibits a discretization-dominated regime under sparse designs, too. Our results further apply to estimators of cross-covariance and long-run covariance kernels, as well as to covariance kernels of derivative processes. We also present results of numerical experiments in which we use the Nyström method to compute FPCs and eigenvalues, and give an empirical illustration to series of daily temperature curves.

</details>


### [235] [A note on "The volume of random simplices from elliptical distributions in high dimension"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02169)
*Shan Xizheng,Li Yanpeng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文放宽了Gusakova等人关于高维椭球框架下随机单形和对凸体对数体积的中心极限定理和稳定极限定理的技术条件，将原本要求总体矩阵接近单位矩阵倍数的限制条件推广到更合理的条件，覆盖了尖峰模型、依赖结构模型等多种场景，并特别应用于Toeplitz/AR(1)协方差结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gusakova等人（2023）在高维椭球框架下建立了随机单形和对凸体对数体积的极限定理，但其假设条件（Assumption (B)中(2.6)式）要求总体矩阵AA*在Frobenius范数下接近单位矩阵的倍数，这一条件过于严格，排除了尖峰模型和依赖结构模型等许多统计应用场景，因此需要放宽这一条件以扩大适用范围。

Method: 通过提出一个更一般化的条件来替代原假设中过于严格的(2.6)式，从而放松技术约束，使得新条件能够覆盖更多实际情形，并证明在该条件下原极限定理仍然成立。

Result: 得到了一个更合理的条件，该条件涵盖了多种场景，包括尖峰模型和依赖结构模型。特别是，该结果覆盖了Jiang和Pham（2025）研究的Toeplitz/AR(1)协方差结构，为高维依赖协方差模型提供了具体应用。

Conclusion: 本文成功推广了Gusakova等人的理论结果，使其适用于更广泛的统计模型，为高维随机单形和对凸体的体积极限理论提供了更通用的框架。

Abstract: Recent work by Gusakova et al. (Stochastic Process. Appl. 164 (2023) 357-382) has shown a central and a stable limit theorem for the logarithmic volume of random simplices and random convex bodies under an elliptical framework in the high dimensional regime, that is, if p and n tend to infinity in such a way that the ratio tends to γwithin (0,1). A technical condition (Equation (2.6) of Assumption (B) therein) requires that the population matrix AA* is close in Frobenius norm to a multiple of the identity matrix, which is rather restrictive and rules out various settings for statistical application, such as spiked models and dependent structure models. In this note we offer a general relaxation of this condition, which arrives at a reasonable condition and covers numerous scenarios, as well as consequences for the volume of general random simplices and random convex bodies. In particular, our results covers the Toeplitz/AR(1) covariance structures studied by Jiang and Pham (Ann. Stat. 53 (2025) 907-928), giving a concrete application of our theorem to high-dimensional dependent covariance models.

</details>


### [236] [Aggregation with Exponential Weights is Optimal in Expectation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02247)
*Mikael Møller Høgsgaard,Patrick Rebeschini,Tobias Wegel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了AEW估计器在模型选择聚合中期望风险的公开问题，证明在无需Bernstein型假设条件下，当温度T足够大时，AEW能够达到T log(M)/(n+1)的期望超额风险，并揭示了AEW存在尖锐的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自Lecué和Mendelson(2013)以来悬而未决的公开问题：带指数权重的聚合估计器(AEW)在平方损失、随机设计的模型选择聚合基本设定下，是否能在期望意义下实现极小极大最优速率。

Method: 通过理论分析，证明在不要求Bernstein型假设的条件下，当温度T满足(L^2/T)exp(B/T) ≤ μ/2时，AEW实现期望超额风险T log(M)/(n+1)；对平方损失，当预测和标签取值于[0,b]时，T ≥ 4b^2即可满足条件。

Result: 证明了AEW在足够大但恒定的温度下达到极小极大最优速率，并确认了Lecué和Mendelson猜测的尖锐相变现象：低于某常数温度时AEW期望次优，高于该阈值时达到最优。

Conclusion: 完整解决了AEW在模型选择聚合中的最优性理论问题，明确了温度参数的临界条件，为指数权重聚合方法的理论理解画上句号。

Abstract: The aggregation with exponential weights (AEW) estimator is not fully understood in the basic setting of model selection aggregation with squared loss. In particular, whether it is minimax-rate optimal in expectation for large enough fixed temperatures and under random design has been an open problem since its introduction, which was explicitly posed by Lecué and Mendelson (2013). In this paper, we settle this problem by showing that \emph{without} requiring a Bernstein-type assumption, the AEW indeed achieves the excess risk $T \log (M) / (n+1)$ in expectation, whenever the temperature $T$ satisfies $(L^2/T)\exp(B/T)\leq μ/2$. Here, the number of dictionary elements is $M$, the estimator has observed $n$ i.i.d. samples from any distribution, and the loss is assumed to be bounded by $B$, $L$-Lipschitz continuous and $μ$-strongly convex. For squared loss, we show that $T\geq 4 b^2$ suffices when the predictions and labels are $[0,b]$-valued. Because AEW is known to be suboptimal in expectation for temperatures below some constant, this shows that AEW has a sharp phase transition when the temperature is large enough but constant, as conjectured by Lecué and Mendelson.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），通过将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入神经网络，结合认知诊断模型的可解释性与深度学习灵活性，并设计含L2惩罚的损失函数及可解释对齐评估指标，实现兼具预测性能与结构一致性的认知诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有良好的结构可解释性但灵活性不足，而深度神经网络虽预测能力强但缺乏认知理论层面的可解释性，两者难以兼顾。M-QCDNet旨在弥合心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，确保潜在掌握特征具有认知理论一致性；2) 设计含L2惩罚项的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不对齐的技能进行惩罚，平衡预测性能与结构对齐；3) 开发可解释对齐评估指标，量化预测技能激活与题目层面技能之间的对应程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持预测性能的同时，实现了技能激活与Q矩阵结构的高度对齐，在可解释对齐指标上表现优于传统方法，能够早期发现学习困难并支持掌握导向的干预。

Conclusion: 通过将诊断效度嵌入模型设计，M-QCDNet成功融合了心理测量透明性与神经网络灵活性，为推动可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI提供了新范式。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [238] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Intra-Inter Riemannian流形注意力网络(IIRMANet)，通过在频域构建空间协方差矩阵、频率聚类聚合以及跨切片注意力机制，解决跨被试EEG压力检测中的个体差异和频域特异性问题，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs达到82.78%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了对认知状态解码至关重要的神经振荡信息；标准时间分词常破坏跨切片的时序连贯性，导致跨被试EEG压力检测中判别性模式难以捕捉。

Method: 1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道间几何和频域判别信息；2) 频率聚类聚合，选择信息性频谱成分，形成与EEG节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇；3) 跨切片注意力模块，自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态和全局时序上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上超越五个SOTA基线，最高达82.78%平衡准确率，模型仅1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs。

Conclusion: 提出的频域黎曼流形注意力机制有效提升了跨被试EEG压力检测性能，兼具高准确率与计算效率。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [239] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程示例系统（PBE）在面对恶意对抗样本时的鲁棒性问题，提出固定集最坏情况损坏形式化、有限池精确搜索和启发式搜索方法，以及版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御机制。实验表明：低裕度PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，随机噪声评估无法覆盖；VPA仅在干净语义保持分区投票裕度时有效，而这在现实任务中常失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE系统通常假设错误示例来自随机噪声，通过最小化期望/经验损失来处理。本文关注一种不同的失败模式：攻击者知晓合成器，故意选择最能破坏返回程序的示例进行污染。这种对抗性威胁被现有随机噪声评估所忽视。

Method: 1) 形式化有限PBE版本空间下的固定集最坏情况损坏；2) 针对字符串变换DSL，实现有限池内精确搜索和启发式损坏搜索；3) 提出版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御：将示例分为不相交组分别合成，然后通过语义签名投票聚合结果。

Result: 1) 一个精心编辑即可翻转所有8个spike任务，而200次随机控制仅分别成功10.3%、11.0%、16.7%；2) 生成裕度1的行在预算1下被翻转，VPA可恢复；3) 在公有SyGuS数据上投票裕度接近1，自适应攻击使VPA准确率降为零；4) Playgol在141个接受行上对随机控制有正配对引导差距；5) 20个裕度1任务的LLM测试呈现相同定性模式。

Conclusion: 核心主张是有意受限且部分消极的：低裕度PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，这是随机错误和噪声PBE评估无法捕捉的；语义分区聚合仅在干净语义保持分区投票裕度时才有帮助，而在现实任务中这一条件通常不成立。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [240] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于ECG识别，通过双流有向图建模心电周期内和周期间的空间与时间依赖关系，在中文ECG竞赛数据集上实现88.1%平均F1分数，尤其在稀有类别上表现优异（76.3%），优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医疗等专业领域存在可解释性不足的问题，传统端到端卷积神经网络难以融入领域知识，导致对ECG关键特征（如PRQST波）的利用不充分，尤其在稀有类别检测上表现不佳。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络：将PRQST关键点作为领域知识嵌入；构建双流有向图，其中空间有向图建模同一心电周期内关键点的位置关系，时间有向图建模相邻周期间的时序依赖关系。

Result: 在首届中国心电图智能竞赛九分类数据集上，总体平均F1分数为88.1%，稀有类别平均F1分数为76.3%，均超越最先进模型。领域知识的引入有效提升了检测性能，尤其是对稀有类别。

Conclusion: 融合领域知识的图卷积网络能有效提升ECG识别的可解释性和性能，尤其对稀有类别检测具有显著优势，验证了将专业领域知识引入深度学习模型的有效路径。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [241] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的性能，发现该算法在GloVe嵌入数据集上存在维度缩放交叉现象，在近线性查询缩放和低索引成本方面具有优势，尤其适用于高维或频繁重建场景，并对高效Transformer架构的成本分析有指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的近似最近邻（ANN）搜索方法在现代缩放分析中缺失，缺乏对其在大数据集和高维场景下性能的系统性表征。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统表征，在GloVe嵌入数据集上从数据集大小N和维度d两个维度进行实验分析，并与图、树、分区等其他ANN方法对比。

Result: 发现此前未报告的维度缩放交叉现象：多探针网格搜索维持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而其他方法吞吐量下降；该算法具有近线性查询缩放和对数线性索引成本。

Conclusion: 多探针网格算法在索引重建频繁或高维场景下具有竞争力；其N和d的缩放特性可用于指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [242] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IonSense-QKG，一个面向锂离子电池数据集的量子就绪元数据框架，通过丰富元数据并引入量子就绪评分（QRS），将数据集选择转化为数据管理问题，为混合量子-经典机器学习工作流提供可复用的数据集发现与筛选基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构和预处理复杂度等方面差异显著，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，而现有研究缺乏系统性的量子就绪评估方法。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，为公共电池数据集记录扩充量子相关元数据（包括任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围及NISQ可行性），并引入透明量子就绪评分（QRS）作为数据集排序启发式指标，支持基于查询的数据集发现。

Result: 实现了基于扩充元数据的查询式发现，可识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集；发布了包含元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具及SQL风格查询示例的可复用工件。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为数据中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础框架，但QRS仅作为数据集选择启发式指标，而非量子优势的证据。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [243] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的DNA甲基化分类方法，用于中枢神经系统肿瘤分类。在2801样本参考队列上达到96%平均准确率，在1104样本独立临床队列上达到86%类级准确率和93%家族级准确率，较现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA甲基化分析已成为中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的重要方法，但仍面临跨队列迁移性、方法学正确性和稳健多类评估等挑战，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出结合稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维，并使用多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）进行分类的机器学习方法，在参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 参考队列上3折交叉验证平均准确率96%；独立临床队列上91类级准确率86%，甲基化家族级准确率93%。相比现有最优方法的82%类级和88%家族级一致性，分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在更强的机器学习方法学基础上，一致性地优于现有最优方法，能实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，其临床改善直接影响癌症亚型分类和治疗选择。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [244] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书《从逼近到涌现》对现代深度学习理论进行了统一的、基于证明的梳理，从经典逼近、优化和泛化理论出发，延伸到过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、尺度定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等当代机制，为研究人员和研究生提供了该领域的系统化理论地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已经超越了任何单一数学解释的范畴，需要一部统一的理论著作来整合分散的研究成果，形成连贯的研究叙事，以理解现代深度学习理论的全貌。

Method: 采用统一的理论框架，将每项理论通过其控制的对象、使其成立的假设以及未能解释的现象三个维度进行审视，构建从经典基础到当代机制的理论演进路径。

Result: 形成了一部对现代深度学习理论进行严谨映射的专著，涵盖逼近、优化、泛化、过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、尺度定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等核心主题。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论当前的状态是强大但不完整的，其核心问题日益聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现出来。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [245] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NightVision攻击方法，仅通过当前最严格的API限制（仅返回单个token的log概率、无logit偏置功能），就能从黑盒LLM中恢复隐藏维度、深度和参数量等架构信息。该方法利用通用提示集技术进行频谱分析估计隐藏维度，结合TTFT测量估计深度和参数量。在32个开源LLM上验证，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%），30亿参数以上模型深度和参数量误差53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商为保护架构细节，已将API限制为仅返回每个解码token的单个logit，并移除logit偏置功能。然而，即使在这种严格限制下，是否仍能恢复LLM的架构参数？

Method: 提出NightVision攻击：1) 通用提示集（common set prompting）技术，让多个提示对同一组输出token暴露log概率；2) 对这些结果进行频谱分析以推断隐藏维度；3) 结合端到端首个token时间（TTFT）测量和已估计的隐藏维度，进一步估计深度和参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上评估，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%），30亿参数以上模型深度和参数量误差53%。通过大量消融实验展示了精度如何随token预算和模型属性变化。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，存在严重的信息泄露风险。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [246] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态AI系统，利用铁路道口的图像和官方事故报告等结构化数据，进行安全评估和评分，并取得了与专家评估和FRA标准相一致的结果。该系统使用路由微调的紧凑型VLM管道，在HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK分类上F1分数达0.757，FRA安全评分预测RMSE为0.078，相关性为0.492。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否利用视觉线索稳健估计铁路道口的安全性，以及引入事故报告等结构化数据是否能提升评估能力，最终构建与专家意见和联邦铁路管理局安全评分一致的AI系统。

Method: 提出一个概念验证管道，包含从数据准备到不同学习范式的多个环节，核心采用路由微调的紧凑型视觉语言模型(VLM)管道，融合多模态数据（图像+事故报告）进行安全评估。

Result: 高风险/低风险分类宏F1分数0.757；FRA安全评分预测RMSE为0.078，相关性0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态AI系统能够有效评估铁路道口安全性，输出与专家和FRA标准对齐的评分，验证了融合视觉与结构化数据提升安全评估能力的可行性。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [247] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer在处理电子健康记录数据时对数值的离散、连续和混合编码策略，发现混合token方法在精度与鲁棒性间取得最佳平衡，最优分箱数遵循与数据集大小相关的经验幂律，且模型倾向于"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer在处理电子健康记录数据中数值型数据的编码方式，解决离散、连续和混合编码策略在数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间的权衡问题。

Method: 在真实EHR数据嵌入的合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务中，系统比较了离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略，并研究了分箱数量与数据集大小之间的幂律关系。

Result: 1) 显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上表现最佳但有架构限制；2) 混合token方法（保留数值但先分箱再投影）更鲁棒且适用性广；3) 最优分箱数遵循经验幂律；4) 模型呈现"足够好"的计算而非精确算术；5) 临床获益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 在实际应用中，鲁棒性和可部署性往往比最大数值精度更重要，混合token方法可作为实用的默认选择。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [248] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG
